A saturated, stage-like 3D block of the Upper West Side under a red-orange Technicolor sunset: red brick, zigzag fire escapes, rooftop water towers, posable dancers. It restages the aerial opening, the Jets' snaps and the poster's leap against the red sky.红橙特艺七彩晚霞下的上西区 3D 街区，饱和、舞台化：红砖、之字形消防梯、屋顶水塔、能摆姿势的舞者人偶。重演电影的俯拍开场、Jets 打响指和海报上那一跃。

Style prompt风格提示词

Technicolor 3D stage set, like a 1960s studio musical. Simplified geometry, dense detail on every built surface (openings with sills, ledges, railings, ladders, strung lines); textured brick, plaster or rock, never plain. Theatrical light: a low warm sun rakes surfaces with long shadows; cool fill a quarter of the key. Painted sky of red-orange #E0481E, gold-edged cloud bands, violet only at the top edge; warm haze in the distance. The one hero subject in saturated orange #D97757, at least 1/6 of frame height, set against dark brick or given a black rim so it never melts into the sky. Puppet-like mannequin figures. Warm, saturated grade, fine grain (±3%). Signature shot: a low angle against the orange sky; then one continuous descent into the set and a ground-level push. Avoid: photoreal grime, violet-pink skies, plain walls, poses that pop.

Technicolor 3D 舞台布景，像 60 年代棚拍歌舞片。几何简化，每个面都细节密集（窗洞窗台、檐口、栏杆、梯子、绳线）；表面是有质感的砖、灰泥或岩石，不能素面。剧场布光：低角度暖阳斜扫表面拉出长影；冷补光约为主光四分之一。手绘天空以橙红 #E0481E 为主，云带镶金边，只在最上沿带一点紫；远处融进暖雾。唯一的主角用饱和橙 #D97757，至少占画面高六分之一，背后衬深色砖墙或加一圈黑边，不能化进天空。人物是木偶式假人。暖而饱和的调色，细颗粒（±3%）。招牌镜头：衬着橙色天空的低角度仰拍；其次一个连续镜头从高处降进布景、再贴地推进。避免：写实脏旧、紫粉天空、素面墙、动作跳变。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. aerial1.3s

    Straight-down aerial over the West Side grid (rooftops, water towers, the playground court), descending and tilting up into the street.

    正上方俯拍西区街区网格（屋顶、水塔、操场球场），一路下降、抬头落进街道。

  2. street1.7s

    Low at the court's edge, the Jets' wedge snapping fingers behind Clawd by the graffiti wall; one continuous push swings round and in to a close-up of Clawd snapping on the drum hits.

    球场边低机位，Jets 帮在涂鸦墙前排成楔形打响指，Clawd 在前；一条连续的推镜绕过去推到 Clawd 近景，它的响指卡在鼓点上。

  3. leap1.2s

    Low angle under the fire escape: Clawd crouches and leaps in one arc against the red sky beside a dancer's jeté, the camera tilting up with the arc.

    消防梯下仰拍：Clawd 下蹲、起跳，一条抛物线压在红天上，旁边是舞者的大跳；镜头随弧线抬起。

  4. escape0.8s

    Close on the fire-escape landing: Clawd strikes a match, the camera pushes in until the flame sits at frame centre, then it is blown out.

    消防梯平台近景：Clawd 划亮火柴，镜头推近直到火苗停在画面正中，随后被吹灭。

Clawd joins the Jets with no costume; the set does the identifying. Snaps land on the drum hits, and the leap is one clean arc from crouch to landing squash. The opening aerial answers The Apartment's desk grid, and the struck match sits at frame centre exactly where Lawrence of Arabia's sun rises after the cut.Clawd 混进 Jets 帮，没有戏服，靠场景认片。响指卡在鼓点上；腾跃是一条干净的抛物线，从下蹲到落地缓冲一气呵成。开场俯拍呼应上一部的桌阵网格；结尾划亮的火柴在画面正中，正是下一部日出的位置，做匹配剪辑。

No.351962

Lawrence of Arabia阿拉伯的劳伦斯

2DTerpning Epic Poster GouacheTerpning 史诗海报水粉

  • Gouache and oil poster painting水粉加油彩海报画
  • Howard Terpning's 1962 posterHoward Terpning 1962 海报
  • Freddie Young's 70mm desert lightFreddie Young 70mm 沙漠光

Painterly epic-poster gouache with visible directional strokes and a hard warm–cool split: amber sand against cobalt sky. Lawrence of Arabia's original poster was painted by Howard Terpning in exactly this manner.看得见笔触方向的史诗海报水粉，冷暖对比强烈：琥珀色沙漠对钴蓝天空。《阿拉伯的劳伦斯》原海报就是 Howard Terpning 用这种画法画的。

Style prompt风格提示词

1960s hand-painted epic film poster, gouache with oil highlights. Draw forms solidly, then paint: an opaque mid-tone base, directional strokes following each form (1/80–1/50 of frame width on large areas, smaller on faces and hands), soft-blended edges; model every form in three values; dry-brush only on dust, cloth and distant figures; sharp warm-cream highlights last on lit edges. Warm–cool contrast: cobalt sky #1F4D97 to pale blue #A9C3D6, warming to cream near a low sun; ground #EEC27A (any terrain); ochre-red #B56A33 for skin, earth and costume; violet #6C6394 for distant shadows. The hero centred and largest, a diagonal gesture, cloth streaming; smaller, looser figures may recede behind. Light board grain (±3%), warm vignette, haze. Motion: bounce, slow push to a low close-up, a whip pan out. Avoid: scratchy uniform hatching, flat digital fills, cold-only colour.

60 年代手绘史诗电影海报，水粉打底、油彩提亮。先把形体画扎实再上色：铺不透明中间调底色，再用顺形体走的笔触（大面宽约画面宽的 1/80 到 1/50，脸和手用小笔），边缘柔和衔接；每个形体分亮、中、暗三级；干笔只用在尘土、布料和远处人物上；最后在受光边缘压锐利的暖奶油色高光。冷暖对比：钴蓝天空 #1F4D97 过渡到浅蓝 #A9C3D6，低垂的太阳周围转暖；地面 #EEC27A（任何地形），皮肤、泥土和衣服用赭红 #B56A33，远处阴影用紫 #6C6394。主角居中最大，一个斜向动作，布料飘起；身后可以有更小更松的人物退远。轻画板颗粒、暖暗角、雾气。运动：起伏、缓推到仰角近景、甩镜出画。避免：又碎又匀的刮擦线、平涂数码色、只有冷色。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. sun0.7s

    Extreme wide of an empty desert at dawn: a huge sun rising through heat haze at frame centre, matched to the blown-out match; slow push in.

    空旷沙漠的黎明大远景：一轮巨大的太阳在画面正中透过热浪升起，和上一部吹灭的火柴做匹配剪辑；缓慢推近。

  2. charge1.0s

    Poster framing from the camera car: Clawd (Lawrence) gallops in on a camel among charging riders, scimitar low, then raised on the beat with a glint; jolting shake, gentle push.

    跟拍车上的海报构图：Clawd（劳伦斯）骑骆驼混在冲锋的骑兵里奔来，弯刀先放低，卡拍举起、刀尖一闪；颠簸晃动，缓慢推近。

  3. whip0.8s

    Low angle pushing up to the raised blade, then a whip pan right, out of the frame and into the next film.

    低角度推向高举的弯刀，然后向右甩镜头出画，接下一部。

Clawd plays Lawrence: white robes, headcloth and gold agal, a raised scimitar, riding a camel. It opens on the most famous match cut in cinema, West Side Story's match flame becoming the rising sun. The charge plays at a constant 1.5×, the blade goes up on a beat with a glint, and a whip pan hands over to Tom Jones.Clawd 扮劳伦斯：白袍、白头巾、金色头箍，骑骆驼举弯刀。开场就是影史最有名的匹配剪辑：上一部的火柴火苗变成沙漠日出。整段冲锋恒定 1.5 倍速，弯刀卡拍举起、刀尖一闪，最后甩镜头出画，接《汤姆·琼斯》。

References: 对标：David Lean, 1962 · Howard Terpning (1962 poster) · Freddie Young (70mm cinematography)

No.361963

Tom Jones汤姆·琼斯

2DHand-Coloured Georgian Satirical Print手工上色讽刺铜版画

  • Hogarth copperplate engraving霍加斯铜版线刻
  • Rowlandson/Gillray hand colouring罗兰森/吉尔雷式手工上色
  • Satirical speech balloon讽刺画对白气泡

A Georgian satirical print: Hogarth's swelling burin lines and directional hatching under bright Rowlandson-style hand colour brushed slightly off the line, plus a speech balloon. The film itself imitates Hogarth's 18th-century England.乔治王时代的讽刺版画：霍加斯式粗细变化的刻线和有方向的排线，上面刷罗兰森式鲜艳的手工淡彩，稍稍出线，再加一个对白气泡。电影本身就在模仿霍加斯笔下的 18 世纪英国。

Style prompt风格提示词

Georgian satirical print: a copperplate engraving, hand-coloured. Brown-black line only: swelling burin outlines about 1/400 of frame width, heavier on the shadow side; hatching follows each form and covers every surface (spacing about 1/250 of frame width), cross-hatched in the darks, ruled on flat areas. Colour covers nearly the whole picture, clear but strong: washes spilling a few pixels off the line in claret #B3262E, brass #D8A93A, flesh #F0C4A4, slate blue #6D8AA6, warm ochre and umber on large surfaces. Sepia laid paper, foxing, plate mark, engraved border. Stage it like William Hogarth: big-nosed caricatures, bystanders or comic props and animals around the subject; one slanted handwritten speech balloon in the user's language. Camera: hold the full plate 2 s, whip in, glide to another detail, close-up. Avoid: even mechanical hatching, blank paper, colour exactly inside lines, muddy colour.

乔治王时代讽刺版画：先刻铜版，印完再手工上色。墨只有棕黑色的线：雕刀轮廓约画面宽的 1/400，起伏鼓胀，背光一侧更粗；排线顺形体走、铺满每个面（线距约画面宽的 1/250），只在暗部交叉，平面用直尺排线。颜色几乎铺满整幅，清透但饱满：水色溢出墨线几像素，用酒红 #B3262E、黄铜 #D8A93A、肉色 #F0C4A4、石板蓝 #6D8AA6，大面用暖赭和棕褐。浅棕褐帘纹纸，淡霉点，版框印痕，刻线边框。按 William Hogarth 的方式布置：大鼻子的漫画人物，主体身边是旁观者或搞笑的道具、动物；一个手写斜体对话气泡，用用户的语言。镜头：全幅停两秒，急推，横滑到另一处细节，再给近景。避免：机械均匀的排线、大片空白纸、颜色严丝合缝填在线里、颜色发脏。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. two1.3s

    Whip in from the left onto the two-shot across the laden table, Clawd (Tom) tearing into a drumstick; the camera glides across the table onto Mrs Waters as her speech balloon appears ('Another Leg, Mr Jones?') and she winks.

    从左边甩进隔着满桌菜的双人镜头，Clawd（汤姆）大口啃鸡腿；镜头横滑过桌面落到沃特斯夫人，她冒出对白气泡（Another Leg, Mr Jones?）并眨了一下眼。

  2. clawd1.2s

    Close on Clawd: heart eyes pop, a swoon and a lovesick nibble, then it doffs the tricorn and claps it over the lens to black; slow push in.

    Clawd 近景：眼睛变成心形，陶醉地一晃，又啃一小口，然后摘下三角帽扣向镜头，画面变黑；缓慢推近。

Clawd plays Tom Jones: a gold-trimmed tricorn, a lipstick kiss on its cheek, a drumstick in hand. One glide replaces shot/reverse, carrying the two-shot across the feast onto Mrs Waters' wink and balloon. Then the close: heart eyes, a swoon, and the hat comes at the lens, so the next film opens from black.Clawd 扮汤姆·琼斯：金边三角帽、脸上一个口红唇印、手拿鸡腿。不用正反打，用一次横滑：从隔桌的双人镜头滑到沃特斯夫人眨眼、冒出对白气泡。再切近景：心形眼、陶醉一晃，摘下三角帽扣向镜头，画面全黑，下一部从黑里开场。

References: 对标：Tony Richardson, 1963 · William Hogarth, A Rake's Progress / Marriage A-la-Mode · Thomas Rowlandson · James Gillray

My Fair Lady窈窕淑女

2DBob Peak Fashion IllustrationBob Peak 时装插画

  • Fast ink line快速墨线
  • Pink and magenta watercolour粉色与品红水彩
  • Bob Peak's 1964 posterBob Peak 1964 海报
  • Cecil Beaton's Ascot costumesCecil Beaton 阿斯科特服装

Fast, dry-edged ink line with pink and magenta watercolour washes, splatter and edges fading to white paper. The original 1964 poster was Bob Peak's ink and pink; the scene is Cecil Beaton's black-and-white Ascot.快速、带飞白的墨线，配粉色和品红水彩晕染、甩点，四周化进白纸。1964 年原海报就是 Bob Peak 的墨线加粉彩；场景是 Cecil Beaton 设计的黑白阿斯科特。

Style prompt风格提示词

1960s fashion illustration after Bob Peak. Fast ink line, thicker in turns, dry-brush tail, a flick off; 1/600–1/250 of frame width, thinning on tight curves. Solid black for dark clothing, hats and stripes; faces bare paper #FBF7F1. Loose, soft-edged watercolour pools, darker at drying edges: pink #EE84B2 as the main field, magenta #CE3076 for hot spots, rose #F4B2CC, a touch of plum #80285C; warm grey for secondary figures and shading; directional splatter. Settings are a few lines in these washes, not literal colour. The frieze is essential: slender profile figures 9–10 heads tall repeated across the frame (the hero plus grey-wash figures or echoes), black-and-white pattern over pink; washes cover 60–80% of the frame. Camera: slow drift along the figures; graphic-mask transitions. Avoid: even-width outlines, flat digital fills, polygon-edged washes, a lone figure on an empty page.

仿 Bob Peak 的 60 年代时装插画。墨线很快：转弯处变粗、直线处变细，后段飞白，收笔一甩；粗细在画面宽的 1/600 到 1/250 之间，小弯处变细。深色衣服、帽子和条纹用实心墨块；脸留白纸 #FBF7F1。水彩是松散柔边的色池，干后边缘积色更深：粉 #EE84B2 做主色，品红 #CE3076 做热点，加玫瑰粉 #F4B2CC 和一点梅紫 #80285C；次要人物和阴影用暖灰；带方向的甩点。场景只用几根线和这几种水彩带过。横排人物是核心：修长侧身，九到十个头高，横向重复（主角加暖灰人物或重影），黑白图案压在粉色上；水彩盖住画面六到八成。镜头：沿人物慢横移；转场借图形遮罩。避免：轮廓粗细一样、平涂数码色、多边形硬边色块、空白页上孤零零一个人。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. reveal0.8s

    从黑里，阿斯科特大帽的帽檐抬起，露出 Clawd（伊莉莎）的近景；它把目光转向镜头，微微屈膝行礼，画面轻轻往后收。

Clawd plays Eliza: an enormous white hat with striped ribbon, roses and ostrich plumes, a lace collar and hem. No.381965

The Sound of Music音乐之声

3DStorybook 3D Alpine Aerial绘本感 3D 阿尔卑斯航拍

  • Procedural 3D terrain (three.js)程序生成 3D 地形（three.js）
  • Todd-AO widescreen colour托德宽银幕色彩
  • Ted McCord's helicopter openingTed McCord 直升机开场
  • Miyazaki meadows宫崎骏的草原

Storybook 3D over procedural Alps: saturated emerald, cobalt and snow white, sculpted ridges, cotton-ball cumulus and gusts running through the grass. It restages the helicopter opening that ends on Maria spinning on the hilltop.绘本感的 3D 程序阿尔卑斯：饱和的翠绿、钴蓝、雪白，雕塑感的山脊，棉花团积云，风吹过草地的草浪。重演开场的直升机航拍，最后落到山顶转圈的玛丽亚。

Style prompt风格提示词

Storybook 3D landscape in saturated 1960s widescreen colour: smooth sculpted forms, not low-poly or photoreal. Default light: bright sun high, a little behind the camera: emerald grass #3FA33A, cobalt sky #2F6FD0, clean white snow and clouds. Rounded ridges, snow lines following slope, grey-ochre rock bands, cloud shadows. Haze only on the farthest ridges, about 25% lilac. Cumulus are lit white cotton puffs. Knee-high grass and wildflowers fill the foreground, bending in gusts. A lower sun lengthens shadows and warms only cloud edges; other settings keep these colours. Camera: one continuous move: 0–2 s high flight, 2–4 s descent, then a low orbit through the grass around the subject (about 20° per second, subject about 1/4 of frame height). References: cinematographer Ted McCord (*The Sound of Music*, 1965); Miyazaki's meadows. Avoid: violet wash, needle peaks, grey photoreal rock, static grass.

绘本感的 3D 风景，60 年代宽银幕的饱和色，形体圆润光滑有雕塑感，不写实也不做低多边形。默认光：太阳高挂、略在镜头身后，大地满色：翠绿草地 #3FA33A、钴蓝天空 #2F6FD0、干净的白雪白云。风化山脊圆润，雪线顺坡，岩层暖灰赭，有云影。空气透视只加在最远的山脊，约两成半淡紫。积云是受光的白色棉花团。前景是齐膝密草和野花，随阵风弯伏。太阳低时只拉长影子、让云边变暖；其他场景也用这些颜色。一个连续镜头：前两秒高空飞越，再两秒降到草坡，之后贴草低机位环绕主体（每秒约 20°，主体约占画面高四分之一）。对标摄影师 Ted McCord（《音乐之声》，1965）和宫崎骏的草地。避免：泛紫、针尖山峰、写实灰岩、不动的草。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. far0.7s

    Inside the two binocular circles carried over from My Fair Lady, which open to full frame: a high aerial gliding over snow peaks through cotton-ball clouds.

    画面从上一部的两个望远镜圆圈里打开到全屏：高空航拍，穿过棉花团积云掠过雪峰。

  2. approach0.8s

    Descent onto the hilltop meadow: Clawd small at frame centre, already turning with carpet bag and guitar case flung out, forest and field patchwork beyond; the camera drops toward it.

    下降逼近山顶草坡：Clawd 小小地在画面正中，已经甩着毯包和琴盒转起来，远处是森林和田地拼块；镜头朝它降下去。

  3. meadow1.0s

    Low in the grass, the camera orbits as Clawd twirls a full turn, bag and case swinging, snow peaks and clouds behind, then throws its arms up.

    贴着草的低机位，镜头环绕转了一整圈的 Clawd，毯包和琴盒甩开，身后是雪峰和积云；最后它双臂高举。

Clawd plays Maria: an edelweiss and a small headscarf knot, with the poster's striped carpet bag in one hand and a guitar case in the other. The spin is one continuous curve across all three shots, so every cut lands mid-turn, and the arms go up on the last beat.Clawd 扮玛丽亚：头顶一朵雪绒花和一个小头巾结，照海报一手拎条纹毯包、一手拎吉他琴盒。转圈是一条贯穿三个镜头的连续曲线，每一刀都切在转到一半的时候；最后一拍双臂高举。

References: 对标：Robert Wise, 1965 · Ted McCord (cinematography) · Howard Terpning (1965 poster) · Hayao Miyazaki (meadows)

No.391966

A Man for All Seasons日月精忠

2DHolbein Tudor Portrait in Oils霍尔拜因都铎肖像油画

  • Old Master oil glazing古典油画罩染
  • Hans Holbein, Sir Thomas More (1527)霍尔拜因《托马斯·莫尔爵士像》（1527）
  • Candlelit Tudor interior烛光都铎室内

An Old Master oil portrait: smooth glazed modelling, fur painted hair by hair, velvet highlights, a gold chain link by link, varnish and craquelure. Holbein painted the real Thomas More in 1527, and this is his composition.古典大师油画肖像：平滑罩染塑形、一根根画出的毛领、天鹅绒高光、逐环刻画的金链，再加旧光油和龟裂纹。霍尔拜因 1527 年亲手画过托马斯·莫尔，这里用的就是他的构图。

Style prompt风格提示词

Oil portrait after Hans Holbein the Younger. Paint in layers: a toned underlayer, directional strokes following light and shadow, soft glazes, fine detail last; smooth finish, brushwork faint in background and fabric. Render each material: fur hair by hair, velvet folds with sharp light ridges, metal with two highlights per piece, wool and leather with soft nap. Any subject becomes a formal half-length sitter in its own clothes; its setting shows through a window or in an object it holds. Palette: deep green curtain #1F4A26, black #141113, velvet red #6E0C15, gold #B0801E, fur brown #3E2818; warm, fully modelled skin. Soft light from upper left. Signature shot: the portrait against the curtain; then material close-ups, a slow pull back into a dark room lit by warm pools. Warm varnish, light craquelure. Avoid: flat fills, hard digital edges, even light, unmodelled faces.

仿小汉斯·霍尔拜因的北方文艺复兴油画肖像。一层层画：带色底、顺明暗的笔触、柔和罩染、最后细笔；成品光滑，背景和布料隐约见笔触。每种材质各有画法：毛皮一根根画，天鹅绒褶子亮脊锐利，金属每件两个高光点，呢料和皮革画出柔和绒面。任何主体都画成穿自己衣服的正式半身像；它的场景从窗口露出，或由手里一件东西交代。色板：深绿帘幕 #1F4A26，黑 #141113、天鹅绒红 #6E0C15、金 #B0801E、毛皮棕 #3E2818；皮肤暖色、体积完整。柔光从左上来。招牌镜头：衬着绿帘的半身像；其次是材质特写，再慢慢拉远到暖色光池照亮的暗房间。收尾叠暖色光油和轻龟裂。避免：平涂、数码硬边、均匀亮光、没有体积的脸。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. close0.9s

    Extreme close on the face and the SS collar with its Tudor rose pendant: Clawd (More) looks aside, turns its eyes to the lens and blinks; slow push in.

    脸和 SS 金链、都铎玫瑰吊坠的大特写：Clawd（莫尔）先看向一侧，再把目光转向镜头，眨一下眼；缓慢推近。

  2. pull1.6s

    The Holbein portrait composition (Tudor cap, fur collar, red velvet sleeves, green damask, letter in hand): a grave nod, eyes drop to the letter, then a slow pull-back reveals the candlelit Tudor room; the candle gutters and the frame goes dark.

    霍尔拜因肖像构图（都铎扁帽、毛领、红天鹅绒袖子、绿色锦缎帘幕、手里的信）：庄重地点一下头，目光落到信上，然后缓慢拉远，露出烛光下的都铎房间；烛火将熄，画面暗下去。

Clawd plays Thomas More: a Tudor cap, black gown with fur collar, red velvet sleeves, the SS chain and a sealed letter. After the spinning meadow, stillness: one cut from close-up to portrait, then a single long pull-back, and the guttering candle carries the dark into the next film's night.Clawd 扮托马斯·莫尔：都铎扁帽、黑袍毛领、红天鹅绒袖子、SS 金链，手拿封了红蜡的信。接在转圈的草坡之后，这里是静：从大特写切到肖像只有一刀，之后一路拉远；蜡烛将熄，暗下来的画面接上下一部的夜。

References: 对标：Fred Zinnemann, 1966 · Hans Holbein the Younger, Sir Thomas More (1527) · Holbein, The Ambassadors (1533) · Ted Moore (cinematography)

No.401967

In the Heat of the Night炎热的夜晚

2DThree-Ink Risograph Night三色 Risograph 夜景

  • Risograph overprintRisograph 叠印
  • 1967 poster orange-red1967 海报橙红
  • Haskell Wexler night exteriorsHaskell Wexler 夜景
  • Silhouette staging剪影式调度

A three-ink Risograph print in deep blue, poster orange-red and fluorescent pink, with halftone, grain and misregistration; light is printed as less ink. It echoes the 1967 poster's orange heat field and black silhouettes.三色 Risograph 印刷：深蓝、海报橙红、荧光粉，带网点、颗粒和套色错位；光是用「少上墨」印出来的。呼应 1967 年海报的橙红热浪底和黑色剪影。

Style prompt风格提示词

Three-ink Risograph: orange-red #EC5032 as the dominant field (about 60%, with drip streaks), deep blue #162C76 for silhouettes and shadows, fluorescent pink #FF48B0 only for rims and halos, on warm paper #F7F2E7. Each ink is halftoned at its own angle, dots 1/300 of frame width, plates misregistered 2–4 px; grain, pinholes, slight boil. Knock orange out under blue silhouettes so they print pure blue; only the darkest accents overprint to near-black. Light is the absence of ink: beams, lamps and lit ground show bare paper, ringed in pink. Figures: blue silhouettes, pink rims; skin is 30–60% orange with a blue shadow step. At cooler times keep the red field; blue rises only into the top of the sky. Camera: handheld behind someone carrying a light, a reverse close-up, a push. Avoid: blue-dominated frames, smooth gradients, a fourth ink, clean registration.

三色专色 Risograph 印刷：橙红 #EC5032 是主场（约占画面六成，带竖向流淌痕），深蓝 #162C76 给剪影和阴影，荧光粉 #FF48B0 只给轮廓光和光晕，印在暖白纸 #F7F2E7 上。每色是各自网角的半调，点径约画面宽的 1/300，版间错位二到四像素；有墨粒、针孔，逐帧轻微抖动。蓝剪影底下挖掉橙色，印成纯蓝；只有最暗处两色叠成近黑。光就是没有墨：光束、灯和被照亮的地面露出纸白，外圈一道粉。人物是带粉色边缘光的蓝剪影，肤色用三到六成橙色网点加一级蓝暗部。时间偏冷时仍保留红场，只让蓝色升到天空顶部。镜头：手持跟在提着光源的人身后，一个反打近景，一次缓推。避免：蓝色占满画面、平滑渐变、第四种墨、套印太准。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. pov0.8s

    Handheld flashlight POV out of the dark: a deputy's beam sweeps the depot platform and finds Clawd (Tibbs) by its suitcase; it looks up, eyes wide, and the other beams snap on.

    黑暗中的手持手电主观镜头：副警长的光束扫过车站站台，找到手提箱旁的 Clawd（蒂布斯）；它抬头睁大眼，其他几束手电随即亮起。

  2. sheriff0.5s

    Reverse close on Chief Gillespie: peaked cap, aviator shades, chewing gum, then stopping; slow push in.

    反打警长吉莱斯皮近景：大檐帽、飞行员墨镜，嚼着口香糖，然后停住；缓慢推近。

  3. standoff1.2s

    The stand-off, medium wide: Clawd between the deputies and the chief in crossing flashlight beams, the blue cruiser and the Sparta water tower behind; it straightens up and squares its shoulders as the camera pushes in.

    对峙中全景：Clawd 站在副警长和警长之间，几束手电光交叉打在它身上，身后是蓝色警车和斯巴达镇水塔；它挺直身子、端平肩膀，镜头推近。

Clawd plays Virgil Tibbs: white collar points, a narrow dark tie, shirt cuffs, a suitcase at its side. The night carries over from the previous film's snuffed candle. Three cuts build the confrontation: the flashlight finds it, Gillespie stops chewing, and Clawd draws itself up for "They call me MISTER Tibbs!"Clawd 扮维吉尔·蒂布斯：白衬衫领尖、深色窄领带、袖口，脚边一只手提箱。上一部熄灭的烛光把夜带进来。三个镜头堆出对峙：手电找到它、警长停下嚼口香糖、它挺直腰板，像在说「They call me MISTER Tibbs!」

References: 对标：Norman Jewison, 1967 · Haskell Wexler (cinematography) · 1967 original poster · Tom Haugomat

No.411968

Oliver!雾都孤儿

2.5DVictorian Toy Theatre维多利亚玩具纸剧场

  • Pollock's toy theatrePollock's 玩具剧场
  • Hand-coloured lithograph sheets手工上色石印纸偶
  • Cut card with footlight shadows剪卡与脚灯投影
  • Cruikshank caricatureCruikshank 漫画人物

A Victorian toy theatre: hand-coloured lithographed card figures with a white scissor margin, layered flats on wire slides, a gilt proscenium and flickering candle footlights throwing shadows up the scenery. Oliver! is a musical, staged here on the Victorian home stage.维多利亚玩具纸剧场：手工上色的石印卡纸人物留着剪刀白边，景片分层插在滑杆上，镀金台口下的烛光脚灯把影子投上景片。《雾都孤儿》是歌舞片，正好搬上维多利亚家庭小舞台。

Style prompt风格提示词

Victorian toy paper theatre (Pollock's), all printed card: black lithographic outlines about 1/400 of frame width, sparse crayon hatching on one side of each piece; muted antique hand colouring: deep red #C8262C and dull gold #E0AB36 on proscenium and curtains (never bright yellow), blue #2F5A9A for costumes; scenery, sky, water and skin in greyed browns, greys and cream card #F4ECD6. Every cut-out keeps a white scissor margin (1/200 of frame width). Flats are parallel 2.5D layers in parallax, flat within each plane. Candle footlights throw each cut-out's shadow onto the scenery behind, about 1.5× enlarged, soft, flickering. Gilt proscenium, red drapes and painted audience boxes frame a stage opening 80–90% of frame width. Camera: slow push, lateral tracking, shaky close-ups; a final pull-back shows the dark room. Avoid: bright vector colour, true 3D depth, soft studio light, figures without margins.

维多利亚玩具纸剧场（Pollock's 式）。一切都是印出来的卡纸：黑色石印轮廓约画面宽的 1/400，每张剪纸一侧有稀疏蜡笔排线；手工上色是沉稳旧色：深红 #C8262C 和暗金 #E0AB36 用在台口和帷幔（金色不能是亮黄），蓝 #2F5A9A 给服装；布景、天空、水和肤色用发灰的棕、灰和奶油卡纸 #F4ECD6。每个人物和景片留一圈剪刀白边（约画面宽的 1/200）。景片是平行的 2.5D 层，层间视差，层内无透视。烛光脚灯把剪纸影子放大约一倍半投到后面景片上，柔边，随烛火闪动。镀金台口、红帷幔和画出来的包厢观众框住舞台，台口宽占画面八到九成。镜头：慢推、横移，近景微震；结尾拉远露出暗房间。避免：鲜艳矢量色、真 3D 纵深、柔和摄影光、没有剪边的人物。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. table0.5s

    Wide: the toy theatre on a mahogany table in a dark parlour, the 'Theatre Royal' proscenium, a sheet of cut-out characters and the player's hand at the side; slow push in.

    全景：暗房间里红木桌上的玩具剧场，「Theatre Royal」台口，桌上一张人物剪纸，旁边伸进来一只操偶的手；缓慢推近。

  2. stage0.9s

    Tracking shot across the stage, the workhouse hall: Clawd (Oliver) steps out of the line of boys and lifts its empty bowl ('Please, sir…') as Mr Bumble's brow gathers by the steaming copper; the camera tracks right with it.

    沿舞台横移，济贫院饭堂：Clawd（奥利弗）从一排孩子里走出来，举起空碗（Please, sir…），冒着热气的粥锅旁，班布尔的眉头皱起来；镜头随它向右跟。

  3. close1.1s

    Close on Clawd and Mr Bumble: Bumble roars, Clawd cowers with a shiver but keeps the bowl up; a small camera shake on the roar.

    Clawd 和班布尔的近景：班布尔一声大吼，Clawd 缩了一下、发抖，碗还是举着；吼声那一下镜头轻震。

Clawd plays Oliver: a cut-out on its own tin slide, holding out an empty bowl. Three clearly different sizes, wide to stage to close, give the request its build: step forward, bowl up on the beat, Bumble's roar, and Clawd still holding the bowl up.Clawd 扮奥利弗：插在自己滑杆上的纸偶，手里举着空碗。全景、舞台、近景三种景别拉开层次：上前一步，卡拍举起空碗，班布尔一吼，它缩了一下，碗还是举着。

References: 对标：Carol Reed, 1968 · Benjamin Pollock (toy theatre sheets) · George Cruikshank · Pollock's Toy Museum

Midnight Cowboy午夜牛郎

2DWarhol Photo-Silkscreen, Double Elvis沃霍尔照片丝网 · Double Elvis

  • Photo silkscreen照片丝网印
  • Andy Warhol, Double Elvis (1963)沃霍尔《Double Elvis》（1963）
  • Silver paint ground银漆底
  • Off-register colour blocks错位色块

Warhol's photo-silkscreen: a black halftone photo printed unevenly over loose flat colour blocks on silver, the image repeated two and three times. The film has a Factory-style party in it, and his Elvis is a cowboy.沃霍尔的照片丝网：黑色网点照片层印得不匀，错位地压在松散的平涂色块上，底是银色，画面重复两次、三次。片里就有沃霍尔工厂式的派对，而他画的猫王正是牛仔。

Style prompt风格提示词

Andy Warhol photo-silkscreen. Two plates: a high-contrast black halftone photograph at 45°, dots about 1/200 of frame width, few mid-tones; and hand-painted flat colour blocks underneath, offset about 1/100 of frame width with wobbly edges: taxi yellow #F3BE14, skin pink #F2A197, cream #EFE6CF, red #E2462B, on brushed silver paint #BABCC0 with a soft sheen. The one hero subject takes the strongest block colour. Uneven squeegee pressure: dots flood into blotches in places, drag into dry horizontal streaks and pinholes elsewhere, over faint canvas weave. Repetition is the composition: the same figure or object, full-length, printed side by side two then three times, slightly overlapping, on empty silver. Settings appear only as drained-to-silver prints in the repeat. Camera: handheld shake, a push in, flat frontal tableaux. Reference: Andy Warhol, *Double Elvis* (1963). Avoid: clean even halftone, perfect registration, uniform crispness.

安迪·沃霍尔式照片丝网印。两块版：一块是 45° 的高反差黑色半调照片，网点约画面宽的 1/200，几乎没有中间调；下面是手刷平涂色块，相对照片错开约画面宽的 1/100，边缘歪扭：出租车黄 #F3BE14、肤粉 #F2A197、奶油 #EFE6CF、红 #E2462B，底是带柔和光泽的刷涂银漆 #BABCC0。唯一的主角用最强的那块颜色。刮板压力不匀：有的地方网点糊成墨团，有的地方拖成横向干笔条纹和针孔，底下隐约有画布纹。重复就是构图：同一个人物或物体全身，在空银底上并排印两次、再印三次，略有重叠。场景只以褪成银色的印样出现在重复里。镜头：手持晃动、推近、正面平面定格。对标沃霍尔《Double Elvis》（1963）。避免：干净均匀的网点、套色完全对齐、处处一样清晰。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. street1.1s

    Sixth Avenue crosswalk: a yellow cab brakes in, Clawd (Joe Buck) jumps back and stares as Ratso slaps the hood; handheld shake, slow push.

    第六大道的人行横道：黄色出租车急刹冲进来，Clawd（乔·巴克）往后一跳、瞪着眼，拉索拍了一下车头；手持晃动，缓慢推近。

  2. taxi0.6s

    Closer on Ratso yelling into the cab, cigarette on his lip, finger pointing ("I'm walkin' here!"); push in.

    拉索的近景：叼着烟冲出租车大吼、手指着司机（I'm walkin' here!）；推近。

  3. warhol0.8s

    The street drains to silver and prints itself into Warhol's Double: two overlapping Clawds in Stetsons with revolvers, then three; flat frontal frame.

    街景褪成银色，印成沃霍尔的 Double：两个戴斯泰森帽、手持左轮的 Clawd 叠在一起，再变成三个；正面平视的定格画面。

Clawd plays Joe Buck: black Stetson, red neckerchief and suede fringe on its arms. First the film's "I'm walkin' here!": cab, jump back, slap, yell. Then the image turns into a Warhol print, with Clawd in Elvis's gunslinger stance, printed twice, then three times.Clawd 扮乔·巴克：黑色斯泰森帽、红色领巾、手臂上的麂皮流苏。先演电影里那句「I'm walkin' here!」：出租车急刹、它往后一跳、拉索拍车头大吼。然后画面变成沃霍尔丝网，Clawd 摆出猫王的枪手站姿，先印两次，再印三次。

References: 对标：John Schlesinger, 1969 · Andy Warhol, Double Elvis (1963) · Andy Warhol, Silver Car Crash (Double Disaster) (1963) · 1969 original poster

No.431970

Patton巴顿将军

2DWWII War-Bond Lithograph二战战争债券石印海报

  • Stone lithograph poster石印海报
  • 1942–45 US war-bond and recruiting posters1942–45 美军征兵与战争债券海报
  • 45° halftone, off-register colour stones45° 网点、套色错位

Flat-colour stone lithograph in the manner of 1940s US war-bond posters: posterised folds, a dark key line, halftone shadows, yellowed stock. It fits Patton's opening speech, where a tiny general stands before a stage-high flag.仿 1940 年代美军战争债券海报的石印画法：分级平涂的褶皱、深色轮廓、网点阴影、泛黄的纸。对应《巴顿将军》开场：小小的将军站在一面占满舞台的巨幅国旗前训话。

Style prompt风格提示词

1940s US war-bond lithograph poster. Flat inks: red #C2342C, navy #213261, olive #5F5C31, khaki #B7975E, on fibrous, foxed cream #E6D5B3 paper. Three values per form (bare paper, flat ink, ink plus halftone), posterised in hard steps. Brown-black key line about 1/500 of frame width, printed last; shadows as coarse 45° halftone bands (dots about 1/150 of frame width) with crayon grain; colour plates offset about 1/600 of frame width. Default: a symmetrical frontal stage, a small subject before a huge graphic shape filling most of the frame (flag, emblem, sunburst), lights as hard pale shapes or bare paper; outdoors too, light falling as a hard pale pool. Lettering optional, in the user's language. Camera: slow push-ins, one crisp pull-back. Reference: US war-bond posters, 1942–45. Avoid: airbrushed gradients, perfect registration, halftone so fine it reads grey.

1940 年代美国战争债券石印海报。平涂油墨：红 #C2342C、海军蓝 #213261、橄榄绿 #5F5C31、卡其 #B7975E，印在米白 #E6D5B3 的纸上，有纤维、霉点和磨边。每个形体三档：留纸、平涂、油墨加网点，压成硬边色阶。棕黑轮廓线约画面宽的 1/500，最后压上；阴影是粗的 45° 网点带（点径约画面宽的 1/150），带蜡笔颗粒；色版错开约画面宽的 1/600。默认像舞台：对称正面，小主体站在占满大半画面的巨大图形前（旗帜、徽章、放射光芒），灯光是硬边浅色块或留纸；户外也一样，光落成地上的硬边浅斑。文字可有可无，用用户的语言。镜头慢推，配一次干脆的拉出。对标 1942–45 年美国战争债券海报。避免：喷枪渐变、套色完全对齐、网点细到像一片灰。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. reveal1.0s

    Red and cream stripes fill the frame (a graphic match from the previous film's Warhol grid), then one pull-back finds Clawd walking up from below frame to its mark before the flag.

    红白条纹铺满画面（接上一部沃霍尔网格的图形匹配），随即一个拉镜头，Clawd 从画面下方走上来，站到巨幅国旗前。

  2. close0.9s

    Medium close-up, a slow push with a slight shake: Clawd snaps up the salute under the four-star helmet, holds it, drops it, and the riding crop cracks into its palm with a hard nod.

    中近景，镜头带轻微抖动慢推：Clawd 在四星钢盔下啪地敬礼，停住，放下，马鞭拍进手心，用力一点头。

  3. wide0.5s

    Wide: the camera pulls out past the flag to the whole empty theatre, red curtains, gilt proscenium and footlights, Clawd tiny at centre stage.

    全景：镜头拉出，越过国旗看到整个空剧场：红幕、镀金台口、脚灯，Clawd 在舞台中央缩成一个小点。

Clawd plays Patton: four-star helmet, ribbon bars and medals, ivory-gripped revolver, riding crop. It walks to its mark under the stripes; on the cut to the close-up the salute snaps up, holds, drops, and the crop cracks into its palm with a nod, as if the speech begins. Two hard cuts; the final pull-back shrinks it before the flag.Clawd 扮巴顿：四星钢盔、勋表和奖章、象牙柄左轮、马鞭。它在条纹下走到位，切进近景时啪地敬礼，停住，放下，马鞭拍进手心、一点头，像训话开始。两次硬切，最后的拉镜头让它在巨旗前缩成一个小点。

The French Connection法国贩毒网

3D16mm Chase Under the El高架铁下追车 · 16mm 颗粒 3D

  • three.js 3D street setthree.js 3D 街景
  • 16mm documentary grain16mm 纪录片颗粒
  • cold cyan-shadow print grade暗部偏青的冷灰印片调色

A full 3D Brooklyn avenue under the elevated railway, graded like grainy 16mm: cold grey, cyan shadows, high contrast, gate weave. The film is remembered for its car chase under the el, and only a real 3D world gives that speed.整条布鲁克林高架铁下的大街用 3D 建出来，再调成粗颗粒 16mm：冷灰、暗部偏青、高反差、片门抖动。这部片最有名的就是高架铁下的追车，3D 才拍得出那种速度。

Style prompt风格提示词

Gritty early-1970s documentary look in a detailed 3D world, finished as pushed 16mm. Grade: low saturation, high contrast; neutral-cold greys #7A7C7A, cyan shadows #1E2A2C, warm-brown mid-tones #6E4A34 in brick, wood, vehicles (at least a quarter of the frame), never an overall blue; the one hero (e.g. #D97757) stays saturated. Default overcast, sky near-white, at any time of day; sun through any overhead grid (track, bridge, pier) falls in hard stripes. Grain clumps about 1/700 of frame width, ±8% brightness; gate weave, flicker, dust, occasional faint ghosts of bright lights. Dense clutter fitting the place. Handheld cameras ride the action: a low 18–25 mm lens rushing forward as repeated objects strobe past, a rig looking back at the subject, a corner wide. References: William Friedkin, *The French Connection* (1971); cinematographer Owen Roizman. Avoid: clean CG shine, blue tint, empty places, mushy grain.

70 年代初粗粝的纪实感，放在细致的 3D 世界里，按增感冲洗的 16mm 收尾。调色：低饱和、高反差；中性冷灰 #7A7C7A、带青的暗部 #1E2A2C，砖、木头、车辆保持暖棕 #6E4A34，至少占四分之一画面，绝不整体泛蓝；唯一的主角（比如橙 #D97757）保持饱和。默认阴天，天空亮到近白，任何时段都一样；阳光穿过头顶格栅（轨道、桥、栈桥）落成硬边条纹。颗粒成团，约画面宽的 1/700，亮度 ±8%；片门晃动、闪烁、灰尘，亮处偶有淡重影。杂物多且符合地点。手持机位跟着动作走：18 到 25mm 低机位向前冲，重复物体频闪掠过；回看主体的跟拍机位；墙角广角。对标 William Friedkin《法国贩毒网》（1971）和摄影师 Owen Roizman。避免：干净的 CG 反光、整体泛蓝、空荡的方块场景、颗粒糊成一片。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. wide0.8s

    Wide at the corner: a train crosses overhead as the brown Pontiac skids onto the avenue with its rear tyres smoking; the lens slowly widens.

    路口全景：列车从头顶驶过，棕色庞蒂亚克甩尾冲上大街，后轮冒烟；镜头视角慢慢放宽。

  2. bumper0.8s

    The famous bumper mount: a low lens rushing up the avenue with motion blur, the el's columns strobing past.

    片中有名的保险杠机位：超低机位带着运动模糊往前冲，高架铁的柱子一根根闪过。

  3. window0.9s

    中景，从车外透过摇下的车窗看 Clawd 扮的道尔：猪肉派帽、咬牙握着方向盘，身后高架铁和店面滑过，带车载抖动。

Clawd plays Popeye Doyle: pork-pie hat, angry eyes, both hands on the wheel. The chase clock runs well ahead of screen time, so the speed-up reads as more speed. Three hard cuts, corner skid to bumper rush to the driver, with no pause in between.Clawd 扮“大力水手”道尔：猪肉派帽、怒眼、双手握方向盘。追车的场景时间比屏幕时间跑得快得多，加速看起来就是车更快。三个硬切：路口甩尾 → 保险杠狂冲 → 驾驶员，中间不停。

References: 对标：William Friedkin, The French Connection (1971) · Owen Roizman (cinematography) · The French Connection one-sheet (1971)

No.451972

The Godfather教父

2DRembrandt Top-Light Oil Painting伦勃朗顶光明暗油画

  • Chiaroscuro oil painting明暗油画
  • Gordon Willis's top light戈登·威利斯的顶光
  • Rembrandt and Caravaggio glazing伦勃朗、卡拉瓦乔式罩染

Oil painting in deep amber and umber where only the top light is warm: thin transparent glazes in the darks, thick impasto on the lit edges. It recreates Gordon Willis's overhead light in the 'I believe in America' study.深琥珀和赭褐的油画，只有顶光落下的地方是暖的：暗部是透明的薄罩染，受光边缘是厚涂。重现戈登·威利斯在“I believe in America”那间书房里的顶光。

Style prompt风格提示词

Chiaroscuro oil painting after Rembrandt and Caravaggio, one overhead light. About 70% of the frame is readable warm dark, mostly umber #24150B, near-black #070403 only in the deepest corners. Amber #8F5D2A mid-tones about 20%; top planes reach #E2B673. The hero stands in the light pool, lit area at least 1/4 of frame height; brows shade the eyes. One red accent #9C1A1A. Darks thin smooth glazes; lights thick impasto, ridged strokes about 1/100 of frame width; modelling soft, never scratchy. Amber varnish, heavy vignette, canvas grain (±3%). A warm bright opening (window, doorway, horizon) may glow behind; a small lamp in frame is a small glow, not a second key. Camera: slow pull-backs and pushes. Reference: Francis Ford Coppola, *The Godfather* (1972), cinematographer Gordon Willis. Avoid: lifting the darks, flat light, faces popping out, frames too dark to read.

伦勃朗、卡拉瓦乔式明暗油画，只有一盏顶光。约七成画面是看得出形体的暖暗部，大多是赭褐 #24150B，近黑 #070403 只在最深的角落。琥珀 #8F5D2A 中间调约两成；朝上的面亮到 #E2B673。主角整个站在光池里，受光部分至少占画面高的四分之一；眉骨把眼睛压进阴影。一处饱和的红 #9C1A1A。暗部薄而平滑地罩染，亮部是带亮笔脊的厚涂，笔触约画面宽的 1/100；过渡柔和，不用刮擦线条。琥珀光油，重暗角，细画布纹（±3%）。背景可有一处暖色亮口（窗、门洞、发亮的天边）；画内的小灯只是一小团光，不算第二个主光。镜头慢拉、慢推。对标 Francis Ford Coppola《教父》（1972），摄影师 Gordon Willis。避免：提亮暗部、平光、人脸跳出来、暗到看不清。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. opening1.7s

    From Bonasera's grey head in the dark, the camera pulls slowly back to reveal the Don in his wing chair, a cat at his knee.

    从暗处博纳塞拉的灰发开始，镜头缓缓拉出，露出坐在翼背椅里的教父和他膝前的猫。

  2. close1.0s

    Close on Clawd under the top light, slowly stroking the grey tabby, bow tie and red rose catching the light; a slow push.

    近景：顶光下的 Clawd 缓缓抚摸灰色虎斑猫，领结和红玫瑰吃到光；镜头慢推。

  3. window0.8s

    推向百叶窗：窗外阳光下的婚礼，虚而过曝，左边是黑根的剪影。

  4. hand1.5s

    The whole study with a slight push: the puppeteer's hand comes down from the top of frame and its strings draw taut over Clawd.

Clawd plays Don Corleone: black bow tie, wing collar, red rose, white cuffs, one hand on a cat. All twelve beats run at 1× to keep Willis's patient camera; each stroke of the cat takes about four seconds, with one blink. Hard cuts; the last shot lowers Fujita's puppeteer hand from the poster over it.Clawd 扮唐·柯里昂：黑领结、翼领、红玫瑰、白袖口，一只手压在猫背上。12 拍全按 1 倍速，保留威利斯那种耐心的镜头；抚猫一下约 4 秒，中间眨一次眼。全部硬切，最后一镜让原海报的提线手降到它头上。

References: 对标：Francis Ford Coppola, The Godfather (1972) · Gordon Willis (cinematography) · Rembrandt, The Night Watch · Caravaggio · S. Neil Fujita, The Godfather poster logo

The Sting骗中骗

2DSaturday Evening Post Illustration《星期六晚邮报》插画

  • Saturday Evening Post cover illustration《星期六晚邮报》封面插画
  • Leyendecker block modellingLeyendecker 式块面塑形
  • Jaroslav Gebr's chapter cardsJaroslav Gebr 的章节卡

Warm, clean magazine illustration in the Leyendecker–Rockwell line: blocky lit planes, parallel strokes, crisp dark contours. The Sting's own chapter cards imitate Saturday Evening Post covers, so its 1936 betting parlour is drawn the same way.Leyendecker、Rockwell 一路的杂志插画：块面受光、平行笔触、利落的深色轮廓。片中章节卡就仿《星期六晚邮报》封面，1936 年的下注厅也照这个画。

Style prompt风格提示词

1930s Saturday Evening Post cover illustration. Realist figures, correct proportions, expressive faces, built from blocky planes (lit, shadow, warm bounce from below) filled with blended strokes about 1/150 of frame width, varied in length, following the form, never stripes. Dark contour thicker on the shadow side, 1/800–1/300 of frame width. Warm key from upper left. Palette: plaster #E2C492, oak #764626, slate #28322C, brass #E2B046, ink #341E18; oak and ink darks about a third of the frame; sky plaster cooled with slate, water light slate, skin plaster plus brass. Grainy board (±3%), warm vignette. Default one-point perspective, period detail, haze, secondary figures if the story has any. Each figure is drawn once and moves whole (breathing, sway, sliding along a path). Camera: one long, smooth push in. References: J. C. Leyendecker, Norman Rockwell. Avoid: stiff cut-out figures, even outlines, muddy colour.

30 年代《星期六晚邮报》封面插画。写实人物，比例准确、表情生动，用块面塑造（受光、背光、下方暖色反光），块面里填顺着形体、长短不一的笔触，宽约画面宽的 1/150，柔和衔接，不成条纹。深色轮廓在背光一侧加粗，画面宽的 1/800 到 1/300。暖色主光从左上来。配色：灰泥 #E2C492、橡木 #764626、石板 #28322C、黄铜 #E2B046、墨 #341E18；橡木和墨色暗部约占三分之一画面；天空灰泥偏石板，水是浅石板，皮肤灰泥加黄铜。轻颗粒画板（±3%）加暖暗角。默认一点透视，时代细节，薄雾，故事有就加次要人物。每个人物只画一遍，整体移动（呼吸、摇摆、沿路径滑行）。镜头：一次长而平滑的推进。对标 J. C. Leyendecker、Norman Rockwell。避免：僵硬剪纸人物、粗细一样的轮廓、发脏的颜色。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. push2.5s

    一镜到底：从下注厅全景（赔率黑板、下注窗口、窗口里的冈多夫）一路推到扮胡克的 Clawd，它把手指贴到鼻侧打暗号，再冲镜头压一下帽檐。

Clawd plays Johnny Hooker: pale fedora, red bow tie, braces. There is no cut, only one push. As the camera arrives it gives the con men's signal, a finger beside the nose, then tips its hat and blinks at the lens, letting the audience in on the con.Clawd 扮胡克：浅色礼帽、红领结、背带。整段不切，只有一个推镜头。推到位时它打出骗子们的暗号（手指贴鼻侧），接着压帽檐、冲镜头眨眼，像把观众拉进骗局。

References: 对标：George Roy Hill, The Sting (1973) · Jaroslav Gebr (chapter cards) · Richard Amsel (poster) · J. C. Leyendecker · Norman Rockwell

2.5DHand-Tinted Sepia Photograph手工上色棕褐老照片

A turn-of-the-century toned silver print with dyes brushed on by hand: sepia, soft lens, silver grain, foxing, a cracked corner, faint red, blue and verdigris. It shows young Vito arriving at Ellis Island in 1901, as in Lewis Hine's photographs.世纪之交的调色银盐照片，手工刷色：棕褐调、柔焦、银盐颗粒、霉斑、裂角，局部淡淡的红、蓝、铜绿。画的是 1901 年少年维托抵达埃利斯岛，像 Lewis Hine 的照片。

Style prompt风格提示词

A hand-coloured toned silver print from about 1900. Build lit 3D forms, soft key from upper left, then 'print' them: soft focus, light halation, grain heaviest in mid-tones (±5%), full sepia range #281A12, #987048, #EEE0C2; at least a fifth of the frame reaches the dark end, in any light. Figures: real proportions (head 1/7 of body), soft shading. Transparent dyes brushed loosely past edges: the one hero subject strongly (e.g. orange-red #D97757 at 60–80% opacity), a faint touch on one or two props; dyes die in shadow. Light ageing: vignette, silvered edges (grey-blue sheen in border darks), small foxing spots, fine scratches, at most one cracked corner. Frontal, documentary: figures stand still facing the lens. Camera: slow pan across faces. References: Lewis Hine; Augustus Sherman (Ellis Island). Avoid: toy figures, digital fill colour, low-contrast fog, big mould blotches.

1900 年前后手工上色的棕褐银盐照片。先做受光的立体形体，柔光从左上来，再“印”出来：柔焦带轻微光晕，颗粒在中间调最重（±5%）；棕褐调从 #281A12 经 #987048 到 #EEE0C2 完整铺开，至少五分之一画面落到最深一端，任何光线下都一样。人物比例真实（头约七分之一身高），明暗柔和。透明颜料松松地刷，溢出边缘：唯一的主角最重（比如橙红 #D97757，不透明度六到八成），一两件道具轻点；颜色进暗部就被吞掉。做旧要轻：暗角、边缘银化（靠边暗部泛灰蓝金属光）、几个小霉点、细划痕，最多一个裂角。构图正面、纪实，人物静立面对镜头。镜头缓慢横摇扫过人脸。对标 Lewis Hine、Augustus Sherman 的埃利斯岛照片。避免：积木人物、数码填色、低反差雾灰、大片霉斑。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. crowd0.8s

    A horizontal sweep across the upturned faces of immigrants on deck, rising and falling with the ship's roll.

    横摇扫过甲板上仰头的移民群像，画面随船身起伏。

Clawd plays the boy Vito Andolini: a grey tweed flat cap and a paper name tag round its neck, small among adults. Screen time averages 1.3×, with Liberty at 1×. Hard cuts run faces, statue (point of view), reaction; the last beat is a glance down at its new name, then up at the new country.Clawd 扮少年维托：灰色粗花呢鸭舌帽，脖子上挂着纸名牌，夹在大人中间显得很小。平均 1.3 倍速，自由女神那一镜是 1 倍。三个硬切：人群 → 雕像（主观）→ 反应；最后的表演点是低头看自己的新名字，再抬头望向新的国家。

No.481975

One Flew Over the Cuckoo's Nest飞越疯人院

2DHopper-Style American Realism霍珀式美国写实油画

  • Edward Hopper realism爱德华·霍珀式写实
  • hard-edged slanting window light硬边斜射窗光
  • flat oil planes with dragged strokes平整油彩块面加拖笔

Quiet American realism after Edward Hopper: big flat planes of pale green, hard parallelograms of low morning sun, figures standing apart. It suits the ward of Cuckoo's Nest, fluorescent order broken by one act of escape.仿爱德华·霍珀的安静写实：大块平整的淡绿墙面、低角度晨光投下的硬边平行四边形、彼此隔开的人。正合《飞越疯人院》的病房：日光灯下的秩序，被一次出逃打破。

Style prompt风格提示词

Edward Hopper-style American realist painting. Large flat planes of restrained, pale colour: institutional greens #9EB8A0 and #5E7C66, grey-green floor #7E8876, cream #ECE6D2, on visible canvas (±3%), with a few long dragged strokes about 1/80 of frame width, 5–10% off the base. Low side sun throws hard-edged parallelograms of warm light #FCE6AC on walls and ground, crossed by crisp shadows of frames or railings, indoors or out. Sky pale cream-green; skin warmed cream; clothes darkened greens. Figures painted from 3–4 overlapping soft-edged value shapes, no outline, real proportions; several people stand apart. Stillness, then one sudden event under a second: a whip pan, a burst of light. Signature shot: a still one-point interior, light on the floor. References: Edward Hopper, *Office in a Small City* (1953). Avoid: heavy outlines, vector figures, dark saturated walls, fuzzy light patches.

爱德华·霍珀式美国写实绘画。大块克制、偏浅的平面色：机构绿 #9EB8A0 和 #5E7C66、灰绿地面 #7E8876、奶油 #ECE6D2，看得见画布纹（±3%），每块拖几笔长笔触，宽约画面宽的 1/80，亮或暗百分之五到十。低角度阳光从一侧射来，在墙和地上投下硬边暖色平行四边形 #FCE6AC，压着窗框或栏杆的清晰影子，室内户外都一样。天空浅奶油绿，皮肤是提暖的奶油色，衣服用压暗的绿。人物由三四块边缘柔和的明暗色块叠成，不勾轮廓，比例写实；几个人时分开站。大部分时间静止，然后一件不到一秒的突发：甩镜或一阵强光。招牌镜头：一点透视的静止室内，地上有光块。对标霍珀《Office in a Small City》（1953）。避免：粗轮廓、矢量人物、深而饱和的墙、模糊光斑。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. throw2.5s

    One travelling shot in the day room: Clawd heaves a marble hydrotherapy console over its head and hurls it; the camera whip-pans with it to the back window as the glass explodes and dawn pours in, then swings back through the light to Clawd cheering, arms up.

    日间活动室里的一镜到底：Clawd 把大理石水疗台举过头顶扔出去，镜头跟着甩到后窗，玻璃炸开、晨光涌进来，再穿过光甩回 Clawd，它举起双臂欢呼。

Clawd wears McMurphy's black watch cap and plaid collar but performs the film's ending: the console through the window. Screen time equals scene time: crouch and strain, lift, the throw at 1.0 s, impact at 1.3 s with a flash and shards flying at the lens, then the cheer. Blurred whip pans replace cuts.Clawd 戴着麦克墨菲的黑色针织帽和格子领，演的是片尾那一下：把水疗台砸出窗外。屏幕时间等于场景时间：蹲下用力、举起、1.0 秒扔出、1.3 秒撞窗，闪光加碎玻璃飞向镜头，然后欢呼。用带运动模糊的甩镜代替剪切。

References: 对标：Miloš Forman, One Flew Over the Cuckoo's Nest (1975) · Edward Hopper, Office in a Small City; Morning Sun · Edward Hopper, Nighthawks · Haskell Wexler and Bill Butler (cinematography)

No.491976

Rocky洛奇

3DPhiladelphia Sunrise, 3D on 35mm费城台阶日出 3D · 35mm 调色

  • three.js 3D citythree.js 3D 城市
  • 1970s 35mm film grade70 年代 35mm 胶片调色
  • Steadicam chase and rising orbit斯坦尼康跟拍与环绕升镜

A full 3D Philadelphia at sunrise, from meat locker to museum steps, graded like 1970s 35mm: cold blue sky, low gold sun, long shadows, haze, fine grain. The run up the 72 steps and the rising orbit at the top are what people remember.清晨的费城整个做成 3D，从肉库到博物馆 72 级台阶，调成 70 年代 35mm：冷蓝天、低低的金色太阳、长影子、薄雾、细颗粒。跑上台阶和顶上的环绕升镜是这部片的记忆点。

Style prompt风格提示词

1970s 35mm realism in a detailed 3D world, sunrise by default (sunset the same). Sun 5–10° up, warm orange #F0A060, lighting the hero from the side or three-quarter front so the lit body and long ground shadows read; no full backlight. Cool blue sky #7FA3CF, pink cloud undersides #E6B2AE; haze golden toward the sun, blue-violet away. Grade: fine grain (±3%), cool shadows, warm highlights, nothing clipped; flare and ghosts only with the sun in frame. Camera: a low Steadicam following the hero from behind up an ascent, then at the top one continuous orbit that circles, rises and pulls back over 6–8 s, showing the lit face, until the place spreads out below. References: John G. Avildsen, *Rocky* (1976); Steadicam operator Garrett Brown. Avoid: flat noon light, detail-less silhouettes, a static climax, plastic CG shine.

70 年代 35mm 写实，放在细致的 3D 世界里，默认日出（日落同理）。太阳离地平线五到十度，暖橙 #F0A060，从侧面或前侧方照亮主角，让受光的身体和拉过地面的长影看得清；不要完全逆光。天空冷蓝 #7FA3CF，云底染粉 #E6B2AE；雾朝太阳一侧发金、背光一侧发蓝紫。调色：细颗粒（±3%），暗部偏冷、高光偏暖，不过曝；只有太阳入画时才有光晕和鬼影。镜头：低机位斯坦尼康从背后跟着主角爬上一段坡或台阶，到最高处接一个连续环绕，边转边升边退，六到八秒，转到受光的脸，直到整个地方在下面铺开。对标 John G. Avildsen《洛奇》（1976）、斯坦尼康操作者 Garrett Brown。避免：正午平光、没细节的剪影、高潮时机位不动、塑料感反光。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. meat0.4s

    Meat locker, medium shot, fixed camera: Clawd in a grey hood jabs at a hanging side of beef among white tiles and bare bulbs.

    肉库中景，定机位：戴灰色兜帽的 Clawd 对着挂着的牛肉出拳，白瓷砖、裸灯泡。

  2. meat_close0.4s

    Closer, fixed camera: the punches land and the beef swings.

    更近一些，定机位：拳头打在牛肉上，牛肉晃荡。

  3. street0.6s

    The Italian Market before dawn, fixed camera: Clawd jogs toward the lens past fire barrels, fruit stands and lit streetlamps.

    黎明前的意大利市场，定机位：Clawd 迎着镜头慢跑，经过油桶火、果蔬摊和还亮着的路灯。

  4. run1.5s

    A low Steadicam chases Clawd from behind as it runs up the 72 steps toward the museum's columns.

    低机位斯坦尼康从背后追着 Clawd 跑上 72 级台阶，前方是博物馆的立柱。

  5. orbit2.1s

    At the top Clawd throws its arms up in a V and hops; the camera circles round it, rising and pulling back until the whole Parkway and the city open up at sunrise.

    到顶，Clawd 双臂举成 V 字一跳；镜头绕着它转、上升、后退，直到整条大道和城市在日出里铺开。

Clawd plays Rocky: a grey sweatshirt hood up and a white towel on one shoulder. The training montage runs at about 1.7× over three quick cuts; from the steps on, the scene plays at 1×. A hard cut into the Steadicam run, then no cut: the run flows straight into the orbit, and the victory hop lands as the camera starts to rise.Clawd 扮洛奇：灰色卫衣兜帽戴起，肩上搭一条白毛巾。训练蒙太奇三个快切，约 1.7 倍速；上台阶以后回到 1 倍。硬切进斯坦尼康跟跑，之后不再切：跑到顶直接接环绕升镜，举臂一跳正好落在镜头开始升起的时候。

References: 对标：John G. Avildsen, Rocky (1976) · Garrett Brown (Steadicam) · James Crabe (cinematography) · Philadelphia Museum of Art, east façade

No.501977

Annie Hall安妮·霍尔

2DNew Yorker Cartoon, Ink and Wash《纽约客》单格漫画 · 墨线淡彩

  • New Yorker single-panel cartoon《纽约客》单格漫画
  • nib-pen ink line钢笔墨线
  • light watercolour wash淡水彩
  • hand-lettered caption手写图说

A New Yorker single-panel cartoon: lively nib-pen lines, a few restrained washes, plenty of white paper and a hand-lettered caption beneath. Woody Allen's comedy shares the magazine's voice, and the lobster scene plays as one gag panel.《纽约客》单格漫画：灵动的钢笔墨线、几块克制的淡彩、大量白纸，画下面一行手写图说。伍迪·艾伦的喜剧和这本杂志是一个调子，龙虾那场戏正好是一格笑话漫画。

Style prompt风格提示词

New Yorker single-panel cartoon, full-bleed, no border. Nib-pen lines about 1/1000 of frame width on straights, swelling to 1/300 in turns, flicking off at the ends; box corners overlap. Pale watercolour washes with pooled edges over most objects, slipping past the lines by at most 1/150 of frame width: grey #9698A0, sea blue #78A0BE, sand #E2CCA0, rust #C45C32, on cold-press paper #FBF8F1 fading to white at the edges. Exaggerated, expressive figures with drawn faces among everyday props. One hand-lettered caption line under the action, in the user's language, about 1/30 of frame height, appearing letter by letter: a deadpan remark. The drawing holds still; living things wobble. Camera: small drifts and pushes; the frame may split into two side-by-side drawings. References: James Thurber, William Steig, Jean-Jacques Sempé. Avoid: saturated colour, uniform vector lines, a small framed panel floating in white.

《纽约客》单格漫画，满版出血，不画格框。蘸水笔墨线直线处细到约画面宽的 1/1000，转弯处粗到约 1/300，收笔一甩；方盒子几笔分开画，角上交叉出头。淡水彩带积色边，铺在大部分物体上，溢出墨线不超过画面宽的 1/150：灰 #9698A0、海蓝 #78A0BE、沙色 #E2CCA0、铁锈 #C45C32，底是冷压纸 #FBF8F1，四边化成白。人物夸张、有表情、画出五官，周围有日常道具，留白但不空荡。画面下方一行手写说明，用用户的语言，字高约画面高的 1/30，逐字写出：一句冷面幽默。画面不动，活物微微颤动。镜头：轻微漂移和推近；画面可一分为二，左右并排两幅。对标 James Thurber、William Steig、桑贝。避免：浓重饱和的颜色、粗细一样的矢量线、白纸中间漂着一个小格子。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

    冰箱后缝的大特写：一只龙虾慢慢探出头；镜头缓慢漂移。

Hard cuts; the split-screen dinner is a graphic exit.Clawd 扮安妮：黛安·基顿那顶宽檐帽、松垮的条纹领带和马甲，手里一台 35mm 相机。约 1.8 倍速，跳起来那一下接近 1 倍：两次闪光拍照，龙虾爬到脚边，吓得一跳，落地笑开。全部硬切，最后用分屏饭桌做一个图形化的出口。

No.511978

The Deer Hunter猎鹿人

2DAndrew Wyeth Egg Tempera安德鲁·怀斯蛋彩

  • Egg tempera on gesso石膏底蛋彩
  • Andrew Wyeth安德鲁·怀斯
  • fine cross-hatched strokes细密交叉短笔

Dry, finely hatched egg tempera after Andrew Wyeth: muted straw, umber and grey-green, grass drawn blade by blade, a pale empty sky. Wyeth was a Pennsylvania painter, and the 'one shot' hunt takes place in the Pennsylvania mountains above a steel town.仿安德鲁·怀斯的蛋彩：干燥细密的交叉短笔，低饱和的草黄、赭褐、灰绿，草叶一根根画出来，天空苍白。怀斯是宾州画家，“一枪”猎鹿就在宾州钢铁小镇上方的山里。

Style prompt风格提示词

Egg tempera on gesso panel after Andrew Wyeth. Dry, matte, very fine: colour built from dense cross-hatched strokes, each about 1/400 of frame width long, hairline thin. Muted palette: pale overcast sky #D9D5C6, straw grass #CDB27A, umber #6A5234, misty grey-green hills #8E938C, rust #8A4E32; water, stone, clothes and skin mixed from these. One accent colour, only on the one hero subject (e.g. a red cap); any light source is a small pale dot, no halo. Ground (dry grass by default, other ground alike): big light and dark patches, fine strokes, dark tufts, then single wind-combed blades; rocks, buildings hatched along their planes. Empty, lonely space; the subject small, off-centre. Soft overcast light; at other times of day stay pale, no warm glow. Camera: slow drift, quiet push; a circular iris may mark a cut. Avoid: wet watercolour blooms, saturated colour, thick shiny strokes.

安德鲁·怀斯式石膏底板蛋彩画。干、哑光、极细：颜色全靠密密的交叉短笔叠出来，每笔长约画面宽的 1/400，细如发丝。低饱和配色：苍白阴天 #D9D5C6、草黄 #CDB27A、赭褐 #6A5234、灰绿远山 #8E938C、铁锈色 #8A4E32；水、石、衣服和皮肤都从这几色调出。强调色只给唯一的主角（比如一顶红帽）；画里的光源只是一个淡淡的小点，不带光晕。地面（默认枯草，其他地面同理）先铺大块深浅，再叠细笔、暗色草团，最后是被风梳过的单根草叶；岩石和房屋用短笔顺着各面排。空间空旷孤独，主体小而偏离中心。默认阴天软光，其他时段也保持苍白、不要暖光。镜头缓慢漂移、安静推近；圆形遮罩可作剪切点。避免：湿水彩晕染、饱和色、厚重发亮的笔触。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. town0.6s

    Wide on the steel town in the valley mist (blast furnaces, smoking chimneys, an onion-domed church), drifting sideways.

    河谷雾里的钢铁小镇全景：高炉、冒烟的烟囱、洋葱顶教堂；镜头缓慢横移。

  2. scope0.7s

    Through the rifle scope: the buck on the crest inside a black circular mask and crosshair, swaying slightly with the hunter's hands.

    瞄准镜视角：黑色圆形遮罩和十字线里，山脊上的雄鹿；画面随猎人的手微微晃动。

  3. deer0.3s

    Close-up of the buck's head and eye; a slow push.

    雄鹿头部和眼睛的特写；镜头慢推。

  4. still0.8s

    Clawd as Michael on a granite outcrop lowers the rifle, shuts its eyes, breathes out and looks up at the buck again, as the camera pulls back to the whole ridge.

    Clawd 扮麦克站在花岗岩上，放下猎枪，闭眼呼出一口气，再抬头望向雄鹿；镜头拉开到整条山脊。

Clawd plays Michael in a red wool hunting cap with a scoped rifle. Valley, scope, eye, hunter: four hard cuts, never faster than about 1.25×, quiet and steady. The beat is the shot not taken: the rifle comes down, eyes shut, one breath out, and it looks up at the buck again.Clawd 扮麦克：红色羊毛猎帽、带瞄准镜的猎枪。河谷 → 瞄准镜 → 鹿眼 → 猎人，四个硬切，平均约 1.24 倍速，安静、稳。表演点是那一枪没开：枪放下，闭眼，呼出一口气，再抬头看那头鹿。

References: 对标：Michael Cimino, The Deer Hunter (1978) · Vilmos Zsigmond (cinematography) · Andrew Wyeth, Christina's World (1948) · Andrew Wyeth, Winter 1946

No.521979

Kramer vs. Kramer克莱默夫妇

2DChild's Wax-Crayon Drawing儿童蜡笔画

  • Wax crayon on textured paper纹理纸上的蜡笔
  • 6–8-year-old's drawing conventions6–8 岁儿童画的画法
  • hand-lettered labels手写标签

A kitchen drawn by a child in wax crayon: wobbly lines, colour pressed into the paper grain and over the edges, labels and arrows, a smiling sun. It is Billy's view of making French toast with his father.孩子用蜡笔画的厨房：歪扭的线、用力涂进纸纹又涂出边界的颜色、标签和箭头、带笑脸的太阳。这是比利眼里他和爸爸做法式吐司的样子。

Style prompt风格提示词

A careful 6–8-year-old's wax-crayon drawing on white paper. Crayon catches the paper grain's peaks; light pressure leaves 40–60% paper showing, heavy pressure fills it; overlaps show through. Outlines in black or dark-brown crayon, waxy, wobbling, about 1/300 of frame width. Fills are back-and-forth scribbles, strokes about 1/40 of frame width long and 1/250 wide, rows overshooting the outlines, each area in its own direction, spacing and angle uneven, never a regular mesh. Primary box: red #E0382C, orange #F28A1E, yellow #F7D23A, green #3AA345, blue #2E6BD8. Child conventions: a ground line, flattened perspective, arrowed labels, a smiling sun, one mirrored letter (in Chinese, a reversed stroke), patterns coloured cell by cell. Text in the user's language, in wobbly crayon strokes, not a typeface. Camera: shaky close-ups, then a calm drift. Avoid: neat adult rendering, random scrawl, uniform stipple or mesh, smooth gradients.

认真的六到八岁孩子在白纸上画的蜡笔画。蜡笔只蹭到纸纹凸起；轻涂露出四到六成纸白，用力处填满；叠色时下层透出。轮廓用黑色或深棕蜡笔，蜡质、发抖，粗约画面宽的 1/300。填色是来回涂抹，每笔长约画面宽的 1/40、宽约 1/250，一排排涂、冲出轮廓，每块各有方向，间距和角度不均匀，不能排成规整的网。基础色：红 #E0382C、橙 #F28A1E、黄 #F7D23A、绿 #3AA345、蓝 #2E6BD8。儿童画惯例：地面线、压扁的透视、带箭头的标注、笑脸太阳、写反一个字母（中文写反一个偏旁）、图案一格格涂。文字用用户的语言、发抖的蜡笔笔画写，不用字体。镜头：先晃动近景，再平稳扫过全画。避免：大人式的工整画法、没细节的乱涂、均匀点状或网状肌理、平滑渐变。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. toast0.7s

    Extreme close-up of the pan the first time: burnt toast, black crayon smoke, eggshells; a shaky handheld slowly pulling back.

    第一次做的平底锅大特写：焦吐司、黑色蜡笔烟、蛋壳；手持抖动，慢慢拉开。

  2. toast20.4s

    Match cut to the same pan at the end of the film, clean now: a golden slice flips up out of it.

    匹配剪辑到片尾的同一口锅，干干净净：一片金黄的吐司从锅里翻起来。

  3. still1.4s

    Father and son side by side under the title 'ME AND DADDY MAKE FRENCH TOAST': Clawd catches the toast on its spatula, Billy beams and a heart pops up, Clawd hops with pride; the camera drifts up to the crayon skyline in the window.

    标题“ME AND DADDY MAKE FRENCH TOAST”下父子并肩：Clawd 用锅铲接住吐司，比利笑开，冒出一颗红心，Clawd 得意地小跳一下；镜头往上漂到窗外的蜡笔天际线。

Clawd plays Ted Kramer with a blue apron tie and a spatula; Billy stands on a chair whisking. The first attempt and the last are joined by a match cut on the same pan, so the whole relationship fits in one jump. About 1.35× overall; the catch lands on the cut to the wide shot, then a hop and a glance at camera.Clawd 扮泰德：系着蓝色围裙带，拿锅铲；比利站在椅子上打蛋。第一次和最后一次用同一口锅做匹配剪辑，父子关系在一次跳切里讲完。整体约 1.35 倍速；切进全景时正好接住吐司，接着小跳一下、看一眼镜头。

References: 对标：Robert Benton, Kramer vs. Kramer (1979) · Néstor Almendros (cinematography) · Kramer vs. Kramer one-sheet (1979) · Children's drawings, ages 6–8

The Blockbuster Era大片年代1980–1989

No.531980

Ordinary People普通人

2DAutumn Transparent Watercolour秋日透明水彩

  • Transparent watercolour glazes透明水彩叠色
  • Winslow Homer and John Singer SargentWinslow Homer 与 John Singer Sargent
  • HB pencil underdrawingHB 铅笔底稿

Transparent watercolour in layered glazes: wet-in-wet sky and lake, tide-mark edges, blooms, white paper left for highlights, autumn gold and rust against cool grey-blue. It matches the quiet, sad Lake Forest autumn of the film's opening.层层叠色的透明水彩：湿画法的天空和湖面、水渍边线、回流花、留白的纸当高光，秋天的金和锈红配上冷灰蓝。对上影片开场那个安静又带点忧伤的 Lake Forest 秋天。

Style prompt风格提示词

Transparent watercolour in layered glazes on cold-press paper. Draw shapes crisply first, then two or three glazes, each smaller and darker, drying to a tide-mark edge; two or three blooms, about 1/10 of frame width, inside large washes, never on figures; light granulation (±4%). Paper is the brightest white; light objects and highlights left as paper. Sky and water wet-in-wet, ripples dry-brush. Roles: autumn gold #ECAA34, scarlet #E46030, rust #B8743C for foliage and accents, against grey-blue mist #C2C6D2 and violet-grey shadows #8A8496; sky pale and cool at any time of day; ground pale green-ochre. Foliage: pale wash, scallop-edged clusters, dark dots, flicked leaves; with few trees, warm colours go to scrub, roofs, clothes. Light from one side. Camera: slow pan, gentle push, leaves drifting past the lens. Reference: Winslow Homer's watercolours. Avoid: opaque gouache, airbrushed blur, vector edges, muddy colour, saturated sunsets.

透明水彩，在冷压纸上一层层罩色。先把形状画清楚，再罩两三层，每层更小更深，干后边缘积成深一点的水渍线；每帧两三处回流花，约画面宽的 1/10，只在大块湿画里，不落在人物上；颗粒轻沉进纸纹（±4%）。纸是最亮的白，浅色物体和高光留纸。天空和水面湿画，涟漪干笔。分工：秋金 #ECAA34、猩红 #E46030、锈色 #B8743C 给树叶和点缀，对着冷灰蓝的雾 #C2C6D2 和紫灰阴影 #8A8496；天空任何时段都浅而冷；地面浅绿赭。树冠：先铺淡色，叠扇贝边叶团，点深色，甩散叶；树少时暖色给灌木、屋顶、衣服。光从一侧来。镜头缓慢横摇、轻推，落叶从镜头前飘过。对标 Winslow Homer 的水彩。避免：水粉式覆盖、喷枪模糊、矢量硬边、脏色、饱和晚霞。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. lake0.8s

    Empty dawn shot of Lake Michigan: a paper-white sun path on grey-blue water, then a slow pan right to the autumn bluff.

    黎明的密歇根湖空镜：灰蓝湖面上留白的太阳光路，镜头慢慢右摇到秋天的崖岸。

  2. still1.0s

    The street under the maples: Clawd as Conrad, long striped scarf and satchel, stops on the sidewalk and turns to look back at the white colonial house where Calvin rakes leaves, then drops its eyes and turns away; a slow drift right with a slight pull back.

    枫树下的街道：扮康拉德的 Clawd 围着长条纹围巾、背着书包，在人行道上停下，回头看那栋白色殖民地式房子（卡尔文在院里耙叶），又垂下眼睛转开；镜头缓慢右移并略微拉开。

  3. porch0.7s

    Beth on the porch, arms folded, cold; the camera creeps in on her.

    门廊上的贝丝抱着双臂，神情冷淡；镜头慢慢推近她。

Clawd plays Conrad: a long navy-and-cream school scarf blown by the lake wind, and a leather satchel. The scene runs at 1.1× throughout, as slow as the Pachelbel opening. Three hard cuts: the lake, the son looking back at the house, the mother who gives nothing back. Its eyes moving from the house to the ground is the whole performance.Clawd 扮康拉德：一条藏青配奶白的长校园围巾被湖风吹起，背一只皮书包。全程 1.1 倍速，和帕赫贝尔卡农的慢开场一样慢。三个硬切：湖、回头看家的儿子、没有回应的母亲。它的目光从房子落到地上，就是全部表演。

References: 对标：Robert Redford, Ordinary People (1980) · John Bailey (cinematography) · Winslow Homer (watercolours) · John Singer Sargent (watercolours)

No.541981

Chariots of Fire烈火战车

2D1924 Paris Olympics Stone Lithograph1924 巴黎奥运石印海报

  • Four-stone lithograph四版石印
  • Jean Droit's 1924 Olympic posterJean Droit 的 1924 奥运海报
  • classical anatomical figures古典解剖人体

A four-colour stone lithograph after Jean Droit's 1924 Paris Olympics poster: classical bodies, a few inks, clumped stone grain, ragged registration on cream paper. Chariots of Fire builds to the 1924 Games, so its beach run becomes that poster coming to life.仿 Jean Droit 1924 巴黎奥运海报的四色石印：古典人体、有限油墨、成团的石版颗粒、参差套色。《烈火战车》讲的是 1924 年奥运，海滩长跑像那张海报活了过来。

Style prompt风格提示词

1920s stone lithograph poster in four inks: sea blue #245A8C, ochre #D49640, deep brown #40271B, warm red #E47E62, on aged cream #F1E7D1, misregistered about 1/700 of frame width. Every tone is ink grain, never a flat fill: specks about 1/1000 of frame width in soft clumps, covering 20–40% of pale areas, 50–80% of mid-tones, nearly all of solids (with pinholes); no stray specks on bare paper. Roles: blue for sky, water, cool shadow; ochre for ground and skin; brown for outlines, darks, clothes; red sparingly. Values calm and pale. Classical figures: ochre lit side, brown core-shadow line down each limb, head 1/8 of body; groups in a row. Slow motion carried by spray, dust, hair, cloth. Camera: long-lens front views, ground-level shots; optional serif title band. Reference: Jean Droit, 1924 Paris Olympics poster. Avoid: coarse speckle, pictogram figures, vector fills.

20 年代石版印刷海报，四色：海蓝 #245A8C、赭黄 #D49640、深棕 #40271B、暖红 #E47E62，印在泛旧的奶油纸 #F1E7D1 上，每色错位约画面宽的 1/700。所有色调都由油墨颗粒组成，不平涂：颗粒约画面宽的 1/1000，聚成软团，浅色区覆盖两到四成，中间调五到八成，实色几乎满版、留针孔；空白纸上不能有零散孤点。分工：蓝给天空、水和冷阴影；赭黄给地面和皮肤；深棕给轮廓、暗部和衣服；红少量点缀。明暗安静偏浅。人物古典：受光侧赭黄，四肢顺一道深棕明暗交界线，头约身高八分之一；多人排成横列。动作慢，靠水花、尘土、头发、布料带出。镜头：长焦正面、贴地低机位；可加衬线字标题横带。对标 Jean Droit 的 1924 年巴黎奥运海报。避免：粗噪点、象形小人、矢量平涂。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. faces0.7s

    Long-lens front view: the pack comes toward camera along the surf, Clawd among them with a white headband and race number 98; a slight pull back.

    长焦正面：跑者队伍沿着浪边迎面跑来，Clawd 在其中，戴白头带、别着 98 号码布；镜头略微拉开。

  2. feet0.6s

    Low and tight on bare feet splashing through the shallows, reflections in the wet sand; the camera slides sideways.

    贴地近景：赤脚踩进浅水溅起水花，湿沙上有倒影；镜头横向滑动。

  3. liddell0.5s

    Close on Liddell, head thrown back and mouth open, Abrahams behind him; a slight pull back.

    利德尔的近景：仰头张嘴，亚伯拉罕斯在他身后；镜头略微拉开。

  4. still0.8s

    Side view of the whole line with Clawd in the middle; as the camera pulls back, the 'JEUX DE LA VIIIe OLYMPIADE · PARIS 1924' poster band prints in over the frame.

    侧面看整队跑者，Clawd 在中间；镜头拉开时，“JEUX DE LA VIIIe OLYMPIADE · PARIS 1924”的海报字框印上画面。

Clawd runs in the pack with a white headband and a pinned number 98. The run plays at 1×, so it reads as the film's slow motion. Four hard cuts (faces, feet, Liddell's head-back stride, the side-on line), and the poster band printing over the last shot turns the scene into the 1924 poster.Clawd 跑在队伍里：白头带、胸前别着 98 号码布。跑步按 1 倍速，看起来就是原片的慢镜头。四个硬切：面孔、脚、利德尔仰头的跑姿、侧面整队；最后一镜印上海报字框，把这场戏变成 1924 年的海报。

References: 对标：Hugh Hudson, Chariots of Fire (1981) · Jean Droit, Jeux de la VIIIe Olympiade, Paris 1924 (poster) · Chariots of Fire one-sheet (1981) · Vangelis (score)

Gandhi甘地传

2DRajasthani Hand Block Print拉贾斯坦手工木刻印花布

  • Bagru dabu indigo resistBagru 泥防染靛蓝
  • Sanganer block-printed bordersSanganer 木版印花边
  • Rajasthani miniature profiles拉贾斯坦细密画侧脸

Hand block print on cotton in indigo, madder and turmeric with a carved black key block, misregistered at the block seams, dye bleeding along the weave. Homespun khadi was Gandhi's symbol, so the Salt March is printed as cloth.棉布上的手工木刻印花：靛蓝、茜草红、姜黄加黑色雕版轮廓，版缝处套色错位，染料顺着经纬渗开。手纺土布是甘地的象征，所以食盐进军就印成一块布。

Style prompt风格提示词

Hand block print on cotton, Rajasthan style (Bagru, Sanganer). Undyed cotton #EEE5CF is the ground, over half the frame, and the skin colour; indigo #2A3A68 for water or sky bands and patterns; madder red #AA322A for borders, clothing and the hero; turmeric #DEA42C for small motifs; black key block #281E1C for outlines. Stamp blocks about 1/8 of frame height, misregistered 2–4 px, tone jumping slightly at seams even in large bands; dye bleeds along the weave, fills unevenly. Mud-resist crackle only inside indigo, fine and sparse. Carved, rough outlines about 1/300 of frame width. Flat frieze in a paisley and vine border; waves as scallop rows; profile figures with miniature-painting faces, kohl-lined eyes. Camera: pan along the frieze, push in; cut on a turning motif. Avoid: vector edges, perfect registration, faceless figures, dense crackle, indigo flooding the frame.

拉贾斯坦手工木刻印花布（Bagru、Sanganer）。本色棉布 #EEE5CF 是主底，占画面一半以上，也是皮肤色；靛蓝 #2A3A68 给水面或天空色带和纹样；茜草红 #AA322A 给边框、衣服和主角；姜黄 #DEA42C 给小纹样；黑色雕版 #281E1C 印轮廓。按雕版一块块盖（每块约画面高的 1/8），错位二到四像素，大色带也在版缝处轻微跳变；染料顺经纬微渗，上色不匀。泥防染冰裂纹只在靛蓝里，细而稀疏。轮廓像刻出来的，略毛糙，粗约画面宽的 1/300。平铺成横幅，外框是佩斯利和花藤；海浪是扇贝纹；人物取侧面，细密画式的脸，描黑的眼尾。镜头沿横幅横摇、推近；借转动的纹样转场。避免：矢量硬边、套色完全对齐、没有五官的人、满屏冰裂纹、靛蓝铺满画面。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. crowd0.8s

    沿海边的队伍横摇：穿白土布的志愿者、穿靛蓝和茜草红纱丽的妇女、自治旗；Clawd 在队伍最前面入画。

  2. salt1.1s

    Medium close-up, a slow push: Clawd as Gandhi in round spectacles and a khadi shawl stoops, scoops a handful of salt, raises it glinting, then turns to the marchers and lifts it higher.

    中近景，镜头慢推：戴圆框眼镜、披土布披巾的 Clawd 弯腰捧起一把盐，举起来盐粒闪光，再转向队伍举得更高。

  3. wheel0.6s

    A village woman spinning at her charkha; the camera pushes in on the turning wheel for the cut to the next film.

    村妇坐在纺车前纺线；镜头推向转动的纺车轮，接下一部。

Clawd plays Gandhi: round wire spectacles, the edge of a khadi shawl, a bamboo staff, a handful of salt. The scene runs at 1.1× with no slow motion. A hard cut from the march to the gesture: stoop, scoop, raise, then turn to the marchers and lift it higher. The charkha's turning wheel ends the film as a spin transition.Clawd 扮甘地：圆框钢丝眼镜、土布披巾的边、竹杖、一把盐。全程 1.1 倍速，不做慢放。从队伍硬切到动作：弯腰、捧盐、举起，再转向队伍举得更高。最后用纺车转动的轮子做转场收尾。

2DFamily Album Polaroids家庭相册宝丽来

An open family album of faded Polaroids and Kodacolor snapshots, with teal-lifted blacks, creamy highlights and white borders, where one Polaroid comes alive as a panning shot of the beach drive. Terms of Endearment spans thirty years of a mother and daughter.摊开的家庭相册，里面是褪色的宝丽来和柯达快照：发青的黑、奶油色高光、白边。其中一张宝丽来活过来，变成海滩飞车的追拍照片。《母女情深》讲的是母女三十年。

Style prompt风格提示词

1970s–80s snapshots in an open album. Faded dyes: blacks lifted to teal grey about #2F3A3A, creamy rolled-off highlights, mild warm cast, slight overexposure, softness, fine grain (±4%), saturation about 60% of normal. White Polaroid borders, photo-booth strips (four frames stacked), ballpoint captions in the user's language. The album fills the frame, cream pages seen from above in soft, even light, a faint plastic sheen. In a photo, a moving subject gets a panning-shutter look (background smeared sideways, subject sharp); still subjects get shallow depth of field. People look photographed: real proportions, soft shading, slight blur, small faces. Camera: an overhead push into one photo until it fills the frame (about 3 s), a cut into the living scene as colour returns, then a pull-back. References: Polaroid SX-70, Kodacolor prints. Avoid: flat cartoon shapes, crisp modern colour, light shafts, saturated prints.

70 到 80 年代的生活快照，摊开在相册里。照片已褪色：黑色抬成偏青的灰（约 #2F3A3A），高光柔和发奶油色，略暖、略过曝，偏软，细颗粒（±4%），饱和度约正常的六成。宝丽来白边、照相馆四连拍条、圆珠笔手写说明（用用户的语言）。相册铺满画面，奶油色页面，从正上方看，光柔和均匀，淡淡塑料膜反光。照片里有东西在动就用追拍：背景横向拖糊，主体清晰；静止主体用浅景深。人物像拍的不像画的：比例真实、明暗柔和、轻微虚焦、脸不大。镜头：俯拍推进一张照片直到铺满（约三秒），切进活起来的场景、颜色恢复，再拉出。对标宝丽来 SX-70 和柯达彩色照片。避免：平面卡通造型、干净的现代色彩、光束、饱和的照片。

Amadeus莫扎特传

2DRococo Silhouettes in Gilt Candlelight洛可可剪影 · 烛光金色歌剧院

Black paper profiles with gold-bronzed rims fill a horseshoe of gilt boxes and red velvet facing a starry stage. Amadeus peaks with Mozart's opening night, so one frame holds the pit, the stage, the audience and Salieri brooding in his box.黑纸剪成的侧面人像镶着金边，坐满马蹄形的金色包厢和红丝绒，对着星空布景的舞台。《莫扎特传》的高光是首演之夜，一个画面装下乐池、舞台、观众和暗处的萨列里。

Style prompt风格提示词

18th-century cut-paper silhouettes. Each figure: a solid black profile #0B0705 with its own cut detail (hair, nose, lace holes, props), a 2–3 px pale-gold rim #F7E3A0 (at 1920×1080) toward the nearest flame, and hairline gold lines along clothing folds. Gilt relief #B88A2E and red velvet #6E0F14 frame the scene like an opera box; an outdoor subject becomes a lit painted backdrop on a stage, cut-paper figures in front. Reds and golds fill most of the frame; night blue #0C1A3A only for the far sky. Only the hero gets a painted, coloured face. Candles flicker gently (±5%), warm halos about five flames wide; light haze, vignette, fine grain. Signature shot: silhouettes framing a bright stage far back; slow lateral glide, then push. References: silhouettists Edouart, Miers; cinematographer Miroslav Ondříček (*Amadeus*, 1984). Avoid: plain black blobs; copy-pasted profiles; blue walls; strobing flames.

18 世纪剪纸剪影，放在烛光照亮的洛可可室内。每个人物是实心黑侧影 #0B0705，各有自己的剪纸细节（发型、鼻子、蕾丝镂空、物件），朝最近烛火的一侧描 2–3px（按 1920×1080）亮金边 #F7E3A0，顺衣褶勾几根细金线。金色浮雕 #B88A2E 和红丝绒 #6E0F14 像包厢一样框住画面；户外题材画成舞台上被照亮的布景，剪纸人物站在布景前。红与金占画面大半，夜蓝 #0C1A3A 只用于远处天空。只有主角侧影里的脸是画出来、有颜色的。烛火轻微跳动（亮度±5%），暖光晕约五个火苗宽，薄烟、暗角、细颗粒。招牌镜头：前景剪影框住远处亮着的舞台，慢横移再推进。对标剪影 Edouart、Miers，摄影师 Miroslav Ondříček（《莫扎特传》，1984）。避免：没有剪纸细节的黑块；复制粘贴的侧影；蓝色墙面；频闪的烛火。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  2. box0.9s

Clawd plays Mozart: a powdered wig with a pink-ribboned queue and a lace jabot, the baton beating time with the music.   • three.js real-time 3Dthree.js 实时 3D
  • David Watkin aerial photographyDavid Watkin 的航拍
  • Warm filmic grade暖调胶片调色
  • Instanced flamingo flocks实例化的火烈鸟群

Real-time 3D flight over a soda lake: a chrome-yellow Gipsy Moth, thousands of flamingos lifting off, hazy amber light. The film's best-remembered sequence is Denys flying Karen over Kenya, down to the hand she reaches back across the cockpits.实时 3D 的低空飞行：铬黄色双翼机掠过苏打湖，上万只火烈鸟惊起，琥珀色薄雾光。这部片最被记住的就是丹尼斯带凯伦飞越肯尼亚，和她向后座伸出的那只手。

Style prompt风格提示词

Real-time 3D like 1980s widescreen location photography: true-scale models with fine detail (e.g. struts, wires, rivets); smooth terrain, not low-poly. Default light: late-afternoon sun low behind the hero as seen from camera, pale cream sky, amber haze, distant ridges in 3–4 paler layers, ochre ground with long soft shadows. Pale overall: about 70% of the frame in light mid-tones, shadows warm grey, not deep brown; under a fifth near-white. Grade: filmic curve, low saturation, light grain, soft vignette. Thousands of instanced birds or animals (e.g. pink #F3B3AE with crimson #DF3F55) lift or scatter in staggered waves as the hero passes. Signature shot: over the moving hero's shoulder across open land; then a high chase 10–20 m up and one shot into the sun. Reference: cinematographer David Watkin (*Out of Africa*, 1985). Avoid: cold clean CG daylight; dark sepia; lockstep flocks.

实时 3D，像 80 年代宽银幕实景摄影：模型按真实比例、细节到位（如支柱、拉线、铆钉）；地形平滑，不要低多边形碎面。默认光：从镜头看，低斜的太阳在主角身后（逆光），奶油色淡天空，琥珀薄雾，远山分三四层渐淡，赭黄大地上长长的柔影。整体明亮：七成画面在中高调，阴影暖灰不深褐；近白不超过五分之一。胶片曲线、低饱和、细颗粒、轻暗角。上千只实例化的鸟或动物（如粉 #F3B3AE 配深红 #DF3F55），主角经过时一波波错开起飞或散开。招牌镜头：越过移动中主角的肩膀看远景；其次是离地 10–20 米的追拍和一个对着太阳的逆光镜头。对标摄影师 David Watkin（《走出非洲》，1985）。避免：冷而干净的 CG 日光；发暗的棕褐调；整齐划一的鸟群。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. flamingos0.8s

    Low chase just above the lake: the yellow biplane skims overhead with Clawd small in the front cockpit, leaning out, as flamingos lift off the water across the lens.

    贴着湖面仰拍追机：黄色双翼机从头顶掠过，Clawd 小小地坐在前座探身往外看，火烈鸟从水面腾起掠过镜头。

  2. backlit0.7s

    Hard cut to a wide shot straight into the sun: the plane comes head-on over the lake between two rising pink flocks.

    硬切到正对太阳的逆光全景：飞机迎面飞来，两侧是正在升起的粉色鸟群。

  3. cockpit1.0s

    On board, from the rear seat: Clawd in a leather flying cap, brass goggles and a streaming white scarf turns round and reaches back, and Denys's gloved hand takes it.

    从后座往前的机上镜头：戴皮飞行帽、黄铜护目镜、白丝巾在风里飘的 Clawd 转身向后伸手，丹尼斯戴手套的手握住它。

Clawd sits in Karen's front seat: leather cap, brass goggles, white silk scarf. Two exterior hard cuts sell speed and scale over the flamingo lake; the cockpit shot then holds for the film's gesture, Clawd turning to reach back for Denys on the last beats. The flight clock runs 1.5–1.6× screen time.Clawd 坐在凯伦的前座：皮飞行帽、黄铜护目镜、白丝巾。两个外景镜头硬切，交代火烈鸟湖上的速度和空间；最后的座舱镜头留给片中那个动作：Clawd 转身向后伸手，丹尼斯握住，落在最后几拍。飞行时间是屏幕时间的 1.5–1.6 倍。

References: 对标：Sydney Pollack, Out of Africa (1985) · David Watkin (cinematography) · Out of Africa (1985) poster · de Havilland DH.60G Gipsy Moth G-ABAK

Platoon野战排

2DCombat Artist Field Sketch · Pen & Wash越战随军速写 · 钢笔淡彩

  • Fast pen line快速钢笔线
  • Transparent wash透明淡彩
  • Sketchbook paper速写本纸
  • Vietnam Combat Artists Program美国陆军越战战地艺术计划

Quick, hard pen lines with loose washes of olive, ochre, smoke grey and fire orange on sketchbook paper left bare at the edges. It reads as a war artist drawing on the spot, close to the film's infantryman's view of Vietnam.快而有力的钢笔线，橄榄绿、土黄、烟灰和火光橙的淡彩，速写本纸四边留白。像随军画家在现场画下的，贴近这部片从步兵眼里看越战的视角。

Style prompt风格提示词

Reportage pen-and-wash field sketch. Pen: warm black, fast confident lines ~1/600 of frame width, flicked ends, overshooting corners; quick hatching and zigzags for shadow, on figures and ground. Bold transparent washes with soft wet edges, blooms and uneven pigment (not flat polygons) cover about 70% of the frame, shifted 2–4 px (at 1920×1080) off the line. Colours: olive #70783E, ochre #CEA054, smoke grey #706C70, one hot accent #F0822E for sky and light sources; skin in diluted ochre; sketchbook paper #F2ECDE. Bare paper only in highlights, a thin edge band and brush gaps; light grain. A scrawled pencil note (place, time, weather) in a corner, 1/40 of frame height, in the user's language. Layer for lateral tracks; hard cuts like turning a page. Reference: US Army Vietnam Combat Artists Program (1966–70). Avoid: polished finish; empty page; muddy washes; heavy noise.

现场报道式钢笔淡彩速写。钢笔用暖黑，线宽约画面宽度的 1/600，快而肯定，收笔甩出，转角冲出头；暗部用快速排线和折线，人物和地面都排线。淡彩透明而大胆：边缘湿软、有回流花、深浅不匀（不要硬边多边形），铺满画面约七成，整体错开线稿 2–4px（按 1920×1080）。颜色：橄榄绿 #70783E、土黄 #CEA054、烟灰 #706C70，加一个热烈点缀色 #F0822E 给天空和光源；肤色用淡土黄；暖米色速写本纸 #F2ECDE。只有最亮处、四边窄条和笔刷漏掉处留纸，纸纹很轻。角落用用户的语言潦草写一句铅笔速记（地点、时间、天气），字高约画面 1/40。分层便于横移；换视角硬切，像翻页。对标美国陆军越战战地艺术计划（1966–70）。避免：插画式精修；空着的纸；发脏的淡彩；重噪点。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. jungle0.8s

    Lateral track along a patrol pushing through elephant grass; Clawd, in a boonie hat and dog tags, stands among them as the soldiers file past.

    横移跟着在象草里前进的巡逻队；戴丛林帽、挂狗牌的 Clawd 站在中间，士兵从它身边走过。

  2. heli0.8s

    Hard cut to the view down from the Huey's door: the burning landing zone, rotor-flattened grass and the helicopter's own shadow, Clawd tiny in the clearing; a slow pull-out.

    硬切到从休伊直升机舱门往下看：燃烧的着陆区、被旋翼压平的草和直升机自己的影子，Clawd 小小地站在空地上；镜头缓慢拉远。

  3. still0.9s

    Hard cut close on Clawd, pulling back to the poster framing: arms flung up in a V under a napalm-orange sky, soldiers running past on both sides as the helicopter lifts away.

    硬切到 Clawd 的近景，拉回海报构图：它在凝固汽油弹的橙色火光下双臂张成 V 字，两侧士兵跑过，直升机拉起离开。

Clawd plays Elias: a boonie hat and dog tags. Two hard cuts go from the ground to the air and back, each shot like a different sketchbook page; right after the last cut the arms fling up into the poster pose and sag a little as the helicopter climbs out. The scene runs at 1.1×.Clawd 扮伊莱亚斯：丛林帽、狗牌。两个硬切从地面到空中再回到地面，每个镜头像速写本里的另一页；切到最后一个镜头，双臂随即猛地张成海报姿势，直升机拉起时手臂微微垂下。场景以 1.1 倍速播放。

References: 对标：Oliver Stone, Platoon (1986) · Platoon (1986) poster · US Army Vietnam Combat Artists Program (1966–70)

No.601987

The Last Emperor末代皇帝

2DGongbi Court Painting on Silk工笔重彩 · 绢本宫廷画

  • Gongbi zhongcai工笔重彩
  • Ruled-line architecture (jiehua)界画
  • Qing court scrolls清代宫廷画卷
  • Storaro's colourStoraro 的色彩

Iron-wire outlines and graded mineral washes of azurite, malachite, cinnabar and gold on silk, with a ruled-line palace and thousands of kneeling officials. The boy emperor's coronation is a Qing court ceremony, painted the way Qing court painters recorded ceremonies.绢本上的铁线描和层层渲染的石青、石绿、朱砂、泥金，界画的宫殿，几千名跪拜的百官。小皇帝的登基是清宫仪典，就用清宫画师记录仪典的画法来画。

Style prompt风格提示词

Chinese gongbi zhongcai (fine-line, heavy-colour court painting) on aged silk. Far to near, each form: flat base, a wash darkening its lower, overlapped edges, an even iron-wire outline (dark brown, ~1/900 frame width), gold and white lifts. Last, a 30% warm ochre glaze over everything, lines still clear. Minerals: cinnabar #C23A22, azurite #2C5A9A, malachite #3A8A6A, gamboge #E8B830, gold #CAA04A, muted by the glaze; ground and sky mid-value warm silk ochre, never near-white, saturated blue or orange; time of day shows only as overall warmth. No cast shadows or glare. Fine-line water ripples. Ruled-line architecture, every tile drawn; crowds all differ; a lone figure works. Faint silk weave (±3%), brown vignette. Symmetrical one-point composition: a large figure before a deep, ordered space. Slow pushes. Reference: Qing court scrolls, e.g. *Kangxi's Southern Inspection Tour*. Avoid: copy-pasted figures; unwashed flat fills; loose ink wash; poster saturation.

绢本工笔重彩（清代宫廷画法）。由远到近画，每个形：平涂底色 → 分染（沿下缘和被遮处加深）→ 均匀铁线描（深褐，线宽约画面 1/900）→ 提金、提白；最后全画面叠三成浓度的暖赭罩染，线仍清楚。矿物色：朱砂 #C23A22、石青 #2C5A9A、石绿 #3A8A6A、藤黄 #E8B830、泥金 #CAA04A，被罩染压稳；地面和天空是中等明度的暖赭绢色，不要近白，不画饱和蓝天和橙色落日，时段变化只靠整体冷暖。不画投影和眩光。水用细线水纹。建筑用界画，瓦片一片片画出；有人群时人人不同，单人也行。绢纹淡（±3%），褐色暗角。一点透视、左右对称，大人物在前，身后深远规整。缓推。对标清代宫廷长卷，如《康熙南巡图》。避免：复制粘贴人物；不分染的平涂；写意水墨；海报式饱和色。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. curtain1.0s

    The yellow dragon-roundel silk fills the frame and parts, and Clawd steps out onto the terrace in a Qing court hat with a red-fringed crown; a slight pull-back.

    绣着团龙的黄色绸帘占满画面，帘子分开，戴红缨朝冠的 Clawd 走上丹陛；镜头微微后拉。

  2. officials0.6s

    Hard cut to a low lateral track across the kneeling court, rows of officials in rank badges bowing in a wave.

    硬切到低机位横移，扫过跪着的百官，一排排穿补服的官员波浪般叩首。

  3. still0.9s

    Hard cut to the poster composition, easing wider: Clawd large on the terrace, the square of kneeling officials and the gold double roof behind, the cricket's gourd in the foreground.

    硬切到海报构图并慢慢拉宽：Clawd 大大地站在丹陛上，身后是跪满百官的广场和金色重檐，前景是蝈蝈罐。

Clawd plays the child Puyi: a Qing court hat with a red silk fringe, court beads, horse-hoof cuffs. The curtain parts and Clawd walks out in step with the beat; a hard cut to the kowtow wave, then the wide poster frame holds the last beats. The scene runs at 1.1×.Clawd 扮小溥仪：红缨朝冠、朝珠、马蹄袖。帘幕分开，Clawd 踩着节拍走出来；硬切到百官叩首的波浪，最后几拍落在海报式的全景上。场景以 1.1 倍速播放。

References: 对标：Bernardo Bertolucci, The Last Emperor (1987) · Vittorio Storaro (cinematography) · The Last Emperor (1987) poster · Kangxi Southern Inspection Tour scrolls · Qianlong's Grand Review

Rain Man雨人

2D80s Airbrush + Raymond's Count80 年代喷枪插画 + 雷蒙的计数

  • 80s airbrush illustration80 年代喷枪插画
  • Patrick Nagel figuresPatrick Nagel 式人物
  • Hajime Sorayama chrome空山基式镀铬
  • Technical-drawing callouts工程图标注

Smooth airbrushed gradients, hard masked edges, chrome glints and pink-and-mint neon, overlaid with thin cyan callouts that count. It pairs the film's 1980s look with what Raymond sees: 82, 82, 82, 246 toothpicks, then cards counted at the table.平滑的喷枪渐变、硬边遮罩、镀铬高光和粉绿霓虹，叠一层细细的青色计数标注。80 年代画风配上雷蒙眼里的世界：82、82、82，共 246 根牙签，还有牌桌算牌。

Style prompt风格提示词

1980s airbrush illustration with a drafting overlay. Every shape: a hard-edged mask filled with a smooth sprayed gradient, 1–2 px overspray rim (at 1920×1080). Bright, high-key: mint #79BDA6 walls or sky, cream, neon pink #F58CB5 and magenta #C9467F accents, black-and-white checker floor or ground; purple and night under 20%; at other times of day keep mint and cream; pink carries any sunset. Figures à la Patrick Nagel (flat pale skin, few crisp lines, dark hair masses, pink and mint rim light), keeping their own age and dress. Metal and glass in Hajime Sorayama chrome: banded sky reflection, hard horizon line, cross-star glints. Overlay: thin cyan callouts #56E6FF, near-white text #D9FBFF (1/40 frame height), dotted leaders, dashed rings, dimension lines; monospace numbers count whatever the story counts, Rain Man style, as each group is circled. Slow push/pull. Avoid: synthwave suns; UI boxes; soft edges.

80 年代喷枪插画，叠一层工程制图标注。每个形是硬边遮罩里喷出的平滑渐变，边缘 1–2px（按 1920×1080）细飞沫。明亮高调：薄荷绿 #79BDA6 的墙或天空、奶油白，霓虹粉 #F58CB5 和品红 #C9467F 点缀，地面铺黑白棋盘格；紫色和夜色不超过两成，其他时段仍是薄荷绿奶油底，晚霞用粉色画。人物学 Patrick Nagel：苍白平整的皮肤、少而利落的线、成块深色头发，粉与薄荷绿轮廓光，但保留本人的年龄衣着。金属玻璃学空山基镀铬：天空色带、硬地平线、十字星光。最上层是细青色标注 #56E6FF、白字 #D9FBFF（字高约画面 1/40）：圆点引线、虚线圈组、尺寸线；像《雨人》的雷蒙，等宽数字数故事里要数的东西，圈一组滚一次。慢推或慢拉。避免：合成波式的条纹太阳；UI 框；发糊的边。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  2. raymond0.8s
