No.621989

Driving Miss Daisy为黛西小姐开车

2DRockwell-style Oil Illustration洛克威尔式油画插画

  • Norman Rockwell oil illustration诺曼·洛克威尔油画插画
  • Saturday Evening Post covers《星期六晚邮报》封面
  • 3D-modelled car repainted in oil strokes3D 建模的车再铺油画笔触

Solid, warm Rockwell realism: clean edges, visible oil strokes, afternoon light through oaks, every neighbour busy with something. The film is two people in one car on a 1950s Atlanta street, which is a Saturday Evening Post cover already.扎实温暖的洛克威尔写实：边缘干净、笔触可见，午后阳光穿过橡树，邻居各忙各的。这部片就是 50 年代亚特兰大一辆车里的两个人，本身就像一张《星期六晚邮报》封面。

Style prompt风格提示词

Norman Rockwell-style mid-century magazine-cover oil illustration. Near-realistic figures: head about 1/7 of body height, faces and hands modelled from 3–4 value planes, not outlines; readable expressions. Smooth modelling; faint strokes about 1/150 of frame width, following form, each within ±5% of the colour beneath. Default light: warm afternoon sun from the upper left, clear cast and dappled shadows in cool blue-grey; other light keeps a warm key and cool shadows. Palette: cream sky #E6DCC2, brick red #9A4A38, leaf greens, bottle green #1F3A24, soft skin. Gloss reflects surroundings. Background storytelling: busy bystanders where there are people, telling objects when the hero is alone. Light canvas texture, warm varnish, soft vignette. Camera: deep three-quarter wide, slow push, closer cuts ending on a hand or face. Reference: Rockwell's *Saturday Evening Post* covers (1940s–50s). Avoid: cartoon faces; texture filters; flat digital shading; empty backgrounds.

洛克威尔式 50 年代美国杂志封面油画。人物接近写实：头约占身高 1/7，脸和手用三四块明暗面塑出、不靠描线，表情读得出。塑形平滑，笔触隐约：宽约画面 1/150，顺形体走，每笔与底色只差±5%。默认光：左上方暖色午后阳光，地上有清楚的投影和树影，阴影冷蓝灰；别的光也保持暖主光冷阴影。中等饱和：奶油天空 #E6DCC2、砖红 #9A4A38、叶绿、瓶绿 #1F3A24、柔和肤色。光面映出环境。背景有小故事：有人处路人各忙各的，主角独处就靠物件。轻画布纹、暖光油、柔和暗角。镜头：有纵深的四分之三全景缓推，越切越近，落在手或脸上。对标洛克威尔 1940–50 年代《星期六晚邮报》封面。避免：卡通脸；重纹理滤镜；无笔触的数码平涂；空背景。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. still0.9s

    Three-quarter front view of the dark-green 1949 Hudson rolling down an oak-shaded street of brick and white-porch houses; Clawd at the wheel in a chauffeur's cap, Miss Daisy stern in the back window; a slow push-in.

    墨绿色 1949 年哈德逊的四分之三前侧，车沿着橡树成荫、红砖房和白门廊的街道慢慢开来；戴司机帽的 Clawd 握着方向盘，黛西小姐板着脸坐在后窗里；缓慢推进。

  2. inside0.8s

    Hard cut to the back seat, locked off: Miss Daisy's profile large in the foreground, Clawd driving seen from behind, the street flowing past the windows.

    硬切到后座往前拍的定机位：前景是黛西小姐的大侧脸，后面是开车的 Clawd 的背影，街景从车窗外流过。

  3. close0.8s

    Hard cut to the rear-view mirror, the poster's framing: Clawd's eyes under the cap brim on the left, Miss Daisy on the right easing into a small smile; a slight push.

    硬切到后视镜特写，也就是海报构图：左边是帽檐下 Clawd 的眼睛，右边是黛西小姐，慢慢露出一点笑；镜头轻推。

Clawd plays Hoke in a navy chauffeur's cap. Three hard cuts move from outside the car to inside to the mirror, tighter each time, and the payoff is saved for the end: Miss Daisy's frown easing into a smile in the mirror on the last beats. Scene time runs about 1.8× screen time, yet the drive stays unhurried.Clawd 扮霍克，戴藏青色司机帽。三个硬切从车外到车里再到后视镜，一个比一个近；结果留到最后：镜子里黛西小姐的板脸在最后几拍慢慢松成微笑。场景时间平均是屏幕时间的 1.8 倍左右，车仍然开得从容。

References: 对标：Bruce Beresford, Driving Miss Daisy (1989) · Driving Miss Daisy (1989) poster · Norman Rockwell, Saturday Evening Post covers

No.631990

Dances with Wolves与狼共舞

2DField Journal · Pencil & Watercolour邓巴日记 · 铅笔 + 透明水彩

  • Graphite under-drawing铅笔底稿
  • Transparent watercolour透明水彩
  • Iron-gall ink handwriting铁胆墨水手写
  • Bodmer and Catlin field sketchesBodmer、Catlin 的野外水彩

A soldier's field journal: pressure-varied pencil, transparent watercolour, yellowed rag paper, inked script and pencil notes in the margins. Dunbar writes and sketches in his diary throughout the film, so the prairie appears as his sketch coming to life.一个军人的野外日记：轻重有变化的铅笔线、透明水彩、泛黄的布浆纸、墨水手写和页边的铅笔批注。邓巴在片中一直写画日记，所以草原是从他的速写里活过来的。

Style prompt风格提示词

A 19th-century field journal come to life: full-colour watercolour over visible pencil. Pencil under-drawing #3A363A, pressure-varied and broken, lightly hatched in shadow, readable through the washes. Brushed washes with soft wet edges, blooms, pooled rims and granulation (not flat polygons) cover about 80% of the frame in rich warm colour. Default magic hour: dusty-rose sky #C88F76 turning lavender above, streaky rose clouds, low sun, ochre-gold land #DEBE87, violet distance, long shadows, opaque pale-gold lifts on sun-facing edges. Yellowed rag paper #F0E4C8, faint stains and foxing, showing through. Iron-gall ink #34221C for small labels and arrows. Signature move, once at the opening (about 2 s): bare pencil, then colour arrives wash by wash, far to near, during a push-in; after that, full colour. References: Bodmer and Catlin field sketches. Avoid: grey, muddy or pale washes; uniform digital outlines; later pencil-only frames.

一本活过来的 19 世纪野外日记：看得见铅笔线的满幅透明水彩。铅笔底稿 #3A363A 有轻重和断笔，暗部轻排线，透过颜色仍读得出。水彩边缘湿软，有回流花、积色深边和颗粒（不要平涂多边形），铺满约八成画面，浓郁偏暖。默认魔幻时刻：灰玫瑰色天空 #C88F76 往上转淡紫，玫瑰色条云，低太阳，赭金大地 #DEBE87，远处紫，长投影；朝太阳的边缘点几笔不透明浅金。泛黄布浆纸 #F0E4C8，淡水渍霉斑，纸色透出来。铁胆墨水 #34221C 写小字草书标注和箭头。招牌动作只在开头约两秒：先只有铅笔，镜头推进时颜色从远到近一块块铺上；之后每帧满彩。对标 Karl Bodmer、George Catlin 的野外速写。避免：灰脏或发白的淡彩；均匀数码描边；开头后仍只有铅笔。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. page1.2s

    Push in on the journal's right page: Clawd's self-portrait and the wolf Two Socks start as bare pencil and are coloured in stroke by stroke, sky first, Clawd last.

    推近日记的右页：Clawd 的自画像和狼「双袜」一开始只是铅笔底稿，水彩一笔笔铺上来，先是天空，最后才是 Clawd。

  2. prairie1.3s

    Cut to the sketch come alive in full colour at sunset: one push from the plain in to Clawd and Two Socks facing each other, the buffalo herd running behind; Clawd tips its hat.

    切到活成满幅彩色的日落草原：一个推镜从平原推到对望的 Clawd 和双袜，身后野牛群奔过；Clawd 抬帽致意。

Clawd plays Dunbar: a black cavalry officer's hat with gold cord, the journal in one hand and a pencil in the other. The push and the colouring-in are one gesture; the cut to the living prairie comes mid-phrase, and the hat tip lands on the last beats. 1:1 time.Clawd 扮邓巴：带金色帽绳的黑色骑兵宽檐帽，一手日记本、一手铅笔。推近页面和上色是同一个动作；切到活过来的草原落在乐句中间，抬帽致意落在最后几拍。1:1 时间。

References: 对标：Kevin Costner, Dances with Wolves (1990) · Dean Semler (cinematography) · Dances with Wolves (1990) poster · Karl Bodmer · George Catlin

No.641991

The Silence of the Lambs沉默的羔羊

2DHand-coloured Natural-history Copperplate手工上色的博物学铜版画

  • Copperplate engraving铜版雕刻排线
  • Hand-colouring手工上色
  • Victorian entomology plates维多利亚昆虫学图版
  • Moses Harris, The AurelianMoses Harris《The Aurelian》

Fine engraved hatching and stipple under thin hand-coloured tints, with plate numbers and Latin labels on yellowed paper. The poster's death's-head moth belongs on a specimen plate, and the same cold engraving carries on into Lecter's cell.细密的铜版排线和点刻，上面薄薄地手工上色，图版编号和拉丁文标签印在泛黄的纸上。海报上的鬼脸天蛾本就该钉在标本图版上，同样冷峻的铜版画法一直延伸到莱克特的囚室。

Style prompt风格提示词

A hand-coloured natural-history copperplate. Every object in engraved warm black-brown line: dense parallel and cross-hatching (spacing about 1/400 of frame width, thicker in darks), stipple for soft tones, hatched shadows beneath; sky bare or sparsely lined. Flat watercolour tints inside the line, medium saturation, clearly coloured: paper #EFE3C4, umber #5A3A22, rust #B4431E, yellow #E6AD24, grey-violet #7D6F8E. Plate: the subject as Fig. 1 in the centre, about 60% of plate height, 3–6 numbered part or object figures around it, each about 1/5 high; spaced small-caps title, numbered legend, bevelled plate mark, light foxing. Even daylight; time of day only shifts the tints' warmth; dark interiors go cold, hard side light. Camera: a still plate where one thing moves (insect, leaf, hand), then a slow push to a key detail. Reference: Moses Harris, *The Aurelian* (1766). Avoid: smooth gradients; garish colour; a tiny vignetted subject.

手工上色的博物学铜版画。所有物体用暖黑褐刻线画：密集平行线和交叉排线（线距约画面宽度 1/400，暗处线更粗），柔和过渡用点刻，物下有排线投影；天空留纸色或只几道稀疏横线。线内平涂水彩，中等饱和、颜色认得出：纸 #EFE3C4、赭褐 #5A3A22、铁锈红 #B4431E、黄 #E6AD24、灰紫 #7D6F8E。图版：主体作图 1 居中，约占版面高六成，四周 3–6 个编号小图（局部或相关物件），各约五分之一高；上方字距拉开的小型大写标题，下方编号图注，压印斜边版框，淡霉斑。默认均匀日光，时段变化只调着色冷暖；暗室内转冷，侧面硬光。镜头：静止图版里只有一样东西在动（虫、叶、手），再匀速慢推到关键细节。对标 Moses Harris《The Aurelian》（1766）。避免：平滑渐变；刺眼的颜色；主体缩成小晕影。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. plate0.9s

    The full entomology plate 'Acherontia styx': a live death's-head moth flies off the nightshade and settles on Clawd's head; a slight push-in.

    整张昆虫学图版「Acherontia styx」：一只活的鬼脸天蛾从茄科植物上飞起，落在 Clawd 头顶；镜头轻推。

  2. cell1.6s

    Hard cut to the basement: Clawd as Clarice, FBI trainee ID clipped on, stands at the glass; then one continuous push right and in until Lecter's face fills the frame and the thin smile appears.

    硬切到地下监区：别着 FBI 学员证的 Clawd（克拉丽丝）站在玻璃前；接着一个连续的推镜向右、向里推，直到莱克特的脸占满画面，嘴角露出一丝笑。

Clawd is 'Fig. 4' on the plate, then Clarice with an FBI trainee ID. The moth lands within the opening beats; a hard cut to the cell, and the push to Lecter runs as one move with no cut, his smile arriving at the very end. Scene time runs about 1.8× screen time.Clawd 先是图版上的「图 4」，再扮别着 FBI 学员证的克拉丽丝。飞蛾在开头几拍落下；硬切到囚室，推向莱克特的镜头一气呵成、中间不切，他的笑留到最后才出来。场景时间约为屏幕时间的 1.8 倍。

References: 对标：Jonathan Demme, The Silence of the Lambs (1991) · Tak Fujimoto (cinematography) · The Silence of the Lambs (1991) poster · Moses Harris, The Aurelian

No.651992

Unforgiven不可饶恕

2DRemington Western Oil · Sunset & Nocturne雷明顿西部油画 · 晚霞与雨夜

  • Remington's warm plains雷明顿的暖色平原
  • Remington's late nocturnes雷明顿晚年的夜景
  • Thick oil impasto厚涂油彩
  • Opening-title silhouette开场字幕的剪影

Thick Western oil painting in two keys: Remington's red-gold plains at sunset and his blue-green nocturnes lit by warm windows. Unforgiven opens and closes on a silhouette at a grave against the sunset, and its showdown comes in a town on a rainy night.两种调子的厚涂西部油画：雷明顿金红晚霞下的平原，和他晚年被暖黄窗光点亮的蓝绿夜景。《不可饶恕》以夕阳下坟前的剪影开场和收尾，决战发生在雨夜的小镇。

Style prompt风格提示词

Frederic Remington-style Western oil painting. Primary register, for anything before full dark: sunset, a red-gold sky (orange into deep red) in long horizontal strokes, the sun on the horizon, land and figures dark warm silhouettes, thin warm rim sunward. Second register, only after dark: a blue-green nocturne (sky #17333A, moonlit edges #9CC4BC, warm window light #FFCC6A on dark ground #1E1C16, rain). Broad, readable shapes first, then loaded strokes about 1/80 of frame width wide, 3–6 times as long, soft broken ends, each within ±6% brightness of the colour beneath, following the surface. Light canvas texture. Low horizon, sky 50–60% of the frame, figures small to medium. Camera: one slow push from a silhouetted wide to the figure. References: Remington's late nocturnes; cinematographer Jack N. Green (*Unforgiven*, 1992). Avoid: smooth gradients; hard-edged flat bands; shattered shapes; grey figures in warm scenes; wide pans.

弗雷德里克·雷明顿式西部油画。主调是晚霞，天黑透之前都用：橙到深红的金红天空用长横笔触，太阳压在地平线上，大地和人物是暗暖剪影，向阳一侧一道细暖轮廓光。第二种调子只在天黑透后用：蓝绿夜景（天 #17333A，月光边 #9CC4BC），暖黄窗光 #FFCC6A 铺在深色地面 #1E1C16 上，加雨。先画清楚的大形，再上笔触：每笔宽约画面宽度 1/80、长为宽的三到六倍，颜料饱满、笔端松散；每笔与底色明暗只差±6%，顺着各个面走。画布纹要轻。地平线低，天空占五到六成，人物小到中等。镜头：从剪影大全景慢推到人物。对标雷明顿晚年夜景画、摄影师 Jack N. Green（《不可饶恕》，1992）。避免：平滑渐变；硬边色带；打碎形体的满屏笔触；暖场景人物发灰；大幅横摇。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. grave1.4s

    One push from the whole sunset (farmhouse, windmill, lone tree, the cross in front of the sun) in to Clawd as Munny at the grave; it lifts its head and brings the rifle up across its body.

    一个推镜从整片晚霞（木屋、风车、孤树、挡在太阳前的十字架）推到坟边的 Clawd（芒尼）；它抬起头，把步枪横到身前。

  2. town1.1s

    Hard cut to Big Whiskey on a rainy night: Clawd rides a dun horse down the muddy street past the lit windows of Greeley's saloon, the camera pushing toward its dripping hat brim.

    硬切到雨夜的大威士忌镇：Clawd 骑一匹兔褐马走过泥街，旁边是亮着窗的 Greeley's 酒馆，镜头推向它滴水的帽檐。

Clawd plays William Munny: a wide black hat, a long coat collar, a rifle. The opening silhouette and the grave are one move, the head lifting just as the push arrives; the only cut jumps to rain and night as Clawd rides into town. 1:1 time, no slow motion.Clawd 扮威廉·芒尼：黑色宽檐帽、长雨衣立领、一支步枪。开场剪影到坟前是一个镜头，推到位时它正好抬头；唯一的一刀硬切到雨夜，Clawd 骑马进镇。1:1 时间，不放慢。

References: 对标：Clint Eastwood, Unforgiven (1992) · Jack N. Green (cinematography) · Unforgiven (1992) poster (Bill Gold) · Frederic Remington, nocturnes

No.661993

Schindler's List辛德勒的名单

2DB&W黑白Black-and-white Film, One Red Coat黑白胶片 · 唯一的红大衣

  • High-contrast B&W film高反差黑白胶片
  • Coarse silver grain粗颗粒银盐
  • Selective colour局部留色
  • Janusz KamińskiJanusz Kamiński

Cold, high-contrast black and white with coarse grain and haloed highlights, where only the girl's red coat and Clawd's orange keep their colour. It follows Spielberg's own device: the red coat and the Sabbath candle flames are the film's only colour.冷灰、高反差的黑白，粗颗粒，高光晕开；只有小女孩的红大衣和 Clawd 的橙色有颜色。这正是斯皮尔伯格的手法：红大衣和安息日烛火是全片仅有的彩色。

Style prompt风格提示词

Black-and-white film look: cold neutral greys, a strong S-curve (blacks near #111, whites near #EEE), highlights haloing in haze, a soft old lens, silver grain re-rolled every frame (±6%), slight flicker, rare dust, vignette. Everything is grey except the one hero the story centres on, saturated, mainly clothing (e.g. terracotta #D97757); optionally one red story object (#A3241F–#C9463C). Lamps and fires stay grey, unless a flame turning from grey to warm is the story beat. Long-lens compression, soft foreground branches; in a crowd, no two people alike in build, tone or gait. Default: overcast mid-key, shadows open. Signature shot: high long-lens view of many small walking figures, the coloured one among them; without a crowd, the hero tiny in wide grey space. Reference: cinematographer Janusz Kamiński (*Schindler's List*, 1993). Avoid: sepia or blue toning; grain-free greys; more than two coloured things.

黑白电影质感：冷中性灰，强 S 曲线（黑接近 #111，白接近 #EEE），高光在雾里泛光晕，略软的老镜头，逐帧变化的银盐颗粒（±6%），轻微闪烁，偶有灰尘，暗角。全画面是灰的，只有故事围绕的那个主角是饱和色，以衣服为主（如陶土橙 #D97757）；可再有一个红色故事道具（#A3241F–#C9463C）。灯和火保持灰色，除非“火苗先灰后转暖”是剧情节点。长焦压缩空间，前景虚焦树枝；有人群时，体型、衣色和步态人人不同。默认阴天中间调，暗部不死黑。招牌镜头：长焦俯看许多走动的小人，带色的那个在其中；没有人群就让主角很小地落在大片灰里。对标摄影师 Janusz Kamiński（《辛德勒的名单》，1993）。避免：棕褐或偏蓝调色；无颗粒的干净灰；超过两样有颜色的东西。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. hill0.7s

    Clawd as Schindler on a dark horse on the hilltop, the ghetto street below packed with people being driven along it, one red dot among them; a slight push-in.

    山坡上骑着深色马的 Clawd（辛德勒），坡下的街道挤满被驱赶的人群，人群里有一个红点；镜头轻推。

  2. pov0.6s

    Hard cut to Schindler's point of view through a long lens, following the girl in the red coat as she walks alone through a gap in the crowd.

    硬切到辛德勒的长焦主观镜头，跟住独自走在人群空当里的红大衣小女孩。

  3. street0.6s

    Hard cut down to street level: a lateral track keeps pace with the girl as three rows of people walk left past the shopfronts, a blurred coat crossing the lens.

    硬切到街面：横移镜头和小女孩同速，三排人从店铺前往左走，一件虚焦的大衣从镜头前掠过。

  4. candles0.6s

    Hard cut to two Sabbath candles in the dark: the flames come up grey, then turn to colour; a slow push-in. Clawd is not in the shot.

    硬切到黑暗里的两支安息日蜡烛：火苗先亮成灰白，再变成彩色；缓慢推进。Clawd 不在画面里。

Clawd plays Schindler: a grey fedora, reins in hand, watching from the saddle. Four hard cuts follow his gaze: the hill, the long-lens POV, the street, and the candles as the closing image, each held about a beat and a half. Restrained throughout: people only walk, nothing violent is shown.Clawd 扮辛德勒：灰色软呢帽、手握缰绳，坐在马上往下看。四个硬切跟着他的目光走：山坡、长焦主观镜头、街面，最后收在蜡烛上，每个镜头约一拍半。全程克制：人只是在走，不出现任何暴力。

References: 对标：Steven Spielberg, Schindler's List (1993) · Janusz Kamiński (cinematography) · Schindler's List (1993) poster

No.671994

Forrest Gump阿甘正传

2.5DSabuda Pop-up Book on a Real Desk立体书 · 真实书桌上的纸艺

  • Robert Sabuda pop-up engineeringRobert Sabuda 立体书结构
  • Watercolour and coloured-pencil print水彩、彩铅印刷质感
  • three.js paper cardsthree.js 纸卡
  • Physical depth of field物理景深

Printed cards standing up in parallel folds, with white cores, grey board edges, layered shadows and shallow focus, on a walnut desk. Forrest tells his life on a bench, so each page that pops up is one chapter.平行折立起来的印刷卡片，白色纸芯、灰色纸板边、层与层之间的投影和浅景深，放在胡桃木书桌上。阿甘坐在长椅上把一生讲成故事，翻开一页就立起一段经历。

Style prompt风格提示词

A real pop-up book filmed on a desk. Every element: a flat die-cut card on parallel folds before a 90° backdrop page, printed with transparent watercolour and grainy coloured-pencil hatching; cut edges show a white core and grey board. Default light: bright window daylight from the upper left, soft shadows between layers. Print colours (sky blue, warm sand, greens) and room light stay daylight whatever the story's time of day; that time appears only in the print (e.g. a low sun, a night sky). Walnut desk #6B3E1E; the room a warm blur. Physical depth of field, focus on the hero's layer. Signature shot: a high three-quarter view of the whole spread. One continuous move: dive into a page, pull back, turn the page instead of cutting, push into the next spread. References: Robert Sabuda, Matthew Reinhart. Avoid: solid-looking cards; dark moody rooms; cartoon faces.

一本放在书桌上实拍的立体书。每个元素是平的模切卡片，靠平行折立在打开成 90° 的背景页前，印着透明水彩和带纸纹的彩铅排线，切口露出白纸芯和灰纸板边。默认光：左上方窗户进来的明亮日光，层间柔和投影。卡面（天蓝、暖沙、绿）和房间都保持日光，故事在任何时段都一样；时段只画在印刷画面里（如低太阳、夜空）。胡桃木书桌 #6B3E1E，后面的房间虚成暖色柔光。物理景深，焦点在主角那层。招牌镜头：高机位四分之三角度看整个跨页。一个连续镜头：钻进一页、拉远到整本书、翻页代替剪辑、再推进下一跨页。对标 Robert Sabuda、Matthew Reinhart 的立体书。避免：像实心模型的卡片；昏暗压抑的房间；卡通脸。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. one5.0s

    One continuous shot: the closed book on the desk opens and Savannah's square pops up; the camera drops in with a falling white feather to the bench, where Clawd offers the nurse a chocolate; it pulls back, the page turns, and it pushes in to US-163 in Monument Valley, where Clawd in a red cap runs in ahead of the bearded followers, stops and turns to them; it pulls back again, the page turns, and it pushes in to the shrimp boat Jenny in a lightning storm.

Twelve beats with no cuts: the feather carries the camera in, and two page turns, at about 2.7 s and 4.2 s, stand in for cuts. Braveheart勇敢的心

2DIlluminated Manuscript with Gold Leaf泥金手抄本 · 贴金细密画

  • Book of Kells interlace《凯尔经》编结纹
  • Gothic miniature (Morgan Bible)哥特细密画（摩根圣经）
  • Luttrell Psalter marginalia《鲁特雷尔诗篇》页边画
  • Burnished gold leaf磨光金箔

A medieval manuscript page: burnished gold with punched dots, lapis blue, vermilion, textura script, and a battle miniature framed like a window. Braveheart is a medieval Scottish legend, and its last cry, FREEDOM, becomes the illuminated initial.一页中世纪手抄本：带冲压点的磨光金箔、青金石蓝、朱红、哥特体字，中间一幅窗口似的战役细密画。《勇敢的心》是苏格兰传奇，片尾那声 FREEDOM 做成泥金首字母。

Style prompt风格提示词

An illuminated manuscript page (cropped, or an open spread) filling the frame. Vellum #ECDCB4, brown ink outlines #2A1A10, lapis #27469A, vermilion #C43A20, burnished gold leaf on raised gesso, with punched dot rosettes; a slow candle glint sweeps the gold only. Miniature backgrounds are mostly gold (night: lapis with gold stars); gold fills at least a tenth of the frame. Gothic figures in flat colour and ink outline. Celtic interlace, ivy borders, marginal grotesques; the story's text (Latin or the user's language) in broad-nib textura, red-and-blue line fillers, one large gilded initial. Layout: the miniature a window about half the page beside the text column, layered for parallax on pushes; figures may break out. Warm candle from upper left, brown vignette, pigment grain. References: Book of Kells, Morgan Bible, Luttrell Psalter. Avoid: flat yellow for gold; modern typefaces; glints on faces.

一页泥金装饰手抄本，取局部或双页填满画面。羊皮纸 #ECDCB4、褐色墨线 #2A1A10、青金石蓝 #27469A、朱红 #C43A20，加抛光金箔：石膏底隆起，有打孔小圆点花，一道烛光高光只在金上慢慢扫过。细密画背景多为金箔（夜景用青金石底加金星），金至少占画面一成。哥特式人物平涂加墨线。凯尔特编结纹、常春藤边框、页边怪诞小兽；正文写故事（拉丁文或用户的语言），宽头笔哥特体，红蓝行尾线，一个大金首字母。版式：细密画如一扇窗，约占半页，旁为文字栏；窗后分层，推进出视差；人物可破框。左上暖烛光，褐色暗角，淡颜料颗粒。对标《凯尔经》、摩根圣经、勒特雷尔诗篇。避免：平涂黄色代替金箔；现代字体；人脸上的高光。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

The English Patient英国病人

2DExplorer's Scrapbook Watercolour探险家剪贴本水彩

Watercolour sketches taped into an old book among maps, photos, notes and pressed flowers, in the poster's amber-sepia. Almásy's Herodotus, stuffed with his paintings and notes, frames the film, so the camera pushes through one painting into the Cave of Swimmers.贴在旧书里的水彩速写，夹着地图、照片、便条和压花，整体是海报的琥珀褐。艾玛殊那本塞满画和笔记的希罗多德是全片的框架，镜头就穿过一张水彩进入游泳者洞穴。

Style prompt风格提示词

A traveller's scrapbook, crowded edge to edge with 6–10 overlapping items, some running off frame: a taped watercolour (the main image, about 40% of frame), a hand-drawn map, a sepia photo, printed pages, handwritten notes, a pressed flower; each softly shadowed, with yellowed tape. The watercolour: wet-in-wet washes, warm ochre lights, hard-edged violet-rose shadows, pen contours, pooled edges, clear value contrast. Palette: amber paper #E8D6B0, sepia #6B4A2E, ochre #D08A3C, rose-violet #9C6A86, ink #2A1E16. Inside the painting: one warm source (torch, lamp) pooling in a dark foreground, bright sky beyond. Light amber grade on the page only, never muddying the painting; vignette about 20%, faint grain. Signature move: push into the painting until the page edges slide out. Reference: cinematographer John Seale (*The English Patient*, 1996). Avoid: smooth gradients; sparse tidy layouts; panning while a page frames the painting.

旅人的剪贴本铺满画面，从边到边挤满 6–10 样互相压叠的东西，有的伸出画框：胶带贴的水彩画（主画面，约占四成）、手绘地图、深褐老照片、印刷页、手写便条、压花；每样带柔影和泛黄胶带。水彩画：湿中湿铺色，受光面暖赭，暗面是硬边紫玫瑰色，钢笔勾轮廓，积色边，明暗对比清楚。配色：琥珀纸 #E8D6B0、深褐 #6B4A2E、赭 #D08A3C、紫玫瑰 #9C6A86、墨 #2A1E16。画里：暗前景里一个暖光源（火把、灯）投下光池，外面是明亮的天。琥珀调色只轻压书页，画里保持干净不发脏；暗角约两成，纸纹淡。招牌动作：推进一张画，直到书页边缘从两侧滑出、人已在画里。对标摄影师 John Seale（《英国病人》，1996）。避免：平滑数码渐变；稀疏整齐的版面；书页还框着画时横摇。

No.701997

Titanic泰坦尼克号

3DBlueprint Developing into 3D Sunset蓝图显影成 3D 实景

  • Shipyard general-arrangement blueprint船厂总布置蓝图
  • three.js ship at true scale按真实尺寸建模的 three.js 船
  • Russell Carpenter's colourRussell Carpenter 的色彩
  • One continuous camera move一镜到底

White lines on Prussian-blue drafting paper stand up into perspective and develop into a full-colour 3D ship at sunset, then fall into night. The ship is the film's icon, so its yard drawing and the real ship are the same model.普鲁士蓝晒图纸上的白线立起来、长出透视，再显影成夕阳下全彩的 3D 船，一路入夜。船本身就是这部片的标志，所以船厂图纸和实景用的是同一艘模型。

Style prompt风格提示词

A blueprint turning into photoreal-leaning 3D. Opening only (1–2 s, under 15% of the film): white edge lines about 1/800 of frame width on Prussian-blue paper (#12306B to #244D8F), hidden lines dashed, figures and terrain as contours, uneven exposure, folds, lettering. A dolly-zoom stands the elevation up into perspective; colour spreads from the hero along a soft warm edge, ghost lines fading within a second. Then the signature look: golden-hour real 3D, low sun, amber highlights #F2B860, warm shadows #3A2530, sun glitter on water, soft mist, shallow focus, gentle bloom, fine grain; then blue hour and night, warm lights coming on. One unbroken move: push in and stand up (blueprint), then, in equal shares, drift low past the hero, rise and orbit, descend. Reference: cinematographer Russell Carpenter (*Titanic*, 1997). Avoid: lingering on the drawing; hard cuts; flat CG light.

一张工程蓝图变成偏写实的 3D。蓝图只占开头 1–2 秒、不超过全片 15%：普鲁士蓝晒图纸（#12306B 到 #244D8F）上的白色棱线，线宽约画面 1/800，被挡的线画虚线，人物和地形用等高线；曝光不匀，有折痕、手写标注和图签。滑动变焦让立面图立成透视，再从主角往外显出全彩，显影边是带暖光的软边，幽灵线一秒内淡掉。之后是风格主体：黄金时刻的真实 3D，低太阳，暖琥珀高光 #F2B860，深暖阴影 #3A2530，水面阳光碎金，薄雾，浅景深，轻泛光，细颗粒；再到蓝调时刻和夜晚，暖灯亮起。一个不间断运镜：推近、立起在蓝图段，之后低掠主角、升起环绕、下降平分余下时间。对标摄影师 Russell Carpenter（《泰坦尼克号》，1997）。避免：在图上停太久；硬切；平而干净的 CG 光。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. long6.7s

    One continuous move: it pushes into the bow on the general-arrangement sheet; the drawing unfolds into perspective and develops into sunset from the lovers outward; a low drift on Rose's backlit silhouette with Clawd flying beside her, arms out; it rises and orbits over the port bow as the sun sinks and the ship's lights come on; it crosses the forecastle into night while Rose steps down from the rail; it comes down behind Clawd as the iceberg emerges from the mist, and Clawd turns and raises the alarm.

    一镜到底：在总布置图上推向船头；线稿立起成透视，从两人身上向外显影成夕阳；低机位贴近罗丝的逆光剪影，Clawd 在她身旁张开双臂；镜头升起、绕过左舷船头，太阳沉下，船灯亮起；越过艏楼进入夜色，罗丝从栏杆上下来；再降到 Clawd 身后，冰山从海雾里出来，Clawd 转身举臂报警。

Clawd plays Jack in a navy wool scarf. Sixteen beats, no cuts: the blueprint has unfolded by 1.5 s and the colour has developed by 2.4 s, with Clawd flying beside Rose; sunset to night is a time-lapse inside the move, and the berg rises in the last two seconds, Clawd's turn and alarm landing on the final beats.Clawd 扮杰克，围一条藏青羊毛围巾。16 拍一个镜头不切：1.5 秒蓝图展开完，2.4 秒显影完，Clawd 在罗丝身旁张开双臂；从夕阳到夜晚是运镜中的延时，冰山在最后两秒出现，Clawd 转身报警落在最后几拍。

No.711998

Shakespeare in Love莎翁情史

2DHilliard Elizabethan Miniature希利亚德伊丽莎白细密画

  • Portrait miniature (limning)宫廷细密肖像（limning）
  • Body colour on vellum羊皮纸上的不透明水彩
  • Shell gold贝壳金
  • Crimson damask still life深红锦缎静物

An Elizabethan portrait miniature in Nicholas Hilliard's manner: body colour on vellum, an ultramarine ground, shell-gold lettering, white eglantine. The film is set in Shakespeare's London, and the miniature was that court's own form of portrait.学尼古拉斯·希利亚德的伊丽莎白时代宫廷细密画：羊皮纸上的不透明水彩、群青底、贝壳金题字、白色野蔷薇。影片发生在莎士比亚的伦敦，细密画正是那个宫廷自己的肖像形式。

Style prompt风格提示词

Elizabethan portrait miniature in Nicholas Hilliard's manner: opaque watercolour laid flat on warm vellum, very fine hatching in shadows (lines about 1/1000 of frame width), lead-white dots on lit edges, red-brown outlines. Palette: ultramarine ground #203E92, shell gold #D6B05C (granular flecks, a few burnished highlights), carmine #A8202C, lead white #F6F2E8, verdigris #346C40. Signature shot: the picture inside a gold-rimmed oval locket lying on rust-red damask; a flowering white eglantine briar fills the oval behind and around the subject; a short motto in gold Roman capitals arcs along the top. The oval's ground stays ultramarine; landscape and sky go in as flat colour bands. Wide scenes may leave the locket, same method, true perspective. Slow push, slight tilt; only flowers, cloth and gestures move. Reference: Hilliard, *Young Man Among Roses* (c. 1587). Avoid: airbrush gradients; thick oil strokes; flat yellow gold.

伊丽莎白时代宫廷细密画，学尼古拉斯·希利亚德：暖色羊皮纸上平涂不透明水彩，暗面极细排线（线宽约画面宽度的 1/1000），受光边点铅白，红褐轮廓线。配色：群青底 #203E92、贝壳金 #D6B05C（细颗粒金点加几处抛光高光）、胭脂红 #A8202C、铅白 #F6F2E8、铜绿 #346C40。招牌画面：主体画在金边椭圆挂坠里，挂坠平放在锈红色锦缎上；一丛开满白花的野蔷薇铺满主体身后和四周，上沿一圈金色罗马大写短铭文。椭圆底色始终是群青，风景和天空放进去时画成平涂色带。大场景可离开挂坠，画法不变。镜头慢推、轻微下摇，只有花、布料和手势在动。对标希利亚德《玫瑰丛中的青年》（约 1587）。避免：喷枪渐变；厚油画笔触；平黄色的金。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. miniature0.8s

    Close on the gold locket lying on crimson damask: Clawd as Will stands among white eglantine in a ruff, the quill wiggling as it writes; a slow push in with a slight tilt down.

    近景：深红锦缎上的金色挂坠细密画，Clawd 扮莎士比亚站在白色野蔷薇里，戴轮状皱领，羽毛笔在写字；镜头慢慢推近，略往下摇。

  2. stage0.6s

    Wide of the Rose playhouse: the camera starts tilted up on the painted heavens and galleries, then tilts down and pushes in to the stage, where Clawd holds a white rose and the Queen sits in her box at right.

    全景：玫瑰剧院。镜头从上方的彩绘天幕和看台往下摇并推近到舞台，Clawd 拿着一朵白玫瑰站在台上，女王坐在右侧包厢里。

  3. balcony0.6s

    Medium shot of the balcony scene: Juliet above leans her cheek on her hand while Clawd below raises the white rose to her; a slight push in.

    中景：阳台戏，朱丽叶在上方托腮，Clawd 在下面向她举起白玫瑰；镜头略推。

  4. queen0.6s

    Close on Queen Elizabeth in her box under the cloth of estate: she turns from the stage, fans herself and gives a slow nod; a slight push in.

    近景：华盖下包厢里的伊丽莎白女王，从看戏转过脸来，摇着扇子，慢慢点头；镜头略推。

Clawd plays Will Shakespeare: a starched cartwheel ruff, a gold hoop earring, a quill in the miniature and a white rose on stage. Four hard cuts move from the private miniature out to the public playhouse, and the Queen's slow nod on the last beat closes the film.Clawd 扮莎士比亚：浆硬的轮状皱领、金耳环，细密画里拿羽毛笔，舞台上换成白玫瑰。四个镜头硬切，从私人的细密画一路走到公众的剧院，最后一拍女王慢慢点头，收住这一段。

References: 对标：John Madden, 1998 · Nicholas Hilliard, Young Man Among Roses (c.1587) · Shakespeare in Love 1998 poster (red damask, amber light)

No.721999

American Beauty美国丽人

2DSuburban Symmetry + DV Camcorder郊区对称电影画面 + DV 录像

  • Conrad Hall frontal symmetryConrad Hall 式正面对称构图
  • Rose-petal fantasy玫瑰花瓣幻想
  • 1990s DV camcorder video90 年代 DV 摄像机录像

Two looks cut together: a clean, saturated, dead-on shot of a suburban house with a red door and red roses, then soft, noisy camcorder video with a REC overlay. It mirrors the film's split between Lester's polished suburbia and Ricky's tape of the dancing bag.两种画面剪在一起：先是干净饱和、正面对称的郊区住宅（红门、红玫瑰），再切到带 REC 界面的低清 DV 录像。对应影片里莱斯特体面的郊区和里基拍下的那段塑料袋录像。

Style prompt风格提示词

Two registers. Primary, about 70%: a clean, saturated illustrated frame, dead-on symmetrical, the hero centred before its home or setting; every shape softly modelled in 2–3 tones, dense detail (shrubs, flowers, foliage of many small leaf clusters). Default bright daylight; under other light stay bright (gold at sunset), never dim. Red accents like a door #C3141F, white trim, saturated greens, orange-red autumn trees; warm grade, light vignette and grain. Transition: curled red petals or leaves (about 1/60 of frame width) thicken into a 1-second blizzard across the lens, hiding the cut. Second register, about 30%: the same world as 1990s DV home video: soft, low-res, chroma bleed, macroblocks, interlace, noise, breathing exposure, greenish shadows, REC/battery/date OSD. One register per frame. Slow push-ins. Reference: cinematographer Conrad L. Hall (*American Beauty*, 1999). Avoid: video artefacts in the clean part; real brands' OSD; flat vector shapes.

两种画面。主画面约七成：干净饱和的插画，正面对称，主角居中，身后是它的家或场景；形状有两三层柔和明暗，细节密（灌木、花丛、由许多小叶簇堆成的树冠）。默认明亮日光；其他光线下也要亮（日落偏金），不发暗。红门 #C3141F 一类红色点缀，白线脚，饱和的绿，橙红秋树；暖调、轻暗角、细颗粒。过渡：卷曲的红花瓣或落叶（约画宽 1/60）越积越密，一秒风暴扫过镜头藏住剪切。副画面约三成：同一世界的 90 年代 DV 家庭录像，发软、低清、色彩渗边、马赛克块、隔行线、噪点、曝光一呼一吸、暗部偏绿，屏显 REC、电量、日期。每帧只用一种。镜头缓推。对标摄影师 Conrad L. Hall（《美国丽人》，1999）。避免：干净画面带录像瑕疵；真实品牌屏显；扁平矢量形。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. house1.3s

    Wide, dead-on symmetrical shot of the Burnham house; the camera pushes in without a cut to Clawd on the sidewalk holding a long-stemmed red rose, while the petals thicken into a blizzard and sweep across the lens.

    正面对称的伯纳姆家全景，镜头不切，直接推到人行道上拿着长茎红玫瑰的 Clawd；花瓣越落越密，最后成片扫过镜头。

  2. bag1.2s

    Through Ricky's camcorder (REC, OCT. 17 1998, 12:07:33): a white plastic bag dances with dry leaves in front of a red-brick wall; a slow push in, drifting right. No Clawd.

    里基的摄像机画面（REC、OCT. 17 1998、12:07:33）：白色塑料袋和枯叶在红砖墙前打转；镜头慢推，往右漂。没有 Clawd。

Clawd plays Lester: a white shirt collar, a navy tie with wine stripes, a red rose in hand. One push-in carries the house to the close-up as the petals build; the cut to video is hidden in the petal gust at 1.3 s, so fantasy flows into Ricky's tape with no visible edit.Clawd 扮莱斯特：白衬衫领尖、藏青底酒红斜纹领带，手里一枝红玫瑰。一个推镜头从房子推到近景，花瓣一路加密；到 1.3 秒，切到录像的那一刀藏在扫过镜头的花瓣里，幻想直接流进里基的录像带，看不出剪辑点。

References: 对标：Sam Mendes, 1999 · Conrad L. Hall (cinematography) · American Beauty 1999 poster (the rose)

The New Millennium新千年2000–2009

No.732000

Gladiator角斗士

2DRoman Floor Mosaic罗马地面马赛克

  • Opus vermiculatum tesserae顺形排列的石子（opus vermiculatum）
  • Gold-leaf glass smalti金箔玻璃嵌片
  • Zliten gladiator mosaicZliten 角斗士马赛克

Everything is laid in square stone and glass tesserae: courses follow the outlines, flat areas run in straight rows, gold-leaf tiles glint. The film's world is Rome, so the arena and Elysium are built as a Roman floor mosaic.整幅画用方形石子和玻璃嵌片铺成：轮廓边的石子顺着形体排，平面处一行行直排，金箔玻璃片会闪。影片本身就在罗马，所以斗兽场和极乐麦田都做成罗马地面马赛克。

Style prompt风格提示词

Roman floor mosaic: over a flat underdrawing lay square tesserae about 1/150 of frame width: two to three courses follow each outline (opus vermiculatum), flat areas in straight rows, figures outlined with one course of dark stones. Muted, warm stone and glass: burnt gold #E2B24C, umber #5B4332, terracotta #8E3C2B, warm marble #E9DDC4, with imperial-purple glass for one accent only; sky and water in pale grey-blue and cream stone, never saturated bands. Tesserae slightly irregular, lit from the upper left, shadowing into dark 1–2 px grout; gold-leaf glass glints slowly on light sources. Frontal, slightly stiff Pompeian figures before a faded field of fine stones. Short pushes and pulls; motion from details (a gesture, a crowd, plants, a light shaft). References: *Alexander Mosaic*, Zliten mosaic. Avoid: a neat grid that reads as pixel art; oversized tiles; smooth gradients; bright grout.

罗马地面马赛克。先画平涂底稿，再铺小方石子，每粒约画面宽度的 1/150：轮廓边的石子顺着边排两三圈（opus vermiculatum），其余一行行横排；人物外面包一圈深色石子轮廓。颜色只用天然石头和玻璃，沉稳偏暖：焦金 #E2B24C、赭褐 #5B4332、陶红 #8E3C2B、暖大理石 #E9DDC4，帝王紫玻璃只做一处点缀；天空和水用浅灰蓝、奶油色石头，不要饱和的色带。每粒切口略不规则，左上受光，往 1–2px 宽的深色灰缝投小影；金箔玻璃片在光源处慢慢闪。人物正面、略僵，像庞贝马赛克；背景是大片褪色的细碎石子。镜头只短推短拉，动的是细节：手势、人群、草木、光柱。对标《亚历山大马赛克》、Zliten 马赛克。避免：整齐网格变像素画；石子太大；平滑渐变；灰缝太亮。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. emperor0.8s

    Close on the purple imperial box: Commodus's thumb turns from up to down; a slight push in.

    近景：紫色的皇帝包厢，康茂德的大拇指从朝上转到朝下；镜头略推。

  2. still1.0s

    Poster-style wide of the arena: Clawd as Maximus in the centre with the gladius held low, the chained tiger at left, Tigris at right, the crowd rising; a slight pull back.

    斗兽场海报式全景：Clawd 扮麦克西姆斯站在中央，短剑朝下，左边是拴着链子的老虎，右边是提格里斯，看台上的观众站起来；镜头略拉。

  3. farm0.8s

    Wide of Elysium: Clawd stands in golden wheat under a low sun, with cypresses and the farmhouse on the horizon; a slow push in.

    极乐世界全景：低垂的太阳下，Clawd 站在金色麦田里，地平线上是柏树和农庄；镜头慢推。

Clawd plays Maximus: one bronze pauldron and a Roman gladius. The thumb turning down opens the film; a hard cut to the arena lets the crowd rise around Clawd; the last cut leaves the fight for the quiet wheat of Elysium. Three hard cuts of about a second each.Clawd 扮麦克西姆斯：一只青铜肩甲、一把罗马短剑。开场是大拇指朝下；硬切到斗兽场，观众在 Clawd 四周站起来；最后一刀离开打斗，落到极乐世界安静的麦田。三个硬切，每个一秒左右。

References: 对标：Ridley Scott, 2000 · John Mathieson (cinematography) · Alexander Mosaic, Pompeii · Zliten mosaic

No.742001

A Beautiful Mind美丽心灵

2DWindow Marker & Chalk, Gold Pattern窗上公式与黑板粉笔 + 金色连线

  • White china marker on glass玻璃上的白色油性笔
  • Blackboard chalk黑板粉笔
  • Glowing gold pattern lines发光的金色连线
  • Deakins window light迪金斯式窗光

Handwritten maths on two surfaces, white marker on a Gothic library window and chalk on slate, with digits that light up and join in gold lines. It shows Nash seeing patterns the way the film does: a light in his head, not a computer interface.两种表面上的手写数学：哥特式图书馆窗玻璃上的白色油性笔、黑板上的粉笔，数字亮起，被金线连起来。这是片中纳什看见规律的方式：是他脑中的光，不是电脑界面。

Style prompt风格提示词

Handwriting as the image: translucent white wax-marker strokes (about 1/300 of frame width) appear stroke by stroke on a window of square leaded panes: formulas, figures or notes about the subject, in small blocks on a quarter of the glass. Outside, the subject's world, soft-focus, mid-toned and warm (overcast gold, muted green), never brighter than the writing. A warm interior (shelves, green-shaded lamp, amber light) frames the window. At the key moment, chosen symbols brighten and soft gold lines join them into a star or polygon, lights running along. Variant: chalk on green slate with ghost writing. Palette: slate green #2F3E35, chalk white #F1EFE6, lamp amber #E0A050, soft gold #D9B45A. Camera: slow push-ins, each cut closer to the writing. Reference: cinematographer Roger Deakins (*A Beautiful Mind*, 2001). Avoid: HUD graphics; typed fonts; writing filling the glass; cold blue outside.

手写就是画面：半透明白色蜡笔 #F1EFE6（笔画宽约画宽 1/300）在方格铅条窗玻璃上一笔笔写出来，写的是与题材有关的公式、数字或笔记，几小块字只占玻璃约四分之一。窗外是题材的世界，柔焦虚化，中间调、暖中性（阴天里的秋黄和柔和的绿），亮度不能超过字迹。暖色室内框住窗子：书架、绿罩台灯、琥珀灯光。关键时刻，选中的符号变亮，柔和的金线在它们之间连成星形或多边形，小光点沿线走。变体：深绿石板粉笔，带残影。配色：石板绿 #2F3E35、粉笔白 #F1EFE6、灯光琥珀 #E0A050、柔金 #D9B45A。镜头缓推，每次切换都离字更近。对标摄影师 Roger Deakins（《美丽心灵》，2001）。避免：HUD 或科幻界面；打字字体；字铺满玻璃；冷蓝色的窗外。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. still1.0s

    Medium wide at the Gothic library window: Clawd as Nash stands on the window seat writing game theory on the glass in white marker, the autumn quad behind; a slow push in.

    中全景：哥特式图书馆窗前，Clawd 扮纳什站在窗座上，用白色油性笔在玻璃上写博弈论公式，窗外是秋天的方院；镜头慢推。

  2. glass0.8s

    Close on the writing on the glass, the campus soft behind and Clawd's marker at lower left: characters light up and gold lines join them into a star; a slight push in.

    近景：玻璃上的字迹，窗外虚焦，左下角露出 Clawd 的笔；字符亮起，金线把它们连成一颗星；镜头略推。

  3. pattern0.8s

    Close on the block of five-digit code groups on the blackboard: the digits light one by one and a gold seven-pointed pattern draws itself; a slight push in. No Clawd.

    近景：黑板上五位一组的密码数字，数字一个个亮起，金色的七角星图案自己画出来；镜头略推。没有 Clawd。

Clawd plays Nash: an open shirt collar, a loose knitted tie, a marker at the window. The writing fills the first shot and the glow takes the next two; each hard cut moves closer to the numbers until Clawd leaves the frame and only the pattern is left.Clawd 扮纳什：领口敞开的衬衫领、松着的针织领带，窗前拿一支油性笔。第一个镜头在写，后两个镜头是光；每次硬切都离数字更近，最后 Clawd 出画，只剩图案。

References: 对标：Ron Howard, 2001 · Roger Deakins (cinematography) · A Beautiful Mind 2001 poster

No.752002

Chicago芝加哥

2DFosse Stage in Red and Black福斯舞台：红黑聚光剪影

  • Bob Fosse staging鲍勃·福斯的舞台调度
  • Red-rimmed backlit silhouettes勾红边的逆光剪影
  • Neon and sequins霓虹与亮片
  • Chicago 2002 poster colours2002 年海报的红黑

A black stage cut by hard spotlights: six cells with women in red-rimmed silhouette, a neon C, sequins, haze, and Clawd in a white follow spot. It is the 'Cell Block Tango' number from Rob Marshall's film, in the poster's red and black.黑色舞台被硬边聚光切开：六间牢房里是勾红边的女囚剪影，背后霓虹 C，亮片和烟雾，Clawd 在白色追光里。这是电影版的「Cell Block Tango」，用海报的红黑。

Style prompt风格提示词

Bob Fosse-style Broadway stage number. Black stage, but about 40% of the frame glows signal red #D0202A: a row of dark barred cells, each backlit red through haze, bright at centre, falling off to black, plus red curtains; hard-edged spots. Dancers are full-bodied black silhouettes with costume shapes and a 2–3 px red rim. One big neon shape (letter or pictogram) in orange-red #FF5A3C, white-hot core, slight flicker, set against black so it reads; glossy black floor reflects it; marquee bulbs #FFD9A0 at the stage edge. The hero, in a warm-white follow spot #FFF1D6, is the only lit figure in its own colours. Accents: sequins, white gloves. Moves on the jazz beat; cells snap on one by one. Push in to end in the follow spot. Reference: Rob Marshall, *Chicago* (2002). Avoid: flat even fills; extra colours; rimless silhouettes.

鲍勃·福斯式百老汇歌舞舞台。黑底，但约四成画面发信号红 #D0202A：一排带栏杆的暗色格间，每格在烟雾里从背后打红光，中心最亮、向四周暗到黑，再加红幕；硬边聚光穿过烟雾。舞者是实心黑剪影，身形饱满、有服装轮廓，外面一圈 2–3px 红色边光。一个大霓虹字形（字母或图形），橙红 #FF5A3C，白热芯、微闪，要放在黑底上才读得出；黑色亮漆地板倒映它；台边一排跑马灯泡 #FFD9A0。主角站在暖白追光 #FFF1D6 里，是唯一被照亮、保留自身颜色的人。点缀：亮片、白手套。动作踩爵士拍子；灯格一间间啪地亮。镜头推进，收在追光里。对标 Rob Marshall《芝加哥》（2002）。避免：平涂匀光；多余颜色；剪影没有边光。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. stage0.8s

    Wide of the whole stage with the neon C behind: the six cells light one by one, each woman snapping into her pose, while Clawd struts centre stage; a slow push in.

    舞台全景，霓虹 C 在后面：六间牢房一间间亮起，每个女囚啪地摆出招牌姿势，Clawd 在台中央走福斯步；镜头慢推。

  2. cell40.5s

    Close on one cell: Velma's silhouette behind the bars in red light, a hip roll and a shrug; a slight push in. No Clawd.

    近景：一间牢房，铁栏后红光里 Velma 的剪影，扭胯、耸肩；镜头略推。没有 Clawd。

  3. spot1.2s

    中近景：Clawd 独自站在白色追光里，身后是亮着的牢房：它走福斯步到脚灯前，然后放下手杖，双手 jazz hands 举起；镜头慢推。

Clawd wears a Fosse bowler with a sequin band and white gloves, and carries a black cane. The cells light on the beats of the first shot; a quick cut to one dancer; then Clawd takes the spot alone and the number ends on jazz hands. The performance runs at real speed.Clawd 戴福斯式圆顶礼帽（帽带一圈亮片）、白手套，拿黑手杖。第一个镜头里牢房跟着拍子一间间亮；快切一个女囚；然后 Clawd 独占追光，以 jazz hands 收尾。表演按真实速度走，不加速。

References: 对标：Rob Marshall, 2002 · Dion Beebe (cinematography) · Bob Fosse, Chicago (1975 stage) · Chicago 2002 poster

No.762003

The Lord of the Rings: The Return of the King指环王：王者无敌

2DAlan Lee Watercolour & Tolkien Map艾伦·李式水彩 + 托尔金地图

  • Watercolour and pencil concept art水彩加铅笔的概念插画
  • Tolkien-style parchment map托尔金式羊皮纸地图
  • Alan Lee and John Howe艾伦·李与约翰·豪

Soft watercolour and pencil landscapes with deep aerial perspective, plus an ink map on stained parchment. Alan Lee and John Howe drew the films' concept art, so the journey is told the way the trilogy was first drawn.柔和的水彩加铅笔风景，空气透视很深，再加一张羊皮纸上的墨线地图。电影的概念画就是艾伦·李和约翰·豪画的，所以这段旅程按三部曲最初被画出来的样子来讲。

Style prompt风格提示词

Fantasy concept-art watercolour and pencil on warm paper. Transparent washes, granulated (pigment speckle, ±6% brightness), a few blooms, darker pooled edges; every contour and ridge carries a dark-brown pencil line (about 1/800 of frame width), short hatching on shadow sides. Aerial perspective: 3–5 receding planes, each paler and cooler. Light is the subject: one strong source per scene (sun, fire, lamp, molten glow) burning through haze, its ember glow on about a quarter of the frame, about half the frame in deep umber and violet shadow. Palette: parchment #E9DCC0, deep umber #3A2418, ember orange #E07A2E, hot red #B23A1E only at the light's core, lavender #B8A8C8 for distance. Journeys may cut to a Tolkien-style map, never the main look. Camera: long lateral pans, slow push-ins to the hero. References: Alan Lee, John Howe. Avoid: smooth digital gradients; vector shapes; pale frames.

奇幻概念设计式的水彩加铅笔，暖色纸。透明水彩要看得出颗粒（细碎颜料斑点，亮度起伏 ±6%），几朵回流花，水迹边缘积色更深；每条轮廓和山脊都有一道深褐铅笔线（约画面宽度的 1/800），暗面短排线。空气透视要强：3–5 层后退，越远越淡越冷。光就是主题：每场一个强光源（低太阳、火、灯、熔岩般的光）穿透雾气，余烬色光晕约占画面四分之一，约一半画面压在深褐和紫色暗部里。配色：羊皮纸 #E9DCC0、深褐 #3A2418、余烬橙 #E07A2E、炽红 #B23A1E 只用在光源核心、远景淡紫 #B8A8C8。旅程题材可插一段托尔金式地图，但不当主画面。镜头：长横摇扫过大地，缓推到主角。对标 Alan Lee、John Howe。避免：平滑的数码渐变；平涂矢量形；发白发灰的画面。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. map1.3s

    Top-down on the parchment map: the camera pushes from the whole sheet into the Shire, then flies along the red dashed line toward Mordor while Clawd, small as a game piece, walks the route.

    俯视羊皮纸地图：镜头从整张图推进夏尔，再沿红色虚线往魔多飞，Clawd 像一枚棋子那么小，沿着路线走。

  2. beacons1.9s

    Wide of the snowy White Mountains above a sea of cloud at dusk: the near pyre catches, then a slow pan right with a slight pull back as beacon after beacon lights along the range.

    全景：黄昏的雪山浮在云海上，近处的烽火先点着，镜头慢慢往右横移、略往后拉，山脊上的烽火一座接一座亮起。

  3. eye0.4s

    Close on the Eye atop Barad-dûr: a push in as it turns and sweeps its beam. No Clawd.

    近景：巴拉多塔顶的索伦之眼，镜头推近，它转过来扫出光束。没有 Clawd。

  4. doom1.5s

    At the Crack of Doom: from the wide basalt ledge over the lava, with the volcano and the Eye behind, the camera pushes in to Clawd as it lifts the glowing Ring up out of the chasm and holds it high.

    末日火山裂缝边：镜头从熔岩上方的玄武岩舌头全景推近到 Clawd，背后是火山和眼睛，它把发光的魔戒从深渊边提起，高高举起。

Clawd plays Frodo: a grey-green cloak collar, a Lórien leaf brooch, the Ring on a chain. As the key film of the decade it gets 12 beats: the map lays out the road, the beacons carry the camera across the range, the Eye is a short beat of threat, and the raised Ring is the payoff. Hard cuts, long moves within each shot.Clawd 扮佛罗多：灰绿色斗篷领、罗瑞恩叶子胸针、链子上的魔戒。作为这十年的重点片给了 12 拍：地图交代整条路，烽火带着镜头扫过山脉，眼睛是一拍短促的威胁，最后举起魔戒收住。镜头之间硬切，每个镜头内部是长运动。

References: 对标：Peter Jackson, 2003 · Andrew Lesnie (cinematography) · Alan Lee · John Howe · J.R.R. Tolkien's maps of Middle-earth

No.772004

Million Dollar Baby百万美元宝贝

2D16-bit Arcade Fighting Game16-bit 像素格斗游戏

  • 16-bit pixel art16-bit 像素画
  • Arcade fighting-game HUD街机格斗游戏界面
  • CRT scanlinesCRT 扫描线

An original 16-bit arcade fighting-game screen: health bars, a round timer, ROUND 1 / FIGHT!, a combo counter, hit-stop and a big K.O. A boxing film about a shot at the title becomes one round told in arcade grammar.原创的 16-bit 街机格斗游戏画面：血条、回合计时、ROUND 1 / FIGHT!、连击数、命中停顿和巨大的 K.O.。一部拳击冠军赛的电影，变成用街机语法打完的一回合。

Style prompt风格提示词

An original 16-bit arcade fighting game. Draw at 320×180 and nearest-neighbour upscale by a whole number (×6 for 1080p); CRT scanlines (alternate lines ~15% darker). A side-on arena from the subject's world, flattened into stage and backdrop (e.g. a ring with a dark purple crowd band #1D1435). UI in a self-drawn 5×7 pixel font: two health bars (yellow #F5C53A, red damage trail), timer, banner, ROUND 1 / FIGHT!, combo counter. Chunky sprites about 50 px tall, 1-px highlights, dark outlines, ~32 colours per frame. The story becomes a contest between the hero and a rival (if none exists, personify an obstacle: a storm, a deadline). Hits spark, freeze 3–4 frames and flash white; the loser is knocked back, then K.O. and WINNER. Fixed side-on camera, no cuts. Reference: early-1990s arcade fighters. Avoid: real games' characters or logos; anti-aliased pixels.

原创的 16-bit 街机格斗游戏。画在 320×180 上，用最近邻放大到输出尺寸的整数倍（1080p 用 6 倍），加轻微 CRT 扫描线（隔行压暗约 15%）。横版场地取自题材：把题材的地点压成平的台面加一层背景（比如拳台配深紫观众带 #1D1435）。界面用自绘 5×7 点阵字：两条血条（黄色 #F5C53A，掉血时拖一段红色）、计时、横幅、ROUND 1 / FIGHT!、连击数。角色是方块胖像素，高约 50 像素，一像素高光加深色边，全画面约 32 色。故事改写成主角和一个对手的较量；题材里没有敌人，就把它世界里的阻碍拟人化当对手（风暴、截止期限）。打中有火花、停顿 3–4 帧、白闪，输家被打飞，然后 K.O. 和 WINNER。固定横版视角，不剪。对标 90 年代初街机格斗游戏。避免：照搬真实游戏的角色或 logo；像素抗锯齿。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. game2.5s

    One locked side-on game screen: ROUND 1, FIGHT!, the shadow boxer swings a hook and Clawd ducks under it, dashes in for jab, jab and an uppercut with hit-stop and a white flash; the rival flies back, K.O., and Clawd raises its glove as the winner.

    一个固定的横版游戏画面：ROUND 1、FIGHT!，影子拳手挥出勾拳，Clawd 低头躲过，冲上去刺拳、刺拳，再一记上勾拳，命中停顿加白闪；对手飞出去，K.O.，Clawd 举起拳套赢下比赛。

Clawd wears a green satin robe with white trim and red gloves; the rival is a faceless navy boxer called SHADOW. The fight is choreographed to the clock: the uppercut lands at 1.46 s with a hit-stop, K.O. at 1.6 s, victory at 1.95 s. One shot, no cuts, the game's own camera.Clawd 穿白边绿缎拳击袍、戴红拳套；对手是没有脸的深蓝色影子拳手 SHADOW。整场按时间编排：1.46 秒上勾拳命中并停顿，1.6 秒 K.O.，1.95 秒胜利。一镜到底，用游戏自己的镜头。

Crash撞车

2DLA Night Light Trails & Snow洛杉矶夜景光轨 + 飘雪

  • Long-exposure night photography长曝光夜景摄影
  • Sodium orange and neon cyan钠灯橙配霓虹青
  • Anamorphic bloom and film grain变形宽银幕光条与胶片颗粒

A polished night photograph made in code: freeway light trails in white and red, sodium orange against neon cyan, wet asphalt, bokeh, and snow that shows only where light hits it. Crash ends on a rare snowfall over Los Angeles.用代码画的精修夜景照片：高架上白色和红色的车灯光轨、钠灯橙对霓虹青、湿路面反光、焦外光斑，还有只在光里才看得见的雪。《撞车》的结尾正是洛杉矶难得的一场雪。

Style prompt风格提示词

Long-exposure night street photography. Sodium-vapour orange #F2A24A washes structures, signs and wet ground (about half the frame in warm light), against cyan neon #3FD0D8 and the odd red-blue emergency light; blacks cyan-tinged, never grey. Layered composition: foreground pavement with figures, lit shopfronts and signs mid-frame, roads or bridges crossing the upper third (away from cities, a lamp-lit road in the subject's own setting). Headlights become thin continuous ribbons (about 1/150 of frame width) along those roads, white #FFF4E0 and red #E0303A, glowing with hot cores. Snow: three layers of short soft streaks, seen only in light cones. Finish: two-level bloom, blue lens streaks, edge bokeh, vignette, moving grain. The hero stands sharp in a lamp cone mid-frame; only traffic streaks. Camera: one continuous tilt, pan and rack focus. Reference: cinematographer J. Michael Muro (*Crash*, 2004). Avoid: evenly lit snow; empty black frames; dotted trails.

长曝光夜景街拍。钠灯暖橙 #F2A24A 铺满建筑、招牌和湿地面（约一半画面在暖光里），对着青色霓虹 #3FD0D8，偶有红蓝警灯；黑色略偏青，不能发灰。构图分层：前景人行道有人，中景亮着的店面招牌，上三分之一横过道路或桥（不在城市就用主体所在地一条有路灯的路）。车灯沿这些路拉成细而连续的光带（约画宽 1/150），白 #FFF4E0 与红 #E0303A，带光晕和发烫的芯。雪分三层，短而柔的拖影，只在光锥里可见。收尾：两级泛光、蓝色横向光条、边缘焦外光斑、暗角、动态颗粒。主角清楚地站在画面中部一盏灯的光锥里，只有车流拖影。镜头：一个连续运镜，俯仰、横摇加转焦。对标摄影师 J. Michael Muro（《撞车》，2004）。避免：雪到处一样亮；大片空黑；光带断成点。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. oner2.5s

    One continuous move: it opens tilted up on the interchange light trails, tilts down onto the rear-end crash behind the police tape with focus on the wreck, then pans right along the kerb to Clawd under the sodium lamp as focus pulls to it; Clawd raises an arm to catch a snowflake and blinks.

    一镜到底：开场仰拍立交桥上的光轨，往下摇到警戒带后的追尾现场，焦点在事故上；再沿着路缘往右摇到钠灯下的 Clawd，焦点拉过来；Clawd 抬起手臂接一片雪，眨了下眼。

Clawd wears a hand-knitted teal scarf and stands under a street lamp looking up into the snow. The v6 cut turns three shots into one camera move, with motion blur on the fast stretches: trails, then the crash, then Clawd. The snow thickens as the camera reaches it, and the catch comes near the end.Clawd 围一条手织的深青色围巾，站在路灯下仰头看雪。v6 把原来的三个镜头改成一个连续运镜，快速移动的几段带运动模糊：先光轨，再车祸，最后是 Clawd。镜头到它那里时雪变大，接雪的动作落在快结束的地方。

No.792006

The Departed无间行者

2DStreet Stencil on Boston Brick波士顿红砖墙模板涂鸦

  • Multi-layer spray stencil多层喷漆模板
  • Banksy's rats班克斯的老鼠
  • Red-brick wall texture红砖墙肌理
  • Halftone paste-up poster半调糊贴海报

Flat spray-paint stencil layers with overspray and drips, the paint sinking into brick mortar, beside a torn paste-up poster. The Departed ends on a rat crossing a railing below the State House dome, and street stencils have their own tradition of rats.平涂的多层喷漆模板，有喷雾毛边和流挂，漆吃进砖缝，旁边一张撕破的糊贴海报。《无间行者》最后一个镜头是老鼠爬过州议会金顶下的栏杆，街头模板涂鸦也一直爱画老鼠。

Style prompt风格提示词

Street stencil graffiti on an old red-brick wall. Each piece is 3–5 stencils sprayed as flat colour, photo-derived in look (smallest cut detail about 1/200 of frame width): hard cut edges, soft overspray where a stencil lifted, spray dots, drips, stencil bridges. Palette: black #151312, mid grey #8C8A85, gold #C99A2E, red #C3261F; brick #7A3224, cream painted patches under the main pieces. Brick relief, mortar and rain streaks show through, so paint sinks into the joints. A busy wall: several pieces side by side, short hand-sprayed slogans about the subject (user's language), old tags, a torn halftone paste-up. Light: soft raking daylight, mild contrast, a faint swaying wire shadow; no heavy vignette. Camera: pieces spray on layer by layer, then close-ups and lateral tracks. Reference: Banksy. Avoid: clean vector edges; paint floating on the brick; too many colours; a dark wall.

老红砖墙上的街头模板涂鸦。每件作品用 3–5 块镂空模板喷成平色块，有照片感（最小镂空约画宽 1/200）：硬切口，模板翘起处有柔喷雾，散落喷点，流挂，以及模板必须留的连接筋。配色：黑 #151312、中灰 #8C8A85、金 #C99A2E、红 #C3261F；红砖 #7A3224，主要作品喷在刷上去的奶油色补丁上。砖面凹凸、灰缝阴影和雨痕透过漆显出来，让漆吃进砖缝。一面墙并排放好几件作品，加关于题材的手喷短标语（用观众的语言）、旧 tag 和一张撕角的半调糊贴海报。光：柔和的白天光斜扫过墙，反差适中，电线影子轻轻晃；不加重暗角。镜头：先让作品一层层喷出来，再切近景、横移。对标班克斯。避免：边缘干净像矢量；漆浮在砖上；颜色太多；墙面昏暗。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. wall0.8s

    全景：整面墙，模板画一层层喷出来：城市、金顶、栏杆、老鼠、拿手机的 Clawd、红色标语；镜头略推。

  2. phone0.6s

    Close on the two sprayed flip phones ringing across the gap, with Clawd's own phone at its head; Clawd listens, eyes sliding left; a slow drift right.

    近景：两部喷漆翻盖手机隔空响铃，Clawd 耳边也贴着手机；它听着，眼睛往左瞟；镜头慢慢往右漂。

  3. rat0.6s

    中景：老鼠沿着金顶下的栏杆跑过，左上角喷着「WHO'S THE RAT?」，警灯的红蓝光扫过砖墙；镜头跟着往右横移。

  4. close0.4s

    近景：Clawd 把手机往下一放，眼睛看向右边；镜头略推。

Clawd plays an undercover cop with a flip phone pressed to its head, sprayed as one layer of the wall. The piece paints itself in over the first two beats; then the phones, the rat and Clawd hanging up follow in fast cuts. Nobody speaks; the ringing and the rat tell it.Clawd 扮卧底警察，耳边贴着翻盖手机，本身也是墙上喷出来的一层。前两拍作品一层层喷出来，之后手机、老鼠、Clawd 挂电话，快切接连出现。没人说话，靠铃声和老鼠讲完。

No Country for Old Men老无所依

3D3D West Texas Dusk, Deakins Light3D 西德州荒原黄昏（迪金斯光）

A real-time 3D West Texas at dusk: the sun pressed onto the horizon, long shadows toward the camera, desaturated ochre and grey-green, fine grain. It follows Roger Deakins' light for the Coens and the poster's backlit man with a case.实时 3D 的西德州黄昏荒原：太阳压在地平线上，长影子朝着镜头，低饱和的土黄和灰绿，细颗粒。照着迪金斯为科恩兄弟拍的光，和海报上逆光里提着箱子的人。

Style prompt风格提示词

Photoreal-leaning 3D landscape at dusk. Sky fills 60% of the frame, dusty ochre #D8A060 at the horizon up to pale cream, thin streaked clouds lit from below. Sun 1–2° above the horizon, still casting shadows; its light burns along the far ridge. Low saturation: hundreds of grey-green scrub clumps receding to the horizon, stones, dry grass, dark warm earth #2A2622 (default arid; elsewhere keep sparse clumped growth). The hero stands backlit in the mid-ground, about a third of frame height, long shadow toward the lens. Expose for the sky: the sun may clip, its glow within a tenth of the frame. Grade: neutral tone mapping, warm highlights, fine grain, vignette. Signature shot: locked, low, wide (~35 mm). Reference: cinematographer Roger Deakins (*No Country for Old Men*, 2007). Avoid: saturated orange sunsets; blown-out glare; bare ground; busy camera moves.

偏写实的 3D 黄昏旷野（three.js）。天空很大，约占画面六成，地平线处尘土赭色 #D8A060，往上渐到淡奶油色，几缕细长云被下方照亮。太阳高出地平线 1–2°，仍能投影，光沿远处坡脊烧成一线亮边。低饱和：成百丛灰绿小灌木退向地平线，碎石和干草散在暗暖色泥土 #2A2622 上（默认干旱荒地，换别的地貌也保留稀疏成丛的植被）。主角逆光站在中景，约占画面高度三分之一，长影子朝镜头拉来。按天空曝光：太阳可以过曝，光晕不超过画面十分之一。调色：中性色调映射、暖高光、细颗粒、暗角。招牌镜头：基本固定，低机位广角（约 35mm）。对标摄影师 Roger Deakins（《老无所依》，2007）。避免：饱和的橙色晚霞；大片发白眩光；光秃的地面；忙乱的运镜。

Two registers. 两种画面。主画面约占七成：光鲜的插画式答题演播室，明亮饱满，略俯的四分之三视角：底光玻璃圆台和一圈圈蓝光环，背后坐满观众，摇头灯在烟雾里打出蓝紫光束 #5A3FD0，大屏上长六角形答案框，一个锁成琥珀色 #F0A020；加泛光和横向光斑；越紧张四周越暗，最多暗到一半，不全黑。人物圆润干净，柔和明暗。第二种画面约占三成，整屏硬切：主题所在地方的黄金时刻景象，饱和橙青，密而热闹（比如铁皮屋顶、晾衣绳、人群）。点缀：洋红 #E2206B、青 #0F7C7A、橘黄 #F06A14。镜头推向大屏和座位，光束收到一人身上；其余慢摇。对标摄影师 Anthony Dod Mantle（《贫民窟的百万富翁》，2008）。避免：真实节目 logo；大片漆黑的演播室；平均打光；灰暗空荡的场景。

  • Olive and sand flat tints橄榄绿与沙色平涂

A page from an army bomb-disposal manual: even black lines, hatched shadows, stippled sand, numbered callouts, an exploded IED diagram, a little olive and sand. The Hurt Locker is about procedure under pressure, and a manual page is procedure drawn.一页美军拆弹手册：均匀黑线、排线阴影、点描沙土、带圈编号和引线、IED 分解图，只有一点橄榄绿和沙色。《拆弹部队》讲高压下的程序，手册图就是画出来的程序。

Style prompt风格提示词

Military technical-manual illustration. Warm off-white paper #EFEADB with a faint engineering grid, uniform black ink #1B1A16 (outlines ~1/700 of frame width, hatching thinner), shading only by hatching, ground by stippling, flat tints of olive #6C6D42 and sand #D9C79C on 20–30% of the drawing; lights as ray lines, not glows. Main figure: a one-point-perspective view of the subject's setting filling most of the page; around it exploded views of the subject's key object along a dash-dot axis, circled numbers on leader lines, a parts table, WARNING and CAUTION boxes, typewriter labels (user's language) and FIG numbers, a black chapter card. Animate on twos: parts slide apart, a tool works, a figure walks. Camera: whole page, then cuts to enlarged insets. References: US Army technical manuals; Kathryn Bigelow, *The Hurt Locker* (2008). Avoid: gradient shading; too much colour; decorative fonts; sparse pages.

军用技术手册插图。暖米白纸 #EFEADB 上一层很淡的工程网格，统一黑墨 #1B1A16（轮廓线宽约画面宽度的 1/700，排线更细），明暗只靠排线，地面用点刻，只在两三成的画面上平涂橄榄绿 #6C6D42 和沙色 #D9C79C；灯光画成放射线，不画光晕。主图：题材场景的一点透视画面，占页面大半，里面有人物和器物；四周是题材关键器物沿点划线轴展开的爆炸图、引线上的带圈数字、零件表、WARNING 和 CAUTION 框、打字机字体的标注（用观众的语言）和 FIG 编号，一块黑底章节卡。动画一拍二：零件滑开、工具动作、人物走路。镜头先看整页，再切到放大插图式的细节。对标美军技术手册，Kathryn Bigelow《拆弹部队》（2008）。避免：渐变明暗；颜色太多；花哨字体；空荡的页面。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. still0.8s

    The whole manual page, FIG 4-6 'The Long Walk': a soldier in a bomb suit walks down the Baghdad street toward camera while Clawd stands over the daisy chain of shells with its cutters; a slow push toward the street.

    整页手册，FIG 4-6「THE LONG WALK」：穿拆弹服的士兵沿巴格达土路朝镜头走来，Clawd 站在串起来的炮弹中间拿着剪线钳；镜头慢慢推向街道。

  2. suit0.6s

    Close on the bomb-suit soldier walking, his frowning face behind the visor; a slow push in.

    近景：穿拆弹服的士兵在走，面罩里是一张皱着眉的脸；镜头慢推。

  3. close0.5s

    Close on Clawd in the EOD helmet with the visor up: its eyes drop to the firing lead and the cutters come up; a slight push in.

    近景：戴拆弹头盔、面罩掀起的 Clawd，眼睛往下看向起爆线，剪线钳举起来；镜头略推。

  4. wire0.6s

    特写：剪线钳咬住炮弹上方的双绞起爆线，一捏、咔，线断开；镜头推近。

Clawd wears an olive EOD helmet with the visor up and holds wire cutters among six half-buried 155mm shells. Four cuts tighten from the page to the snap: the long walk, the face in the visor, Clawd's eyes, the cut. The snap comes just before the film ends.Clawd 戴橄榄绿拆弹头盔、面罩掀起，手拿剪线钳，站在六枚半埋的 155mm 炮弹中间。四个镜头从整页一路收紧到剪断：漫长的走路、面罩里的脸、Clawd 的眼睛、剪线。咔的一声落在这段快结束的地方。

References: 对标：Kathryn Bigelow, 2008 · Barry Ackroyd (cinematography) · The Hurt Locker 2008 poster · US Army EOD technical manuals

The King's Speech国王的演讲

2DPeeling Wall & Gold Broadcast Booth斑驳墙 + 金色广播间

  • Peeling layered-paint wall层层剥落的漆墙
  • 1930s broadcast booth30 年代广播间
  • Gold sound-wave lines金色声波细线

Painterly interiors with heavy wall texture: Logue's peeling, many-coloured wall in cool grey-green, then a padded broadcast booth in amber gold with a sprung microphone and fine gold sound waves. It follows Eve Stewart's famous wall and the poster's gold.墙面肌理很重的油画质感室内：罗格诊室那面冷灰绿的多层斑驳墙，再到琥珀金的软包广播间、弹簧吊着的话筒和细细的金色声波。照着 Eve Stewart 设计的墙和海报的金黄。

Style prompt风格提示词

Painterly interiors where the wall carries the image (outdoor subjects move into a related room with a window). Primary: a diamond-tufted padded wall in gold #C9A050 over deep brown #3A2610, brass button highlights, a warm glow behind the sound source (e.g. a hanging microphone). Secondary, for quiet scenes: a cool grey-green wall worn through to blue, pink, ochre and plaster, with damp lines and fine cracks; patches irregular, under a third of the wall. Clean-edged shapes under a subtle brush texture (strokes ~1/120 of frame width, ±5% brightness); faces, small objects crisp. Sound: 4–8 thin gold oscilloscope lines crossing the wall, spiky, later smooth. Figures in a corner, lots of wall. Camera: slow push-ins, one close-up of the source. References: cinematographer Danny Cohen, designer Eve Stewart (*The King's Speech*, 2010). Avoid: centred symmetry; coarse dabbed strokes; map-like peel; neon-bright waves.

墙面就是画面的油画质感室内（户外题材搬进相关的有窗屋子）。主调：菱形钉扣软包墙，金 #C9A050 压在深褐 #3A2610 上，扣子带黄铜高光，声源（比如吊着的话筒）后面一团暖金光。副调用于安静的场景：冷灰绿墙面，局部磨穿露出蓝、粉、赭和灰泥，有潮线和细裂纹；剥落块不规则，最多占墙面三分之一。形状边缘干净，只罩一层淡淡的笔触纹理（笔触约画宽 1/120，亮度起伏 ±5%）；脸和小物件画清楚。声音画成 4–8 条细金色示波线，扩过墙面，先带尖刺，后来平滑。人物偏到角落，留大片墙。镜头慢推，插一个声源特写。对标摄影师 Danny Cohen、美术指导 Eve Stewart（《国王的演讲》，2010）。避免：居中对称；粗糙点戳笔触；地图般的剥落；霓虹般亮的声波。

    全景：罗格的诊室，Clawd 缩在旧皮沙发左端夹着烟，上方是大片斑驳墙，右边一台喇叭留声机；镜头慢推。

  2. mic0.9s

    大特写：弹簧吊环里的大理石话筒，左边是 Clawd 的王冠和眼睛；金色声波先是断断续续（口吃），然后变得流畅；镜头慢推。

The Artist艺术家

Style prompt风格提示词

A modern homage to late-1920s silent film in black and white. Clean tones: glowing highlights, soft mid-greys, deep blacks; a gentle S-curve, grey halation around lights, slight flicker (±3%), fine grain, rare dust, a 1.33 pillarbox inside 16:9. Intertitles on black with an Art Deco border and spaced capitals, in the user's language. Settings: the subject's world staged as grand, ordered interiors or stage sets (e.g. a wrought-iron staircase, a gilt proscenium, a crowd in backlit silhouette); figures in elegant profile. The one hero subject the story centres on is the only coloured thing, wholly in tones of terracotta #D97757; lamps and fire stay grey. Camera: smooth push-ins and pull-backs, title cards flashed in, cuts landing on gestures (a bow, a wave). Reference: cinematographer Guillaume Schiffman (*The Artist*, 2011). Avoid: heavy scratches; sepia tint; colour bleeding into the greys.

致敬 20 年代末默片的现代黑白片。影调干净：发光的高光、柔和的中灰、深黑；温和的 S 曲线，灯周围有灰色光晕，轻微闪烁（±3%），细颗粒，偶有灰尘，16:9 里加 1.33 的左右黑边。字幕卡是黑底、装饰艺术风边框、字距拉开的大字，用观众的语言。场景：把题材的世界布置成宏大规整的室内或舞台布景（比如带铁艺栏杆的楼梯、金色台口、逆光剪影的人群），人物多取优雅侧面。故事围绕的那个主角是画面里唯一有颜色的东西，整个都用陶土橙 #D97757 的深浅来画；灯和火都保持灰色。镜头：平滑的推拉，字幕卡闪入，剪辑落在动作上（鞠躬、挥手）。对标摄影师 Guillaume Schiffman（《艺术家》，2011）。避免：重划痕；棕褐色调；颜色渗到灰色里。

Style prompt风格提示词

1970s Jack Kirby comic page. Heavy tapered brush contours, feathered spot blacks, exaggerated perspective, Kirby Krackle energy bubbles, block sound effects, hand-lettered balloons and yellow captions. CMYK flats printed as 45° Ben-Day dots: cyan #1696CD, magenta #DE2C70, yellow #FFD616, black #1C161A on aged newsprint #EEE2C2, colour plates 1 px off register. 70 年代杰克·柯比漫画页。粗细变化的毛笔墨线，大块涂黑，透视夸张，黑色柯比能量泡，方块拟声字，手写对白框。CMYK 平涂用 45° 网点印出：青 #1696CD、品红 #DE2C70、黄 #FFD616、黑 #1C161A，泛黄新闻纸 #EEE2C2，色版错位 1 像素，纸有纤维和中缝阴影。分格叙事，镜头在格间推进、横移，推近时网点放大成波普圆点。避免：顺滑数码渐变、雪白的纸、僵硬的管状机械。

12 Years a Slave为奴十二年

2DB&W黑白Cased Daguerreotype, Developing显影中的银版老照片

An 1850s silver-plate photograph in a brass mat and velvet case, developing from blank: a violin on a chair, Clawd beside it, head bowed. It is the photography of Solomon Northup's own time, and tells the film with one object and no people.铜框丝绒盒里的 1850 年代银版照片，从空白显影：椅子上靠着小提琴，Clawd 低头站在旁边。这是所罗门·诺瑟普那个年代的照相术，只用一件物件讲这部片，不画人。

Style prompt风格提示词

A cased 1850s daguerreotype, developing. Signature shot: the silver plate fills about 85% of the frame width inside a beaded brass mat #8a6a2c with a rounded-rectangle opening; velvet and leather only at the edges. Greyscale silver, near-black #1c1a18 to pale #ecebe6; a thin blue-violet to amber tarnish halo round the opening. Light swirls, hairline scratches, specks (±4% brightness). Soft window light from upper left; the subject poses still before a plain backdrop (an outdoor place is painted on it). One shot: the camera pushes from the open case to this framing in ~1 s, then holds; the image develops from blank mirror-grey, shadows first, a front sweeping left to right, done by ~3 s. Only the story's one hero is hand-tinted: a translucent flat wash (e.g. #D97757), shading showing through; lamps stay grey. Avoid: sepia, a small plate, fast cuts.

1850 年代盒装银版照片在显影。招牌镜头：银版宽度占画面约 85%，外圈是带珠串的黄铜衬框 #8a6a2c、圆角矩形开口，红丝绒和皮盒只露在边上。银版是灰阶银色，从近黑 #1c1a18 到浅银 #ecebe6；蓝紫到琥珀的氧化晕只在开口边缘窄窄一圈。抛光旋纹、发丝划痕和斑点都要轻（亮度 ±4%）。左上方柔和窗光，主体静止摆姿，背后素色布景，户外地点画在布景上。一镜到底：前 1 秒左右镜头从打开的盒子推到招牌景别，然后停住；影像从空白的镜面灰开始，暗部先出，显影前沿从左扫到右，约 3 秒显完。只有故事围绕的那个主角手工上色：一层半透明平涂（如 #D97757），透出底下的明暗；灯保持灰色。避免：棕褐色、银版太小、快切。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. plate2.5s

    One continuous shot: wide on the cased plate lying on the table, blank silver; the picture develops across it (a violin leaning on a ladder-back chair, Clawd standing beside it) while the camera pushes slowly in until the plate fills the frame and Clawd lowers its head.

    一镜到底：开场是桌上整只照相盒，银版一片空白；画面从一侧显影出来（小提琴靠在梯背椅上，Clawd 站在一旁），镜头慢慢推进直到银版铺满画面，Clawd 低下头。

No costume and no people: Clawd stands beside Solomon's violin and slowly bows its head. The only movements are the image developing, one slow push-in and the bow, held as one uncut shot for the whole 2.5 seconds.没有戏服，也不画人：Clawd 站在所罗门的小提琴旁，慢慢低头。整段只有三种动：影像显影、一次慢推、低头，2.5 秒一刀不剪。

  • Lubezki handheld long take卢贝兹基手持长镜头
  • Tungsten and fluorescent light钨丝灯与日光灯
  • Digital-cinema grain数字电影细颗粒

A detailed 3D backstage of a Broadway theatre shot as a handheld long take, warm tungsten against cold fluorescent, ending in the glare of Times Square. Birdman is remembered for looking like one unbroken shot, so the style is the camera move itself.细致的 3D 百老汇剧院后台，用手持长镜头拍：暖钨丝灯对冷日光灯，最后冲进时代广场的强光。《鸟人》最出名的就是假装一镜到底，所以这段的风格就是那条运镜。

Style prompt风格提示词

Real-time 3D, one unbroken handheld long take, after cinematographer Emmanuel Lubezki (*Birdman*, 2014). A narrow, lived-in passage (default a backstage corridor; an outdoor subject gets its nearest interior, e.g. a stairwell): chipped blood-red enamel dado #6e2a22 on the lower third, stained nicotine plaster #cdb98e above, pipes, cable trays, doors, pinned notices. Light only from practicals every 2–3 m: warm caged tungsten bulbs, one cold flickering fluorescent tube, a red exit sign #ff2a1a; the far door glows. Mid-key: plaster light near lamps, never below mid-grey between them; floor visible. Wide lens (18–24 mm), slow sway (±1°), soft ambient occlusion, light bloom, fine grain (±3%). Over 20–40 s, in any order: lead the subject walking backwards, whip aside to a doorway, let it brush past the lens, turn and follow. Avoid: a locked-off camera, blank walls, repeated textures, a crushed dark frame.

实时 3D，仿摄影师埃马努埃尔·卢贝兹基（《鸟人》，2014），一条不断的手持长镜头。主体在窄而旧的通道里（默认后台走廊；户外题材放进最近的室内）：下三分之一是掉漆的血红搪瓷墙裙 #6e2a22，上面是带水渍的烟黄石膏墙 #cdb98e，头顶水管和桥架，墙上有门和告示。光只来自画内灯具，每两三米一盏：铁笼钨丝灯（暖）、一根在闪的日光灯（冷）、一盏红色出口灯 #ff2a1a；远处门外发亮。中等曝光：灯旁墙亮，灯间不低于中灰，地面看得清。广角 18–24 毫米，低频晃动约 ±1°，柔和环境光遮蔽、轻辉光、细颗粒（±3%）。运镜 20–40 秒，次序不限：倒退领路，甩向一侧的门洞，让主体擦过镜头，再转身跟上。避免：锁死机位、空白墙面、重复纹理、压得太黑。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. oner1.3s

    Handheld medium shot backing down the corridor ahead of Clawd in its Birdman helmet, whipping left to the jazz drummer in his alcove, then swinging back as Clawd passes the kit and brushes past the lens.

    手持中景，镜头在戴鸟人头盔的 Clawd 前面倒退着走，向左甩到小房间里打爵士鼓的鼓手，再甩回来，Clawd 走过鼓组、擦着镜头过去。

  2. door0.7s

    Cut to behind Clawd at the stage door under the red EXIT light; the camera drifts sideways as Clawd shoves the door open onto white glare.

    切到后门，从 Clawd 背后拍，头顶是红色 EXIT 灯；镜头轻轻侧移，Clawd 一把推开门，门外一片白光。

  3. times0.5s

    Cut to Times Square at night: wide on Clawd on the sidewalk with walls of LED screens behind it; it takes a step and looks up at the screens while the handheld camera drifts slightly.

    切到夜里的时代广场：全景，Clawd 站在人行道上，身后是成片的 LED 屏；它迈一步、抬头看屏幕，手持镜头轻轻漂移。

Clawd plays Riggan in a slate-blue Birdman helmet with a gold beak and feather wings. The corridor runs as one fast handheld move with the drummer's swing in frame; the shove on the stage door is the beat that cuts us out into Times Square, where Clawd stops and looks up.Clawd 扮里根，戴一顶石板蓝的鸟人头盔，金色鸟喙、两侧羽翼。走廊是一条很快的手持运镜，鼓手的摇摆鼓点就在画面里；推开后门那一下卡在拍点上，切到时代广场，Clawd 停下抬头看。

References: 对标：Alejandro G. Iñárritu, Birdman (2014) · Emmanuel Lubezki (cinematography) · Kevin Thompson (production design) · Antonio Sánchez (drum score)

No.882015

Spotlight聚焦

2DMicrofiche Reader, Newsprint Halftone缩微胶片阅读机 · 报纸网点

  • Library microfiche reader图书馆缩微胶片阅读机
  • 2001 newspaper CMYK halftone2001 年报纸 CMYK 网点
  • Phosphor-green monochrome磷光绿单色

A newspaper-halftone picture seen on the green-grey screen of a library microfiche reader, with scan lines, a rolling scan bar and dust on the glass. The reporters dug the story out of archives like this, and the fiche transport replaces the cuts.印成报纸网点的画面，放到缩微胶片阅读机的灰绿屏幕上：扫描线、滚动的扫描光带、玻璃上的灰尘。片中记者就在这种档案里挖出真相，换镜头也做成胶片横移。

Style prompt风格提示词

A 2001 newspaper colour halftone on a library microfiche reader. Signature shot: the picture fills the frame, no headline or columns; lines and lettering inside it stay solid. CMYK screens at 15°, 75°, 0°, 45°, dots about 1/250 of frame width, 1 px misregistration. Convert to low-contrast phosphor-green monochrome, dots still clearly visible: film base #1b2320, frame gaps #0c110f, highlights no brighter than #a0dcaf. Only the story's one hero keeps its colour, whole figure (e.g. #D97757), screened and equally low-contrast; every light source turns green. Scan lines every 3 px, a faint scan bar every 4 s, a lamp hot-spot, dust, a hair, a light rounded hood vignette (outer ~5%), a small monospace fiche index. Scene changes slide the fiche sideways past a dark gap (0.5 s); slow push-ins. Avoid: newspaper layout, colour leaking, hard cuts.

2001 年报纸彩色网点，放在图书馆缩微胶片阅读机上看。招牌镜头：图片本身铺满画面，不要报头和文字栏；图里的线条和字（招牌、标签）保持实线。四色网点角度 15°、75°、0°、45°，网点约为画面宽度的 1/250，套色错 1 像素。整体转成低反差荧光绿单色，网点依然清楚可见：片基 #1b2320，帧间隙 #0c110f，高光不亮过 #a0dcaf。只有故事的主角整个人保留原色（如 #D97757），照样带网点，反差一样低；光源一律变绿。每 3 像素一条扫描线，每 4 秒滚过一道淡扫描带，一块柔和灯斑，玻璃上有灰和一根头发，圆角遮光罩只轻压外圈约 5%，一行小等宽胶片编号。换场把胶片横向滑过暗色帧间隙（0.5 秒）；镜头内慢推。避免：报纸版面、颜色外漏、硬切。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. still0.8s

    Wide on the basement newsroom: reporters round paper-buried desks under fluorescent panels, Clawd in front with a press badge and notepad; slow pull-back.

    地下编辑部全景：记者围着堆满纸的桌子，头顶是荧光灯格栅，Clawd 站在前面，挂着工作证、拿着速记本；镜头慢慢拉开。

  2. list0.8s

    The fiche slides sideways to the next frame: top-down on a printed list of priests, a toy-sized Clawd pushing a giant highlighter down the lines while the camera drifts down.

    胶片横向滑到下一帧：俯拍打印的神父名单，玩具大小的 Clawd 推着一支大荧光笔一行行往下划，镜头跟着往下移。

  3. headline0.9s

    Another slide to the loading dock: medium close-up of Clawd beside the front page headline "Church allowed abuse by priest for years", slow push-in.

    再滑到印刷厂码头：中近景，Clawd 站在头版旁，标题是「Church allowed abuse by priest for years」，镜头慢推。

Clawd plays a Spotlight reporter: a staff badge on a lanyard, a notepad, later a highlighter. Three frames of fiche, each joined by the reader's sideways transport instead of a hard cut, landing on the front page as the last beat.Clawd 扮 Spotlight 小组的记者：挂绳工作证、速记本，后面换成荧光笔。三帧缩微胶片，衔接不用硬切，而是阅读机里胶片的横向滑动，最后一拍落在头版上。

References: 对标：Tom McCarthy, Spotlight (2015) · Masanobu Takayanagi (cinematography) · The Boston Globe front page, 6 January 2002 · Library microfiche readers

No.892016

Moonlight月光男孩

2DMoonlight-Blue Oil Portraiture月光蓝调油画肖像

  • Oil portrait brushwork油画肖像笔触
  • Teal / magenta / violet duotones青绿 / 品红 / 蓝紫双色调
  • Split-level waterline camera半水上半水下的机位

Painted oil portraiture in saturated teal, magenta and moon-blue, with sculpted heads repainted in form-following strokes. It takes the film's title line, "in moonlight black boys look blue", literally.饱满的青绿、品红和月光蓝油画肖像，雕出来的头像再用顺着形体的笔触重新画一遍。直接照着片名那句「月光下，黑人男孩看起来是蓝色的」来画。

Style prompt风格提示词

Night oil portraiture: smooth volumes repainted in two passes of short, low-contrast (±8% value) form-following strokes: broad (1/60 of frame width) on water, sky and cloth, fine (1/150) on faces, which stay smooth. Palette by role: sky, sea and far land mid blue-violet #32377a, about 60% of the frame; moonlit planes and sand pale lavender-blue; teal #128a91 in water and lit sides; magenta #7a2f86 rim; deepest shadows #07071c. Any skin takes blue top planes and a wet highlight, eyes open and lit. Default night: one key, a full moon with a broken glitter path on water; magenta only as a soft rim from a distant glow; other small light sources stay cyan-white points. Grain ±3%. Signature shot: the figure calm, ~2/3 of frame height. Slow push-ins. Reference: Barry Jenkins, *Moonlight* (2016). Avoid: coarse dabs, small dark statue eyes, a murky frame.

夜色油画肖像。平滑实心体积上，顺着形体用两遍短笔触重画，笔触反差小（明度 ±8%）：海、天、衣服用宽笔，约为画面宽度的 1/60；脸用细笔 1/150，保持平滑。按用途分色：天、海、远处陆地用中等明度的蓝紫 #32377a，约占画面六成；受月光的面和沙地提亮成淡薰衣草蓝；青绿 #128a91 用在水里和受光一侧；轮廓光用品红 #7a2f86；最深暗部 #07071c。任何肤色顶面都受蓝光、带湿亮高光，眼睛睁开有光。默认夜晚，唯一主光是满月，水面铺出碎光；品红只是远处光源的柔和轮廓光；其他小光源只是一小点青白光。颗粒 ±3%。招牌镜头：人物平静，约占画高三分之二。慢推。参照巴里·詹金斯《月光男孩》（2016）。避免：粗块笔触、石膏像小黑眼、发闷的近黑画面。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. swim0.8s

    Split-level shot on the waterline of a turquoise Miami sea: a man stands chest-deep and holds Clawd up on his palm, his hand visible under the surface; slow push-in.

    贴着碧绿海面的半水上半水下镜头：一个男人站在齐胸深的水里，用手掌托着 Clawd，水下能看见他的手；镜头慢推。

  2. chapters0.8s

    Cut to three painted portraits side by side in teal, magenta and violet; they slide into a single wedge collage as the camera reframes, with Clawd standing in the teal panel.

    切到并排的三张油画肖像，青绿、品红、蓝紫各一张；镜头重新取景，三张脸滑拼成一个楔形拼贴，Clawd 站在青绿那一块里。

  3. beach0.9s

    Cut to the moonlit beach: two-shot of Kevin and Clawd sitting on the blue sand, full moon upper right, surf breaking; slow push-in.

    切到月光下的海滩：凯文和 Clawd 并排坐在蓝色沙滩上的双人镜头，满月在右上，浪花拍岸；镜头慢推。

Clawd plays Little with no costume: held on the waterline in the swimming lesson, standing among the three faces, then sitting on the sand beside Kevin. Three chapters in three hard cuts; the sea and beach shots push in slowly so the stillness carries.Clawd 扮小喀戎，不穿戏服：先在游泳课上被托在水面，再站在三张脸之间，最后坐在凯文身边的沙滩上。三个章节三个镜头硬切；海里和海滩两个镜头都慢慢推近，让安静延续下去。

References: 对标：Barry Jenkins, Moonlight (2016) · James Laxton (cinematography) · Moonlight (2016) three-face poster

No.902017

The Shape of Water水形物语

3D3D Underwater Teal Caustics3D 水下青绿焦散

  • Real-time 3D (three.js)实时 3D（three.js）
  • Projected caustics and volumetric light shafts投射焦散与体积光束
  • Dan Laustsen teal-and-gold gradeDan Laustsen 的青绿配暖金调色

A flooded 1960s bathroom and a lab tank in real-time 3D, soaked in teal with moving caustics, light shafts from above, bubbles and warm practical lamps. Del Toro's film lives in that green-blue water, so the whole segment is shot inside it.实时 3D 的被淹浴室和实验室水箱，整片浸在青绿里：流动的焦散、从上方落下的光束、气泡和几盏暖色灯。德尔·托罗这部片就泡在这种水里，所以整段在水中拍。

Style prompt风格提示词

Real-time 3D, fully under water (land subjects sink into a flooded room or sunken place), in teal: background #041414, hemisphere light #5fb3a6 above and #0c2a24 below; teal fog, reds fading first with distance. A strong source above the surface projects moving caustics: bright cells about 1/20 of frame width on near surfaces, sliding over floor, walls and the subject. Shafts fan down; edges stay dark. Bubbles, motes, the rippled surface from below. Warm gold on 1–3 practicals, at least one large (a lit window or lamp ~1/10 of frame width), plus cyan bioluminescence. Smooth-shaded models, ambient occlusion, shallow focus, modest bloom, grain ±3%. Signature shot: low angle looking up, the subject half the frame height, off-centre beside the light; slow push-ins. Reference: Guillermo del Toro, *The Shape of Water* (2017). Avoid: flat water, caustics as sky stripes, tiny warm accents.

实时 3D，全在水下（陆上题材就沉进淹水的屋子或水下遗迹），泡在青绿里：背景 #041414，半球光上方 #5fb3a6、下方 #0c2a24，青绿雾，红色随距离最先消失。水面上方一个强光源投下流动焦散：近处表面上的亮格子约为画面宽度的 1/20，滑过地面、墙面和主体。光柱向下散开，画面四边保持暗。气泡、微粒、从下仰看的波纹水面。暖金色给一到三处实际灯光，至少一处要大（亮窗或灯，约为画面宽度的 1/10），加青色生物荧光。平滑着色，环境光遮蔽、浅景深、适度辉光、颗粒（±3%）。招牌镜头：低机位仰拍，主体约占画面高度一半，偏在光源一侧；慢推。参照吉尔莫·德尔·托罗《水形物语》（2017）。避免：平板的水、焦散画成天上的条纹、暖色点缀太小。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. still0.9s

    Low-angle wide inside the flooded bathroom: the amphibian man holds Clawd in an embrace under the light shafts, a red shoe sinking; slow push-in.

    被淹浴室里的低机位全景：两栖人在光束下抱着 Clawd，一只红鞋在下沉；镜头慢推。

  2. embrace0.5s

    Cut to a close-up of the faces: his crest and gills against Clawd's corner, the red ribbon bow, bubbles rising; easing in.

    切到脸部近景：他的头冠和鳃贴着 Clawd 的一角，红色发带蝴蝶结，气泡上升；镜头缓缓推近。

  3. tank0.6s

    Cut to the lab: medium shot of Clawd in front of the glowing tank holding a boiled egg, reaching back to lay its hand on the glass; slow push-in.

    切到实验室：中景，Clawd 站在发光的水箱前，手里拿着白煮蛋，向后伸手贴上玻璃；镜头慢推。

  4. glass0.4s

    Cut to a close-up of the two hands meeting through the glass as a cyan glow blooms where they touch.

    切到特写：两只手隔着玻璃相贴，接触处亮起一团青光。

Clawd plays Elisa with a deep-red velvet ribbon and bow, later a boiled egg in hand. Four cuts in 2.5 seconds, a steady 1.25× pace: the embrace under the light, the faces, the tank, then the hands, with the cyan glow on the touch as the closing beat.Clawd 扮埃莉莎，头上一条深红丝绒发带和蝴蝶结，后面手里拿一个白煮蛋。2.5 秒四刀，节奏稳定在 1.25 倍：光束下的拥抱、贴着的脸、水箱，最后是两只手，接触处亮起青光作为收尾的一拍。

References: 对标：Guillermo del Toro, The Shape of Water (2017) · Dan Laustsen (cinematography) · Paul D. Austerberry (production design) · The Shape of Water (2017) poster

Green Book绿皮书

2D1960s Linen Postcard60 年代亚麻纹明信片

  • Curt Teich linen postcardCurt Teich 亚麻纹明信片
  • "Greetings from" large-letter type「Greetings from」大字母
  • Airbrushed hand colouring喷枪手工上色
  • Offset-litho misregistration胶印套色错位

A "Greetings from the Deep South" linen postcard that comes alive: woven paper texture, airbrushed saturated colour, chrome sparkles. The film is a 1962 road trip through the South, the same roads these cards sold to tourists.一张「南方问候」亚麻纹明信片活了过来：布纹纸、喷枪上的饱和色、镀铬上的星形闪光。影片是 1962 年穿越美国南方的公路旅行，走的正是这种明信片上的路。

Style prompt风格提示词

A 1950s–60s linen postcard, Curt Teich manner. Linen-weave card: ink only on raised threads, a fine crosshatch about 1/300 of frame width, white specks in the hollows. Saturated hand-separated colour, airbrush blends: sky blue #3fa0dc, grass green #4fae57, pink #f08cb0, teal #2fb5b8 for the main object or water, cream card #f6e4b8 for paper and border. The main object large, side-on, about half the card's height. Star glints on shiny surfaces; registration off 1–2 px. Big navy 3D block letters with white outlines across the top third spell the place or title (invent one if needed), a small scene in each letter, a red ribbon banner above. Open on the card on a desk, push in until it fills the frame and comes alive (water, figures move), then track sideways. Avoid: weave-less gradients, greyish colour, photographic perspective.

五六十年代亚麻纹明信片，柯特·泰希出版社那一路。平纹布纹卡纸：油墨只落在凸起的经纬上，形成约为画面宽度 1/300 的细格纹，凹处露白点。手工分色的饱和色，喷枪柔和过渡：天蓝 #3fa0dc、草绿 #4fae57、粉红 #f08cb0，主体物或水面用青绿 #2fb5b8，奶油卡纸 #f6e4b8 作底色和白边。主体物要大，正侧面，约占卡片高度一半。亮面加四角星闪光；套色错位 1–2 像素。藏青立体大字带白描边，横在卡片上三分之一，写地名或片名（没有就按题材编一个），每个字母里嵌一小幅风景，上方配红飘带。开场明信片放在桌上，推进到满屏后画面活过来（水在动、人在走），再侧向横移。避免：没有布纹的渐变、发灰的颜色、照片式透视。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. into1.9s

    One continuous move: the postcard on a motel desk beside a Green Book and a room key; the camera pushes into the card until the picture comes alive, the teal Cadillac pulls away and the camera pulls back into a side-on tracking shot of Clawd at the wheel with Dr. Shirley in the back.

    一个连续运镜：汽车旅馆书桌上的明信片，旁边是绿皮书和房门钥匙；镜头一路推进明信片，画面活过来，青绿色凯迪拉克开动，镜头往后拉开，变成侧面跟拍，Clawd 握着方向盘，雪利博士坐在后座。

  2. piano0.6s

    One cut to the concert hall: wide on Dr. Shirley at the grand piano with his trio under red velvet curtains, Clawd standing at the wing watching; slight push-in.

    唯一一刀切到音乐厅：全景，雪利博士和他的三重奏在红丝绒幕前演奏三角钢琴，Clawd 站在侧幕边看；镜头轻推。

Clawd plays Tony: a slicked black pompadour, short sky-blue sleeves, a lit cigarette, one hand on the wheel. The push into the postcard carries us into the road without a cut; the single cut lands on the concert hall, where Dr. Shirley plays and Clawd watches from the wing.Clawd 扮托尼：油亮的黑色大背头、浅蓝短袖口、一支点着的烟，一手握方向盘。推进明信片的运镜直接带进公路，不切；唯一的一刀落在音乐厅，雪利博士弹琴，Clawd 在侧幕边看。

Parasite寄生虫

2DArchitectural Section Axonometric建筑剖面轴测图

  • Dimetric section drawing二等角剖面轴测
  • Architectural poché and scale figures建筑剖切填充与比例人
  • Rainy-night colour grade雨夜调色

One architectural section through a Seoul hillside: the glass house on top, a hidden bunker beneath, stairs down to a flooding half-basement, in fine lines and soft material colours. Parasite is built on that vertical class divide, so one drawing holds the film.一张剖开首尔山坡的建筑剖面图：山顶的玻璃豪宅、地下的秘密掩体、一路通到被淹的半地下室，细线、柔和的材质色。《寄生虫》建在这种上下的阶级落差上，一张图装下整部片。

Style prompt风格提示词

Vector section axonometric, low dimetric view: the cut face lies along the near-flat 7° axis, facing us, depth along the 42° axis at half scale; roof and near walls cut away to show the rooms. Cut through the subject's place (a house, a tower, a ship, a hillside). Poché on cut faces: dark concrete with aggregate, earth hatched at 45° with stones. 1 px dark-grey lines at every zoom. Default daylight under a pale sky: concrete #b3ada2, oak #d6b58a, charcoal #3e4146, pine #3f6b3e, white walls, green lawn, translucent blue water. Furnished rooms, simple scale figures. Night or other low light is a variation: multiply 30–40% moonlit blue, lit rooms painted back warm. Crane slowly framing 2–3 rooms along one continuous path; end on the whole drawing at ~80% of the frame. Avoid: 30° isometric, thick outlines, cuts between levels, a small drawing.

矢量建筑剖面轴测图，用低角度二等角视图：剖切面沿接近水平的 7° 轴正对观众，进深沿 42° 轴、按一半缩短；屋顶和近侧墙剖掉，露出房间。剖切穿过主体所在之处（房子、塔、船、山坡）。切面填涂黑：深色混凝土带骨料，土层画 45° 斜线夹小石块。任何缩放都是 1 像素深灰线。默认白天、淡色天空：混凝土 #b3ada2、橡木 #d6b58a、炭灰 #3e4146、松绿 #3f6b3e，白墙、绿草坪、半透明蓝水。房间配家具和简洁的小比例人。夜景或其他暗光只是变体：叠乘三到四成月光蓝，亮灯的房间涂回暖色。镜头沿剖面慢慢升降，框住两三个房间，跟主体走一条不断的路线，最后停在占画面约八成的整张图上。避免：30° 等轴测、粗卡通描边、楼层间切镜头、图太小。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. still0.8s

    Daytime section of the glass house and its striped lawn party: Clawd stands in the garden doorway holding the scholar's stone over its head; slow push-in.

    白天的玻璃豪宅剖面和条纹草坪上的派对：Clawd 站在花园门口，双手把山水石举过头顶；镜头慢慢推近。

  2. house1.3s

    Cut to the stormy night: the Parks' living room with Clawd hiding by the coffee table, then the camera cranes down through the floor slab and the basement to the bunker, where the lights blink Morse.

    切到暴雨夜：朴家客厅，Clawd 躲在茶几边；镜头像升降机一样往下，穿过楼板和地下室，落到掩体，灯在按摩斯码闪。

  3. descent2.9s

    Cut to one continuous descent in the rain: the camera follows Clawd, the stone held over its head, from the gate down the long flights, through a tunnel under the road, into the low town and the flooding half-basement, then cranes out to the whole section, the house on top and the flood at the bottom.

    切到雨中一路往下的长镜头：镜头跟着头顶山水石的 Clawd 从大门走下一段段长台阶，穿过马路底下的隧道，进到低处的街巷和正在被淹的半地下室，再升起拉开成整张剖面，豪宅在顶上，淹水在最底下。

Clawd plays Ki-woo, carrying the scholar's stone the whole way: over its head on the lawn, crouched by the table in the storm, carrying it down the hill. Two cuts only; inside each shot the camera travels the section vertically so rich and poor share a frame, and the film's 12 beats end on the full drawing.Clawd 扮基宇，山水石一路不离手：在草坪上举过头顶，暴雨夜躲在茶几边，再一路顶着它下山。全段只切两刀；每个镜头里镜头都沿剖面上下走，让富人和穷人在同一个画面里，12 拍最后停在整张剖面上。

References: 对标：Bong Joon-ho, Parasite (2019) · Hong Kyung-pyo (cinematography) · Lee Ha-jun (production design) · Parasite (2019) poster · Architectural section axonometrics
