No.932020

Nomadland无依之地

2DMagic-Hour Soft Pastel魔幻时刻色粉画

  • Soft pastel on toned paper有色纸上的软色粉
  • Magic-hour dusk palette魔幻时刻的黄昏配色
  • Paper-tooth texture纸面颗粒肌理

Chalky soft-pastel strokes on grey-violet paper, the sky turning from gold to rose to violet over a wide plain with an old camper van. Nomadland lives in that long, empty horizon at dusk, and pastel keeps it soft and quiet.灰紫色纸上的粉质色粉笔触，一片开阔的平原和一辆旧房车，天空从金黄变成玫瑰色再到紫色。《无依之地》就活在黄昏那条又长又空的地平线上，色粉让它安静、柔和。

Style prompt风格提示词

Soft pastel on grey-violet toned paper #7d7079, tooth showing everywhere as fine speckle (±8% brightness). Sky blended smooth, a few long thin cloud bands. The land carries the texture: dry-grass ochre (gold dulled toward the paper grey) under fine upright grass strokes (1/400 of frame width wide, 1/40 long, ±15% value), dense in the foreground, thinning toward the horizon. Objects get soft diagonal hatching, broken edges. Palette by role: gold #f6cf7a at the horizon, rose #c9738a in the lower sky, violet #4b3f78 for high sky, clouds and far land, dusk blue #2b2d5c for shadows. A long horizon near mid-frame over open land or water; the subject mid-sized in the lower half. The sky drifts gold to rose to violet; lamps brighten as it darkens. One slow pan with a light push-in. Avoid: oily impasto, sharp edges, bare smooth ground.

灰紫色纸 #7d7079 上的软色粉画，纸的齿纹处处透出来，成细密颗粒（亮度 ±8%）。天空揉开抹平，只有几条细长的横向云带。质感集中在地面：干草赭色（金黄往纸的灰色压暗），上面铺满直立的细草笔，每笔宽约为画面宽度的 1/400、长约 1/40，明度起伏 ±15%，前景密，往地平线渐稀。物体用柔和的斜向排笔，边缘软而碎。按用途分色：地平线金黄 #f6cf7a，下层天空玫瑰 #c9738a，高空、云带和远处陆地用紫 #4b3f78，暗部用暮蓝 #2b2d5c。地平线长，在画面中线附近，下面是原野或水面；主体和身边的东西中等大小，放在下半幅。天色从金黄转玫瑰再转紫，越暗暖灯越亮。一个缓慢横移带轻推的镜头。避免：油画厚涂感、锐利数码边、光秃平滑的地面。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. oner2.5s

    One continuous wide shot: Clawd walks in front of an old white camper van with its side door open while the camera travels with it and pushes in slightly, the plain and buttes sliding by; it reaches a striped rug by a lantern, sits down and looks out at the horizon as the sky turns from gold to violet.

    一镜到底的全景：Clawd 从一辆侧门敞开的白色旧房车前走过，镜头跟着它横移并轻轻推近，平原和远处的孤丘缓缓滑过；它走到露营灯旁的条纹毯子上坐下，望向地平线，天色从金黄变成紫色。

Clawd plays Fern with no props, just a walk, a sit and a look. One uncut shot: the walk eases to a stop on the rug, it sits, then lifts its eyes to the horizon while the lantern and the van's doorway glow warmer as the dusk falls.Clawd 扮弗恩，不拿道具，只有走、坐、看三个动作。整段一刀不剪：走路慢慢停在毯子上，坐下，再抬眼望向地平线；天色暗下来，露营灯和房车门口的光越来越暖。

References: 对标：Chloé Zhao, Nomadland (2020) · Joshua James Richards (cinematography)

No.942021

CODA健听女孩

2DScrimshaw on a Whale Tooth鲸牙骨雕（Scrimshaw）

  • 19th-century whaler's scrimshaw19 世纪捕鲸水手鲸骨雕
  • Burin hatching and stipple刻刀排线与点刻
  • Tabletop product lighting桌面静物产品布光

A polished sperm-whale tooth on an oak table, engraved in fine black lines with touches of red, blue and yellow: a Gloucester fishing boat, the family hauling nets, Ruby singing at the bow. It is the folk art of CODA's own New England coast.橡木桌上一颗抛光的抹香鲸牙，刻着细密的黑线，点缀红、蓝、黄：格洛斯特的渔船、收网的一家人、船头唱歌的鲁比。这是影片所在的新英格兰海岸自己的民间工艺。

Style prompt风格提示词

Scrimshaw: 19th-century sailor's engraving on a whale tooth. Signature shot: a broad, flat-sided tooth lies horizontal on an oak table, about 85% of frame width and 70% of its height; ink bottle, jackknife beside. Engrave in near-black #17110c: needle hatching (lines 1/800 of frame width, spaced about 1/500), swelling outlines, stipple. Lines cover ~70% of the face, dense in sea, subject and land, sky lightly hatched; seas as rows of stylised waves. The hero is large (~1/3 of the tooth's height), coloured; otherwise red #9a2a22, blue #2b4a78, yellow #e9b526 only as small touches. Warm ivory, growth bands, scratches, honey tip. Folk layout: rope border, ribbon motto, rayed sun, motifs from the subject's world. Shade the edges, one gloss streak; don't warp the picture. Push in, then pan slowly as waves animate. Avoid: thick lines, solid fills, a slim horn.

19 世纪水手在抛光鲸牙上的针刻画。招牌镜头：宽而扁的鲸牙横躺在橡木桌上，占画面宽约 85%、高约 70%，墨水瓶和折刀在边上。用近黑墨 #17110c 刻：细针排线（线宽约画面宽的 1/800，线距约 1/500）、粗细起伏的轮廓线和点刻。刻线覆盖牙面约七成：海、主体和陆地密，天空轻排线；海是成排的程式化波浪。主角要大（约占牙面高三分之一）并上色；此外红 #9a2a22、蓝 #2b4a78、黄 #e9b526 只作点缀。暖象牙底、淡生长纹、抛光划痕、牙尖蜜黄。民间朴拙构图：绳纹边框、飘带题词、放射光芒的太阳、主体世界的图案。弧度靠边缘明暗和一道窗户反光，画面不变形。先拍整颗牙，推进后慢慢横移，波浪随之动起来。避免：粗线、实色填涂、细长的牛角形。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. tooth0.8s

    Wide on the engraved whale tooth lying on an oak table with a jackknife and an ink bottle; the camera pushes in until the tooth fills the frame.

    全景：刻好的鲸牙放在橡木桌上，旁边是折刀和墨水瓶；镜头推进，直到鲸牙铺满画面。

  2. boat1.7s

    Cut into the engraving: medium-wide on the fishing boat, panning slowly left to right with a slight push-in, from Frank signing I-love-you while the net is hauled to Clawd singing at the bow, a stave of notes unrolling from its raised arm.

    切进刻画：渔船的中全景，镜头从左往右慢慢横移并轻推，从一边收网一边打出「我爱你」手语的弗兰克，移到船头唱歌的 Clawd，一行五线谱从它举起的手臂上展开。

Clawd plays Ruby in a yellow sou'wester, standing on a fish crate at the bow. One cut from the object into the picture, then a single gentle pan carries the eye from the father's sign to the song as the stave of notes unrolls.Clawd 扮鲁比，戴一顶黄色防水渔夫帽，站在船头的鱼箱上。只有一刀：从物件切进刻画，然后一次轻轻的横移，把视线从父亲的手语带到歌声上，五线谱跟着歌声展开。

References: 对标：Siân Heder, CODA (2021) · CODA (2021) poster · New Bedford Whaling Museum scrimshaw collection

No.952022

Everything Everywhere All at Once瞬息全宇宙

3DRay-Marched Multiverse Jump-Cuts光线步进多元宇宙跳切

  • GLSL ray-marched universesGLSL 光线步进的多个宇宙
  • One look per universe (office realism, cosmic surreal, landscape, commercial)一个宇宙一种画法（写实办公室、超现实宇宙、风景、广告）
  • Googly-eye rainbow mandala大眼睛彩虹曼陀罗

Four universes, each with its own look, all ray-marched in real time: a cold-green IRS office, a hot-dog-finger piano lit like an advert, a silent canyon of googly-eyed rocks, the Everything Bagel as a black hole. A googly eye jumps between them.四个宇宙四种画法，全部实时光线步进：冷绿的国税局、广告光下的热狗手指钢琴、贴大眼睛石头的寂静峡谷、变成黑洞的万物贝果。像电影里一样，靠大眼睛贴纸在宇宙间跳转。

Style prompt风格提示词

Ray-marched real-time 3D multiverse montage: the subject jumps through 3–4 universes, each its own look, invented from its world, e.g. a realistic workplace under cold green fluorescents, densely dressed; a surreal cosmic version with a gravitational-lens halo; a silent wide landscape with white subtitles; a candy-pink product shot with pink-and-cyan rims. Each holds 2–4 s with its own grade. One link through all: a googly-eye sticker on the subject, about 1/10 of frame height. Transition, 0.5–0.8 s: push into the eye, smear, split RGB, then a rainbow ring of colour segments (#e5322d, #f7c928, #1fa39a, #2a6fc0, #e0478f) studded with googly eyes and pink beads blooms to ~80% of frame height and bursts to white. Signature frame: the ring around the subject; first jump at ~1 s. Reference: Daniels, *Everything Everywhere All at Once* (2022). Avoid: look-alike universes, a small plain ring.

实时光线步进 3D 的多元宇宙蒙太奇：主体在三到四个宇宙之间跳转，各有画法，都从主体自己的世界变出来，例如：冷绿日光灯下的写实工作场所，陈设很满；带引力透镜光环的超现实宇宙；配白字幕的安静旷野远景；糖果粉背景、粉青轮廓光的广告产品照。每个停 2–4 秒，各自调色。贯穿始终的衔接物：主体身上的一只活动眼贴纸，约占画高十分之一。跳转 0.5–0.8 秒：推进这只眼睛，拖影、RGB 分离，再炸开一圈彩虹色块环（#e5322d、#f7c928、#1fa39a、#2a6fc0、#e0478f），环上嵌满活动眼和粉珠，扩到画高约八成后白闪。招牌画面：彩虹环围着主体绽开；第一次跳转在 1 秒左右。参照丹尼尔斯兄弟《瞬息全宇宙》（2022）。避免：宇宙都一个样、环太小太素。

Clawd plays Evelyn with a googly eye on its forehead and a bum bag. Each jump is a push into that eye followed by a smear, RGB split and white flash timed to the cut, then a different action per universe: standing, stomping piano keys, sitting with the rocks, being pulled into the bagel. It ends where it started, arms open in the mandala.Clawd 扮秀莲，额头贴大眼睛、腰挂腰包。每次跳宇宙都是先推进那只眼睛，再接拖影、RGB 分离和白闪，卡在切点上；每个宇宙一个不同的动作：站着、用脚踩琴键、陪石头坐着、被吸进贝果。最后回到起点，在曼陀罗里张开双臂。

No.962023

Oppenheimer奥本海默

3D3D Particle Fireball, IMAX Grain3D 粒子火球 · IMAX 颗粒

  • Real-time 3D (three.js)实时 3D（three.js）
  • GPU particle simulationGPU 粒子模拟
  • IMAX 65 mm print gradeIMAX 65mm 印片调色
  • Trinity test high-speed photographs三位一体试验高速照片

The Trinity test site before dawn rebuilt in real-time 3D, with a fireball of about 720,000 particles, quantum visions of orbits and waves, deep pre-dawn blue against orange-gold fire and IMAX-style grain. Oppenheimer is built on that one light.用实时 3D 重建黎明前的三位一体试验场：约 72 万粒子的火球、轨道和波组成的量子幻象，深蓝夜色对橙金火光，加 IMAX 式颗粒。《奥本海默》就围着这一道光展开。

Style prompt风格提示词

Real-time 3D night lit by one huge particle event: a ~500k-particle fireball (or flare, eruption, wildfire) whose shape follows time: white flash (0.5 s), billowing dome by 1 s, a rolling toroid, a dust wall sweeping out. Right behind the subject like a halo, ~40% of the frame: white-gold core, bright orange-gold billows #ff9a50 with visible texture, black smoke only as thin veins and the top edge (under 15%). Its glow fills everything: warm #ffd8a0 on ground and cloud undersides, a hot rim on the subject, faces lit warm at half strength; long shadows toward camera. The rest is pre-dawn blue #2a3654 and dark earth #16120e. Clip only the core. IMAX grade: grain ±3%, warm halation, vignette, slight gate weave. Low wide angle, subject centred, slow push-ins. Reference: Christopher Nolan, *Oppenheimer* (2023). Avoid: a smooth CG ball, smoke-shrouded fire.

实时 3D 夜景，主角光是一场巨大的粒子事件：几十万粒子的火球（或照明弹、火山喷发、山火），形状随时间走：白闪（0.5 秒），1 秒成翻滚的斑驳穹顶，再卷成环，尘墙外扫。在主体正后方像光环，约占画面四成：核心白金，大部分是有纹理的亮橙金火团 #ff9a50，黑烟只做细暗纹和顶边，不超一成半。火光照亮全场：暖色 #ffd8a0 铺满地面和云底，主体有热轮廓光，脸上仍有半强度暖光；长影子朝镜头拖来，火星飘飞。其余是黎明前的深蓝 #2a3654 和暗土 #16120e。只让核心过曝。IMAX 拷贝调色：细颗粒（±3%）、暖色光晕溢出、暗角、轻微片门晃动。低机位广角，主体居中，慢推。参照克里斯托弗·诺兰《奥本海默》（2023）。避免：光滑的 CG 光球、被黑烟裹住的火。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. tower0.6s

    Low-angle wide on the floodlit steel tower before dawn, the countdown beacon blinking, the camera slowly pushing in.

    黎明前低机位仰拍被探照灯照亮的钢塔，倒计时的信号灯在闪，镜头慢推。

  2. gadget0.4s

    Cut to the bomb in its corrugated shack: the bolted sphere wrapped in cables under a warm work light; locked off.

    切到波纹铁皮小屋里的炸弹：缠满电缆的螺栓球体，暖色工作灯；固定机位。

  3. particles0.4s

    Cut to the quantum vision on black: glowing orbits round a core, interference waves below, a star collapsing.

    切到黑底上的量子幻象：围绕核心的发光轨道、下方的干涉波、一颗坍缩的恒星。

  4. watch0.8s

    A white flash, then the reverse shot: observers in hats hold welding glass to their eyes, lit by the fire, with Clawd in the middle in a porkpie hat and pipe; locked off.

    一道白闪，接反打：戴帽子的观察者举着焊接玻璃，脸被火光照亮，Clawd 戴平顶软呢帽、拿着烟斗站在中间；固定机位。

  5. still1.0s

    Low-angle wide on Clawd standing in the road, the fireball rising behind it like a halo and embers drifting in the air; slow push-in.

    低机位全景：Clawd 站在路中间，身后的火球像光环一样升起，火星在空中飘；镜头慢推。

Clawd plays Oppenheimer with a camel porkpie hat and a briar pipe that smokes. Five hard-cut shots build the countdown: tower, device, vision, then the flash lands on the cut into the watchers, and the music keeps going while the fireball swells behind Clawd for the last second.Clawd 扮奥本海默：一顶浅驼色平顶软呢帽，手里一只冒烟的石楠烟斗。五个镜头硬切，垒出倒计时：铁塔、装置、幻象，白闪正好落在切进观察者的那一刀上；音乐不停，最后一秒火球在 Clawd 身后膨胀。

References: 对标：Christopher Nolan, Oppenheimer (2023) · Hoyte van Hoytema (IMAX cinematography) · Berlyn Brixner, Trinity test photographs (1945) · Oppenheimer (2023) poster

No.972024

Anora阿诺拉

2DSnowy Neon Night雪夜霓虹

  • Neon tube signage霓虹灯管招牌
  • Wet-ground reflections and snow湿地面倒影与积雪
  • 35 mm night photography35mm 夜景摄影

Glowing neon tubes in pink, cyan and orange, smeared into wet boardwalk planks and caught in falling snow, with bloom, bokeh and grain. Anora runs from Brighton Beach's Cyrillic neon to a Vegas wedding sign, so the whole segment is lit by tubes.粉、青、橙的霓虹灯管发着光，倒影拖在湿栈道上，落进飘雪里，加上辉光、虚焦光斑和颗粒。《阿诺拉》从布莱顿海滩的俄文霓虹走到维加斯的婚礼招牌，整段都用灯管照亮。

Style prompt风格提示词

Photographic 2D neon night. Real tubes: glass tube, glow, bright tube, white-hot core, clips, dark jumper wires; rare flicker (every 5–10 s). Neon pink #ff4fa8, cyan #4fdcff, orange #ff9a2e, green #3dff9a plus warm sodium light over a deep purple-blue night #1e1838. Put neon where the subject's world allows (shopfronts, a harbour bar, its own building). Wet ground fills the lower quarter: signs stretch into long vertical reflections broken by plank gaps, paving joints or ripples; snow falls and drifts at the edges, tinted by the spill. The subject keeps a warm key on the face (a lamp, a window, its own light), narrow neon rims on both sides; the distance melts into bokeh. Bloom, vignette, grain ±3%. Short 2–3 s shots, new angles on one place, each a slow push-in. Reference: Sean Baker, *Anora* (2024). Avoid: flat neon lettering, strobing.

照片感的 2D 霓虹夜景。招牌是真实灯管：玻璃管、辉光、亮管、白热管芯，带卡子和深色跳线；轻微起伏，很少抖一下（5–10 秒一次）。粉 #ff4fa8、青 #4fdcff、橙 #ff9a2e、绿 #3dff9a，加暖色钠灯，压在深紫蓝夜色 #1e1838 上。霓虹放在主体世界里说得通的地方（店面、港口酒吧、主体自己那栋楼）。画面下四分之一留给湿地面：招牌拉成长长的竖向倒影，被木板缝、地砖缝或水纹打断；雪在下，边上积成雪堆，被溢光染色。主体脸上保留暖色主光（来自近处的灯、窗或它自己的灯），两侧各一条窄霓虹轮廓光；远处虚成光斑。辉光、暗角、颗粒（±3%）。每个短镜头 2–3 秒，同一地点换角度，各自慢推。参照肖恩·贝克《阿诺拉》（2024）。避免：平涂彩字式霓虹、干地面、频闪。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. wheel0.6s

    Long-lens shot of the lit Ferris wheel and the parachute tower over the sea, a New Year firework bursting; slow push-in.

    长焦拍海边亮灯的摩天轮和跳伞塔，一朵新年烟花炸开；镜头慢推。

  2. still0.7s

    Cut to the snowy boardwalk under Cyrillic neon shop signs: Clawd in a fur trapper hat and collar stands in front, Vanya laughing and waving beside it, the signs reflected in the wet planks; slow pull-back.

    切到下雪的栈道，旁边一排俄文霓虹店招：戴毛皮护耳帽和毛领的 Clawd 站在前面，万尼亚在它旁边大笑挥手，招牌倒映在湿木板上；镜头慢慢拉开。

  3. chapelSign0.6s

    Cut to Las Vegas: the heart-and-arrow "Wedding" sign and the CHAPEL light box fill the frame next to a white chapel; slow push-in.

    切到拉斯维加斯：心形穿箭的「Wedding」招牌和 CHAPEL 灯箱铺满画面，旁边是白色小教堂；镜头慢推。

  4. snow0.6s

    Cut to the final scene inside a parked car: Clawd sits on the dashboard against a windshield where snow falls and settles, Igor at the wheel in the foreground; slow push-in.

    切到最后一场戏，停着的车里：Clawd 坐在仪表台上，背后挡风玻璃上的雪落下、积住，伊戈尔在前景握着方向盘；镜头慢推。

Clawd plays Ani in a cream fur trapper hat, a standing fur collar and a big hoop earring. Four short shots on the beat, each with a slight push or pull: fireworks, the boardwalk with Vanya, the wedding sign, then the quiet car with snow.Clawd 扮阿诺拉：奶油色毛皮护耳帽、竖起的毛领、一只大圆耳环。四个短镜头卡着拍子走，每个都带一点推拉：烟花、和万尼亚在栈道上、婚礼招牌，最后是安静的车里和落雪。

References: 对标：Sean Baker, Anora (2024) · Drew Daniels (cinematography) · Anora (2024) poster

No.982025

One Battle After Another一战再战

3D3D Dip-Road Chase, Long Lens3D 起伏公路追车 · 长焦压缩

  • Real-time 3D (three.js)实时 3D（three.js）
  • Extreme telephoto compression超长焦压缩
  • Heat haze and road mirage热浪与路面蜃景
  • VistaVision fine grainVistaVision 细颗粒

A real-time 3D desert road that rises and falls like a roller coaster, three cars chasing over the dips, shot through extreme long lenses with heat haze, bleached sunlight and fine grain. It rebuilds the film's best-known sequence, the dip-road chase.实时 3D 的沙漠公路，像过山车一样一起一伏，三辆车翻着一道道坡追逐，用超长焦拍，带热浪、漂白的强光和细颗粒。复刻的是这部片最有名的段落：起伏公路追车。

Style prompt风格提示词

Real-time 3D, bleached desert daylight. Signature shot (3–5 s): a straight road over rolling dips and crests at a 2–5° horizontal field of view, stacking dips into waves; the travelling subject sinks and reappears; sky a thin band at most. Then 1–2 s each, a low camera at a crest, a mount on the vehicle or rider. Palette: sky #7fb0e0, haze #ece4d2 only at the far horizon, pale sand #d2ae80 (never orange), hard sun #fff0d8 from high to one side, the main vehicle faded seafoam #86b5ad. Contrast: dark cracked asphalt, crisp shadows, dense green scrub, roadside posts. Default midday; at other hours keep hard sun. Heat shimmer and mirage grow with distance; dust on landings; vehicles leave the ground over crests. Grain ±2%. Reference: Paul Thomas Anderson, *One Battle After Another* (2025). Avoid: wide lenses, a washed-out frame.

实时 3D，漂白般的沙漠日光。招牌镜头（3–5 秒）：一条笔直的路在连续起伏的坡谷上，水平视角 2–5°，让坡谷叠成波浪，行进的主体沉下去又冒出来；构图先保坡谷，天空最多留一条窄带。再接各 1–2 秒的坡顶低机位、车载或骑手机位。配色：天空 #7fb0e0，雾气 #ece4d2 只在最远的地平线，浅沙色 #d2ae80（不能发橙），硬朗阳光 #fff0d8 从高处侧打，主车是褪色海沫绿 #86b5ad。反差来自前景：开裂的深色沥青、清晰阴影、茂密的绿灌木、路边桩。默认正午，其他时段也保持硬光。热浪扭曲和路面蜃景随距离加重；落地扬尘；车过坡顶腾空、落地弹跳。大画幅细颗粒（±2%）。参照保罗·托马斯·安德森《一战再战》（2025）。避免：广角、发白发灰的画面、粘在路上的车。

Shots & direction镜头与导演编排

  1. tele1.2s

    Extreme long-lens shot looking back down the road from far ahead: the dips stacked into waves, the three cars sinking and rising through them, heat shimmer on the asphalt.

    超长焦从远处车队前方往回看公路：起伏压成一层层波浪，三辆车在波浪里沉下去又冒出来，柏油路面上热浪晃动。

  2. car0.8s

    Cut to a hood-mounted shot of Clawd at the wheel of the seafoam convertible, sunglasses pushed up, gripping the wheel then glancing up at the mirror.

    切到引擎盖上的机位：Clawd 坐在海沫绿敞篷车的驾驶座，墨镜推在头顶，双手握紧方向盘，然后抬眼看后视镜。

  3. crest0.6s

    Cut to a low tripod on the shoulder at a crest: the convertible flies over the top toward the lens.

    切到坡顶路肩的低机位三脚架：敞篷车飞过坡顶，冲向镜头。

  4. teleBack1.1s

    Cut to the long lens again, now from behind the pack, the cars dipping out of sight and reappearing over the waves of road.

    再切回长焦，这次在车队后方往前看，几辆车在一道道起伏里消失又出现。

  5. still1.3s

    Cut to a long-lens tracking shot from ahead: Clawd's convertible leaves the ground over a crest with the dark green and grey sedans closing in behind, lands, and Clawd raises one arm out of the car and holds it to the last frame.

    切到车前方的长焦跟拍：Clawd 的敞篷车翻过坡顶离地，深绿和灰色两辆轿车从后面追上来；落地后 Clawd 把一只手伸出车外举起，一直举到最后一帧。

Clawd plays Bob with a plaid bathrobe collar and gold aviators pushed up on its head, driving the lead car. The chase runs at real speed through five set-ups, alternating long-lens waves with close moments; the last shot ends on Clawd's raised arm, a pose held still so the finale can pick it up.Clawd 扮鲍勃：格子浴袍的翻领、推到头顶的金框墨镜，开着最前面那辆车。追车按真实速度走，五个机位在长焦的波浪和近处的瞬间之间交替；最后一个镜头停在 Clawd 举起的手臂上，这个姿势定住，好让终章接过去。

References: 对标：Paul Thomas Anderson, One Battle After Another (2025) · Michael Bauman (VistaVision cinematography) · One Battle After Another (2025) poster

How it was made

No video model drew this film. Claude Opus 5.5, working in Claude Code, wrote each set, the character animation, the camera moves, the edit and the score as code, and the code rendered them to video.

这部片没有用视频模型生成。Claude Opus 5.5 在 Claude Code 里把每个布景、角色动画、运镜、剪辑和配乐都写成代码，再由代码渲染成视频。

The same approach is packaged as Lemo-Opuscar: 43 film styles, each a style prompt plus a demo film, installable as a Claude Code skill so your agent can direct a film about your own topic.

同样的做法整理成了 Lemo-Opuscar：43 种影片风格，每种是一份风格提示词加一支样片，可以装成 Claude Code skill，让你的 agent 给你自己的题材导一支片。
